# ATAIProfile package: working context

This folder is the Swift package `ATAIProfile` (named `AIProfile` until 2026-10-15), extracted from the Atlys iOS app's ProfileV3 module so it can build on its own and then be linked back into the app. This file carries the working context between sessions.

## Keeping this file current

Update this file in the same turn whenever a fix lands, a decision is made, the app's ProfileV3 changes, or a finding changes the plan. Move finished items to Done, correct anything that turned out wrong, and keep the Status date current.

A Stop hook enforces this. `.claude/settings.local.json` runs `.claude/hooks/claude-md-freshness.sh` at the end of every turn, and sends Claude back once to update this file when:

- package files (`Sources`, `Tests`, `AppIntegration`, `Package.swift`, `Package.resolved`) were edited after this file was last saved
- the package has a new commit
- the app's ProfileV3 has a new commit, or a different set of files with uncommitted changes

The hook stores what it last saw in `.claude/hooks/.last-seen-state`. The hook script and settings are personal and gitignored.

## Package setup

`Package.swift` uses tools 6.4, iOS 17, Swift 6 language mode, and the `ApproachableConcurrency` upcoming feature. The `Resources` folder is processed, which ships `ProfileV3.xcassets` and `ProfileV3Localizable.xcstrings` in the package bundle. `Tests/ATAIProfileTests` holds the init scaffold, `ProfileV3TravellerRelationshipTests` (9 tests), `ProfileV3LoyaltyRowTests` (7), `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests` (21), `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (20), `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingTests` (4), `ProfileV3AppAccessTests` (7), `ProfileV3PastTripsTests` (14), `ProfileV3DiscoveryHintTests` (9), `ProfileV3VaultCryptoTests` (3), `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (18), `ProfileV3SettingsRenderTests` (3), `ProfileV3SettingsMonogramTests` (6), `ProfileV3ProgrammeInsightChatTests` (3), `ProfileV3DeleteAccountTests` (4), `ProfileV3PassportScanTests` (4), `ProfileV3GloveBoxTests` (25), `ProfileV3CarouselSelectionTests` (3), `ProfileV3GmailDisconnectTests` (4), `ProfileV3HeaderFadeTests` (2), `ProfileV3CitizenshipLockTests` (4), `ProfileV3AmbientScheduleTests` (3), `ProfileV3SettingsFailureTests` (2), `ProfileV3SettingsGloveBoxTests` (3), `ProfileV3UnderlinedFieldTests` (1), `ProfileV3PreviousPassportsTests` (7), `ProfileV3HubNotchTests` (4), `ProfileV3DestinationTests` (4), `ProfileV3ReviewFixesTests` (5), `ProfileV3AnalyticsEventsTests` (15), `ProfileV3CredentialStoreCardTests` (1), `ProfileV3CustomRulesTests` (9), `ProfileV3PhoneCodeResolverTests` (3), `ProfileV3OnboardingPresentationTests` (4), `ProfileV3ProgrammeDeleteTests` (5), `ProfileV3PassportEmptyStateTests` (5), `ProfileV3HouseholdScrollTests` (1), `ProfileV3MedicalCardTests` (1) and `ProfileV3PermissionsTests` (6), all swift-testing: 250 in the 2026-10-29 run with item 155, of which 247 pass (item 155).

The package, product, target and module are all `ATAIProfile`, and the tests are `ATAIProfileTests`, the same layout as ATCore, ATUIKit and ATAuthKit. The GitHub repo and this folder keep the name `AI-Profile`, so the SwiftPM package identity is still `ai-profile`. `README.md` is the integration guide for other teams.

These match the app's SwiftPM pins in `vdcios/VDCApp.xcworkspace/xcshareddata/swiftpm/Package.resolved`, so the app resolves one copy of each. PostHog was added in PR #3 (see Upstream drift); it was already in the graph through ATLogger 1.0.2, so `Package.resolved` didn't change, and the app's pin for it wasn't checked. Remote images go through Nuke. The app's SDWebImage 5.17.0 pod stays the app's own dependency.

## Building

The package builds. The 2026-10-14 `xcodebuild` exited 0 with no warnings. Without `-IDEBuildingContinueBuildingAfterErrors=YES`, Xcode stops after the first failing batch and hides most errors. A cold build, including package resolution, takes about two minutes.

```sh
cd /Users/aravindh/Desktop/Atlys/AI-Profile
xcodebuild -IDEBuildingContinueBuildingAfterErrors=YES -scheme ATAIProfile \
  -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' -derivedDataPath /tmp/aiprofile-dd \
  -skipPackagePluginValidation -skipMacroValidation ONLY_ACTIVE_ARCH=YES ARCHS=arm64 \
  build > /tmp/aiprofile-build.log 2>&1
grep -cE '^/.*error:' /tmp/aiprofile-build.log
grep -hoE "cannot find (type )?'[^']+' in scope" /tmp/aiprofile-build.log | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn
```

To run the tests, add `-clonedSourcePackagesDirPath /tmp/aiprofile-dd/SourcePackages` to skip resolution, and use a throwaway simulator, never the user's booted one. `simctl create <name> com.apple.CoreSimulator.SimDeviceType.iPhone-16-Pro com.apple.CoreSimulator.SimRuntime.iOS-26-5`, then `xcodebuild -scheme ATAIProfile -destination "id=<udid>" … test`, then `simctl delete <udid>`. All 8 tests passed this way on 2026-10-14, and again on 2026-10-15 after the rename. All 11 passed on 2026-10-16 with Done item 42, all 16 on 2026-10-17 with Done item 48, and all 19 later that day with Done items 49–52. All 59 passed on 2026-10-19 with Done items 53–61, and all 66 on 2026-10-20 with Done item 62, then 68 with item 63, 72 with item 64, 73 with item 65, 75 with item 66, 80 with item 69, 83 with item 72, 91 with item 75, 95 with item 76, 98 with item 77 and 106 with item 78. All 111 passed on 2026-10-21 with Done items 79–81, then 112 with item 82, 115 with item 83, 119 with item 84, 124 with items 85–86, 124 with item 87, 126 with item 88, 126 with item 89, 130 with item 90, 130 with item 93, 133 with item 94, 138 with item 95, 140 with item 96, 141 with item 97 and 141 with item 98. All 149 passed on 2026-10-23 with Done items 99–101, 153 with item 102, 153 with item 103, 156 with items 104–105, 158 with item 106 and 158 with items 107–108. All 158 passed on 2026-10-24 on `ef1fd9e` with items 106–109. All 158 passed again later that day with item 110, and after its traveler follow-up. All 160 passed on 2026-10-26 with items 111–112, 161 with items 113–114, 164 with items 115–116, 166 with item 117 and 166 with item 119. All 170 passed on 2026-10-27 with item 120, 171 with item 121, 183 with item 122, and 187 with item 124 (before item 123's destination tests were on disk). Since then one or more tests fail: on 2026-10-28, 223 of 226 passed with item 143 (failures listed there), 228 of 231 with item 144, 235 of 238 with items 145–147, and on 2026-10-29 236 of 239 with item 148, 242 of 245 with item 149 and again with items 150–152, 243 of 246 with item 153, 245 of 248 with item 154 and 247 of 250 with item 155 (the same three each time).

That count understates the work. An unresolved type hides other errors in the same expression, and Swift 6's data-race checks (`sending '…' risks causing data races`) only run in a file once it type-checks. To see everything, build a scratch copy with stand-ins:

1. Copy `Package.swift`, `Package.resolved`, `Sources` and `Tests` into a scratch folder. Without `Tests`, SwiftPM reports overlapping sources.
2. Add stand-ins with the real signatures of every missing app symbol, and patch the A3 items.
3. Build it with its own `-derivedDataPath`, and `-clonedSourcePackagesDirPath /tmp/aiprofile-dd/SourcePackages` to reuse the checkouts.

On 2026-10-14 the scratch copy reached zero errors once the data-race errors in A3 were patched there too, so the A lists in Status cover all of the existing code. The stand-ins were scratch-only.

Before refactoring a package file, check whether the app changed it since the last sync:

```sh
cd /Users/aravindh/Desktop/Atlys
diff -rq --exclude='.DS_Store' vdcios/VDCApp/Modules/ProfileV3 AI-Profile/Sources/ATAIProfile
git -C vdcios log --oneline -5 -- VDCApp/Modules/ProfileV3
git -C vdcios status --short -- VDCApp/Modules/ProfileV3
```

These differences are intentional package edits. Anything else that differs is upstream drift to port.

- Changed: `Components/` HeroGlobe, InteractiveGlobe, WorldPassCard, WorldPassCarousel; `Data/` APIErrorMapper, BoardStore, LiveRepository, `Mappers/` CaptureMapper and PassportMapper; `Domain/` KYCCapture, Passport; `Presentation/` HubStage, KYCCaptureViewModel, SettingsSheetPresenter; top-level AgentFlowHostingController, EntryPoint, HubHostingController, HubSurface, PassportScanLauncher; `Support/` Analytics, Theme+Globe; `Views/` HubChrome, HubView, KYCCaptureView, WorldPassDetailView. All file names carry the `ProfileV3` prefix.
- Removed: `Networking/ProfileV3APIEndpoint.swift`, `Presentation/ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel+KYCModule.swift`.
- Added: `Host/`, `Components/ProfileV3CardGlobe.swift`, `Networking/` API, DiskCache, ErrorEnvelope, Multipart, NetworkClient, `Presentation/ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel+HostScreens.swift`, `Support/ProfileV3Collection+NonEmpty.swift`.

## Architecture decisions

### Networking runs on ATCore

This replaces the app's `APIEndpoint`, `DataManager`, `NetworkError` and `MultipartUploadHelper`.

- `Networking/ProfileV3API.swift` is a Moya enum conforming to `ATCoreTargetType` with 34 endpoints under `/api/gmail-connector/kyc/v2/board` and `/api/gmail-connector/kyc/v1`, plus the Slack alert and the pensieve profile edit (PATCH). Later additions: the vault (`/api/fort-knox/v1`), the glove box (`/gateway/jaqen/v1`) and permissions (`/api/harness/v1`, item 149). Its `baseURL` is a placeholder, because `ATCoreMoyaProviderFactory` builds URLs from the host's `ATCoreBaseProvider.baseURL` plus the path.
- `Networking/ProfileV3NetworkClient.swift` is public. It wraps `ATCoreRequestExecutor` built with `ATCoreAuthPlugin`, a plugin adding `x-platform: ios` and `x-app-version`, and a plugin that copies the gmail-connector error envelope's message into `ATCoreServerError`. It decodes responses itself through `requestRaw`, which avoids an isolated-conformance warning. Decode failures still surface as `ATCoreError.decoding`.
- `Networking/ProfileV3DiskCache.swift` stores the board snapshot in `ATCoreCacheService.persistent` with deterministic keys.
- `Data/ProfileV3APIErrorMapper.swift` maps ATCore server statuses 401, 409, 422, 501 and 502 to 504 onto `ProfileV3Error`. On 401 it refreshes the Firebase ID token through ATAuthKit and retries once.
- `Data/ProfileV3BoardStore.swift` and `Data/ProfileV3LiveRepository.swift` take the client, session and cache by injection. The `BoardStore.shared` singleton is gone.

### App features are consumed through host seams in `Sources/ATAIProfile/Host`

KYC onboarding, identity capture and the land globe stay in the app. The package defines protocols, and the app passes implementations into `ProfileV3Environment`.

- **`ProfileV3KYCHost`** with value types in `ProfileV3KYC`. It covers `startOnboarding(on:completion:)`, `presentMinimalCapture(from:completion:)`, four step screens that take screen data and actions and return `AnyView` (Gmail, ITR, passport, selfie), `authorizeGmail()`, and `track(_:)` for analytics events. ProfileV3 keeps its own capture state machine and API in `ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel`, and builds the screens in `ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel+HostScreens.swift`.
- **`ProfileV3IdentityCaptureHost`** with value types in `ProfileV3IdentityCapture`. It covers `presentCapture(_:from:completion:)` for full screen and `captureView(_:completion:)` for embedding. A request carries the photos to capture, per-photo copy, progress, an optional header view, and a `verify` closure that returns verified, rejected or failed. The passport rescan in `ProfileV3PassportScanLauncher` uses the full-screen form. The embeddable form is meant for an upcoming profile agent and nothing calls it yet.
- **`ProfileV3GlobeRendering`** with value types in `ProfileV3Globe`. The host only implements `prepare(_:)` and `drawLand(_:in:projection:)`. The package owns the pose (`ProfileV3GlobePose`), projection math, the card globe (`Components/ProfileV3CardGlobe.swift`), ocean, shading, markers and the hand-off between card, hero and detail globes. The renderer travels through the `profileV3Globe` SwiftUI environment value, set by `ProfileV3HubRoot` at the bottom of `ProfileV3HubHostingController.swift`. The default is a dotted placeholder so previews work. The card constants in `Support/ProfileV3Theme+Globe.swift` match the app's KYC particle globe.
- **`ProfileV3SessionProviding`** supplies `currentUserID` and a main-actor `isGuestSession`.
- **`ProfileV3FlowOutcome`** is `.completed` or `.cancelled` for every host-owned flow.
- **`ProfileV3AccountHost`** with `ProfileV3AccountSnapshot`. The app keeps `UserData`. The host returns the first name, residence code, citizenship, passport nationality, contact, and selected traveller id, and it writes residence, citizenship, and a saved contact. `isContactTaken` wraps `mergeAccounts`. `travellerProfileDidChange()` posts `ProfileUpdatedNotification` with `showsConfirmation: false`. The package still parses the phone, decides `ContactUpdateOutcome`, and sends the two profile updates through `ProfileV3API.editProfile`.
- **`ProfileV3CouponsHost`** presents the app's coupons screen. `presentCoupons(from:)` receives the view controller already on screen. The app builds `CouponsView(viewModel: CouponsViewModel(), onBack:)` inside a full-screen `UIHostingController` and dismisses it from `onBack`. The package's `ProfileV3CouponsListView` is not this screen.
- **`ProfileV3AppHost`** covers logout, status flashes, app restart, and the floating tab bar. `showStatusMessage(_:isSuccess:)` is `FlashMessageGenerator`; the package still plays the haptic. `logOut()` is `LoginCoordinator.logOutUser()`. `restartApp()` posts `AVNavigationViewController.RestartApp`, and the app's own restart flow draws the restart screen; the package shows no cover of its own. `floatingBarClearance` is `AVTabBarController.floatingBarClearance` (68pt). `ProfileV3HubRoot` puts that value in the `profileV3FloatingBarClearance` environment, which defaults to 68 so previews match. An environment value set outside the hub would not reach it, because the hub has its own hosting controller. Past trips are added by country only (item 142), so the host has no past-trips option.
- **`ProfileV3Runtime`** is the launch entry, in the shape of AIChat's runtime and host-actions registry.
  - The host calls `registerConfigurationProvider { … }`, optionally with `appVersion` and Moya `plugins`, passing an object that conforms to `ProfileV3ConfigurationProviding` (`ATCoreBaseProvider` plus `ProfileV3SessionProviding`).
  - It also calls `registerHostActions(ProfileV3HostActions(...))` with the account, app, KYC, identity-capture, coupons, analytics, globe and validation hosts, plus an optional country list. A `nil` list falls back to ATUIKit's `ATFormFieldCountryDirectory.defaultCountries`.
  - The internal `ProfileV3Environment` is built from both the first time a screen asks for it, and rebuilt after either one is registered again. Until both are registered, every entry point stops with a `preconditionFailure`.
  - The environment hands the provider to the network client, and to the board store as the session. It keeps the hosts, and installs analytics, validation, and the country list into nonisolated sinks. Pickers and currency lookup read that list while drawing. Since PR #2 (item 138) it also installs the provider's `userResidencyCode` into `PhoneCodeResolver`.
- A DEBUG-only `ProfileV3PlaceholderKYCHost` stands in for KYC screens in SwiftUI previews.

### Client strings come from Ditto

- The app builds from the Ditto project `ai-profile` (text items `mo-aiprofile-*`). The library folder `profilev3strings` holds the same strings as components, keyed without the `mo-aiprofile-` prefix, in the subfolders `account-settings`, `agents`, `general-ui`, `glovebox`, `loyalty-programs` and `travel-documents`. The project items are standalone copies, not linked instances, so every create, edit or delete goes to both (`/v2/components` and `/v2/textItems`). Server-sent copy is the backend's project `ai-profile-board` (`be-aiprofile-*`); leave it alone.
- `Resources/ProfileV3Localizable.xcstrings` and `Generated/ProfileV3L10n+Generated.swift` are generated from the project by the Ditto toolkit (`atlyslabs/ditto-migrate-toolkit`; a copy is at `/Users/aravindh/Downloads/ditto-migrate-toolkit-main`). Don't hand-edit them. Add or change the string in Ditto, then rebuild from the repo root:

  ```sh
  PLATFORM=ios DITTO_TOKEN=… RUN_SCAN=no PROJECT_ID=ai-profile \
  RESOURCES_DIR=Sources/ATAIProfile/Resources XCSTRINGS_NAME=ProfileV3Localizable.xcstrings \
  GENERATED_DIR=Sources/ATAIProfile/Generated SWIFT_OUT_NAME=ProfileV3L10n+Generated.swift \
  ENUM_NAME=ProfileV3L10n LOOKUP_FUNCTION=ProfileV3DittoRuntimeStrings.lookup DIST_DIR=<scratch>/Dist \
  bash <toolkit>/Scripts/ditto-pipeline.sh --skip-scan
  ```

  The token isn't stored anywhere in the repo. `DIST_DIR` gets the runtime-override JSON, which nothing hosts yet, so keep it out of the repo.
- Code reads strings through `ProfileV3Strings`, `ProfileV3GloveBoxCopy` and `ProfileV3VaultStrings`, which forward to `ProfileV3L10n`. Values use Ditto variables (`{{name}}` exports as `%1$@`). With more than one variable, read the generated `fallback:` for the argument order.
- Ditto's `ios-strings` export writes `’` as `'`, so curly apostrophes don't survive a rebuild. Pieces that code joins into one line are stored without the joining space, and code adds it (`agentsHeadingLead`, `kycStepsDone`, the glove box phone headline).
- `ditto_check_component_issues.py profilev3strings` flags `account-measurement-height-cm` (`({{centimetres}}cm)`) as a wrapper leftover. The parentheses are real copy ("(180cm)"), so it's a false positive.
- To add a string, create the component first. It goes in the subfolder of its first key segment and is named as the id in title case (acronyms such as KYC stay upper case). The catalog comment becomes its notes. It gets a tag only if it is a button, an input label, or an empty, error or success state. Then create the project item `mo-aiprofile-<id>` from it. `ditto_copy_folder_to_project.py` copies a whole folder, so for a few new keys copy only those (item 118). To delete a string, send `DELETE /v2/textItems` with `projectId` and `developerIds`, then `DELETE /v2/components` with the unprefixed `developerIds`, and rebuild (item 125).
- Statuses are Ditto's defaults: Draft (`NONE`), In Progress (`WIP`), In Review (`REVIEW`) and Final (`FINAL`). On 2026-10-27 every string was Draft, except the project's `mo-aiprofile-account-button-stay-signed-in`, which is Final while its component is still Draft. The pipeline's export filters only by project, so a status changes nothing in the build. A `statuses` filter there would drop every key with another status, and its generated accessor with it.

### App-side bridge code in `AppIntegration/ProfileV3Host`

These files are written for the app target and move into `vdcios/VDCApp/Modules/ProfileV3Host` at integration.

- `VDCProfileV3Composition.configure()` registers `VDCProfileV3BaseProvider` and the host actions on `ProfileV3Runtime`. AppDelegate calls it once at launch, before any ProfileV3 entry point.
- `VDCProfileV3BaseProvider` reads the base URL from `APIConstants.BaseURL.getBaseURL()`. It captures AppDelegate's `authTokenProvider` once on the main actor, takes residency from `HomeIdentityProvider` and sends analytics to `AnalyticsEventManager`. It conforms to `ProfileV3ConfigurationProviding`, so it also returns `UserData.currentUser?.userUid` and `GuestSession.firebaseResolver()`; the separate `VDCProfileV3Session` is gone.
- `VDCProfileV3KYCHost` wraps `KYCOnboardingCoordinator`, `KYCFactory.presentMinimal`, the four KYC views with their step-model mapping, `KYCGmailOAuth` and `KYCOnboardingAnalytics`.
- `VDCProfileV3IdentityCaptureHost` wraps `IDCFactory.makeViewController` in a hidden-bar navigation controller presented over full screen with a cross-dissolve, the same as the old launcher. Its embeddable view owns an `IDCFlowViewModel`.
- `VDCProfileV3GlobeRenderer` wraps `GlobePoints.load`, `GlobePoints.cache`, `KYCParticleGlobe.drawLand` and `GlobeProjection`.
- `VDCProfileV3Analytics` forwards `ProfileV3AnalyticsReporting` to `AnalyticsEventManager`. `VDCProfileV3BaseProvider.sendEvent` still forwards ATCore's own events.
- `VDCProfileV3FieldValidator` forwards email checks to `InputValidator` and passport checks to `PassportDataHandler`.
- `VDCProfileV3Countries.catalogue` maps `Country.allCountries` to `ProfileV3Country` (ISO code plus the English `rawValue`). `allCountries` drops the multi-country trip case. Composition passes that array into the environment.
- `VDCProfileV3AccountHost` reads `UserData.currentUser`. Residence is `Locale.current.avRegionCode`. Citizenship and passport nationality are mapped from `Country` into `ProfileV3Country`. Saving citizenship assigns both `user.citizenship` and `citizenshipCountryCode`. Saving residence sets `userOverriddenRegionCode`. Both sync and post `citizenshipResidenceUpdated`. Contact saves post `UserDataCacheUpdated`. `isContactTaken` treats a `mergeAccounts` token as taken.
- `VDCProfileV3CouponsHost` presents `CouponsView` full screen over the view controller the settings presenter passes in. Back dismisses that host.
- `VDCProfileV3AppHost` forwards status text to `FlashMessageGenerator`, logout to `LoginCoordinator.logOutUser()`, restart to a `RestartApp` notification, and the hub's bottom clearance to `AVTabBarController.floatingBarClearance`.

## Status as of 2026-10-29

### Done

1. Created the package, copied ProfileV3, and declared resources and the iOS 17 platform.
2. Declared the dependencies above. The private atlyslabs repos resolve with the existing git credentials.
3. Moved networking onto ATCore.
4. Moved KYC, identity capture and the globe behind host seams, and wrote the app-side hosts.
5. Synced the globe and hub files to app commit `19c657bd7` before the globe refactor.
6. Typechecked the app-side hosts against the seams. See Verifying the app-side code.
7. Re-inventoried the blockers with a fresh build and a stub build (see Building), and set up the Stop hook that keeps this file current.
8. Circle icon buttons use `ProfileV3Theme.Palette.floatingControlInk` (RGB 0.09, 0.09, 0.11) and `ProfileV3FloatingControlBackground`. The iOS 26 glass modifier is inlined there; `ChatTheme` is not copied.
9. Package helpers, all internal: `Color(hex:)` (6-digit RGB, 8-digit RGBA with alpha last, invalid input is black), `Font.Inter.get` / `Font.Denton.get` forwarding to ATUIKit's `Font.inter` / `Font.denton`, and `Collection[safe:]` in `ProfileV3Collection+NonEmpty.swift`.
10. The unsupported-capture log in `ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel` goes through `ATCoreLogger.info`. That writes console and file only; the PostHog log record from `AppLog` is not wired.
11. Copied `HapticFeedbackGenerator` into `Support/`, marked `@MainActor`. ATCore 1.0.16 has no haptic API. ATUIKit's `HapticsManager` has light, medium, heavy, success and error, but no `.rigid` and no 0.65-intensity light. Connector haptics use both as fallbacks.
12. `PhoneCodeResolver` lives in the package and owns the only `PhoneNumberKit` instance (`nonisolated(unsafe) static let`). PhoneNumberKit was already a package dependency. `resolve` takes `fallbackRegion`, which defaults to `PhoneCodeResolver.defaultRegion`: the host's residence (installed by `ProfileV3Environment`, item 138), then `Locale.current.region`, then `"IN"`. Until PR #2 it skipped the host and started at the device region. It does not read `Locale.avRegionCode`. `isValidPhoneNumber` and `e164` replace `AppPhoneNumberKit`.
13. ProfileV3 events go through `ProfileV3AnalyticsReporting`. `ProfileV3Environment` keeps the reporter and installs it into a lock, because `ProfileV3Analytics.log` is nonisolated. `VDCProfileV3Analytics` forwards to `AnalyticsEventManager`. This does not use `ATCoreBaseProvider.sendEvent`.
14. Callers pass the app's `AVNavigationViewController` in as a `UINavigationController`, and the package pushes, pops and presents on that instance. Restart goes through `ProfileV3AppHost.restartApp()`; the separate `ProfileV3NavigationHost` was removed (item 39). The app still downcasts in `VDCProfileV3KYCHost`. The presenter's `afterDismiss` is `@MainActor () -> Void` so it can call `restartApp()`. `close(then:)` still takes a plain closure and runs it on the main actor.
15. Email and passport-number checks go through `ProfileV3FieldValidating`. The package still trims input and treats an empty passport number as valid. `nationality` is the country's English name, such as `"India"`. A DEBUG stand-in matches the app's two regexes so previews work before `configure`.
16. The pass number uses `ProfileV3Theme.Typography.worldPassNumberFont` (`"Dotline"`). `AVTextConfig` is not copied. Previews fall back to the system font until the app registers Dotline.
17. `ContactUpdateOutcome` is an internal package enum with `success`, `duplicateEmail` and `failure`, next to `ProfileV3ContactDetails`. The app keeps its own enum on `AccountSettingsViewModel`.
18. `LoginCountryOption`, `LoginCountryPickerSheet` and `SettingsSearchField` are internal package types. The option pairs `ATCountry` with a dial code. India is 10 digits, every other country is 7...15, and an unknown region falls back to `LoginCountryOption.india` (`+91`). The picker maps `LoginTheme` onto `ProfileV3Theme` and uses `HapticsManager.shared.selection()`. The search field keeps the account-settings hairline `#D6D9DC`. `ATSearchField` is not used.
19. The app's `Country` enum stays in the app. `ProfileV3Country` is a public code-and-name value, and `ProfileV3Countries` holds the list installed at `configure`. Lookups are by ISO code and by English name. India checks compare with `ProfileV3Country.india` (`code == "IN"`). Flags stay `ATCountry(isoCode:)`. `ProfileV3CurrencyCodes` rebuilds from that list. Package previews have an empty list until something calls `install`.
20. Account data goes through `ProfileV3AccountHost`. `ProfileV3SettingsDependencies.live(account:session:client:)` builds the settings closures from it, so the `static let live` singleton is gone. The hub first name, passport nationality, and airport-pickup region read the same snapshot. Currency still uses `accountSettings.preferredCurrencyCountryCode`, falling back to the residence code. The account-deletion flag stays in `UserDefaults`, keyed by the session user id. The package sends residence and contact with `ProfileV3API.editProfile`.
21. The extraction commit `a8d78af` (2026-10-14 15:23, "Extract ProfileV3 into the AIProfile package.") was removed. The tree was recommitted as `b611483` (2026-10-14 16:27, "Extract ProfileV3 into the AIProfile Swift package"), 369 files, and it includes the fixes in item 22. `master` has no remote.
22. Fixed after `a8d78af` and included in `b611483`: `ProfileV3SettingsRouting.swift` imports `Foundation`, and `ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel` spells its concurrency tasks as `Swift.Task`. `import ATCore` brings Moya's non-generic `Task` into scope. The 401 refresh calls `getIDTokenForcingRefresh` on the main actor and resumes from Firebase's main-queue completion, which does not capture the user. `ATAuthKit.idToken(forceRefresh:)` on the main thread returns the cached token, which is the one the server just rejected.
23. Coupons are presented by `ProfileV3CouponsHost`. The settings presenter finds the top view controller and calls `presentCoupons(from:)`. `VDCProfileV3CouponsHost` builds the app's `CouponsView`.
24. The two board timestamp parsers are `OSAllocatedUnfairLock`s around the same `ISO8601DateFormatter`s. `ProfileV3BoardStore.decode` is nonisolated, so two fetches can parse at once. The lock is what makes the static safe; parsing options are unchanged.
25. Logout, status flashes, app restart, and the floating tab bar go through `ProfileV3AppHost`. The package no longer names `FlashMessageGenerator`, `LoginCoordinator`, `AICSplash`, or `AVTabBarController`.
26. `makeHubSurface` is `@MainActor`, inherited from `ProfileV3EntryPoint`. It was `nonisolated` and passed the non-Sendable `onClose` into the main-actor `ProfileV3HubSurface`. The app's call at `ProfileTabBarManager.swift:300` needs `MainActor.assumeIsolated` at integration.
27. Comments are removed from `Sources`, `Tests`, `AppIntegration`, and `Package.swift`, except `// MARK` lines. `// swift-tools-version: 6.4` stays, because SwiftPM reads it. SwiftGen will put the `///` notes and `swiftlint` lines back into `ProfileV3L10n+Generated.swift` the next time that file is generated.
28. The residence `legacy` closure passed to `async let` is `@Sendable`. `pushResidenceToServer` is `@Sendable` too. The field save and the legacy profile edit still run together.
29. The relative date formatter is an `OSAllocatedUnfairLock` around the same `RelativeDateTimeFormatter` (`dateTimeStyle` `.named`, `unitsStyle` `.full`). The package build now exits 0.
30. `ProfileV3DateInput.onChange` and `ProfileV3FormSectionView.onTextChange` are `@MainActor`. `Binding`'s setter is `@Sendable`, and the plain closures were the two remaining warnings. The build is clean.
31. App commit `9e92cb981` is ported: agent `hasData`, orb `state`, programme art aspect and logo inset, and `ProfileV3RemoteImage.onLoad`. The new mapper test stays in `VDCAppTests` until B6.
32. `makeHub`, `makeHubSurface`, `presentAddTraveller` and `prefetchBoard` are public and take no repository. The repository comes from `environment.makeRepository()`. `ProfileV3EntrySource` is public; `ProfileV3Analytics.EntrySource` is a typealias to it. There is no internal `makeHub` that still takes a repository, because previews build the views directly.
33. `profile_v3_*` images and the Ditto loader use `.module`. `WhatsApp` and `reserve_logo_clear` stay on `.main`, because those two assets live in the app catalog.
34. `ProfileV3RemoteImage` loads through Nuke 12.9.0 (`ImagePipeline.shared` and `cache.cachedImage`). `onLoad` is unchanged. SDWebImage is gone from `Package.swift`.
35. `feature-profile-ai-spm` links the local package at `../AI-Profile`. `Modules/ProfileV3Host` is the copied bridge and is a synchronized root on the VDCApp target. `AppDelegate` calls `VDCProfileV3Composition.configure()` at the start of `didFinishLaunching`. The three call sites import `AIProfile`, the module's name before the rename (see B item 6), and drop `repository:`. `makeHubSurface` is wrapped in `MainActor.assumeIsolated`. The old ProfileV3 group `A11EFA112F99000000000B01` is off the target. The folder is still on disk. `ProfileV3MapperTests.swift` is excluded from the VDCAppTests synchronized membership so that target does not compile it against the removed module.

47. **The red KYC glow stays on the World Pass card (user, 2026-10-17).**
    - Before: the red `.negative` glow (expired KYC, or the board's `glow: "red"`/`"expired"`) rose, showed the KYC caption, then faded to nothing with the rest of the `PhaseAnimator` cue.
    - Now `ProfileV3KYCGlow` keeps the original cue for both tones (`idle → seeded → risen → captioned`, then back to `idle`): the glow rises from the card's bottom edge, the caption shows, then the caption fades. For the red tone the glow then stays: a `@State hasRisen` is set when the animator reaches `.risen`, and from then on `.idle` draws red at full opacity. The green tone still fades out at `.idle`. A glow view created after its cue already played (trigger > 0 at init, e.g. the carousel rebuilds the card) starts with `hasRisen` true and shows the red at rest without replaying the rise. The cue timing is unchanged: the hub stage reveals the cards, then `armKYCGlow()` plays the selected pass's cue, and every other pass plays its own the first time it is selected.
    - The first pass that day put the red tone through a `.held` phase in place of `.idle` (with `.id(tone)`), which kept the red but dropped the rise; the user asked for the rise back ("we also need the animation of the glow from the start"). `hasRisen` flips at `.risen`, not on the trigger change, so the held red can't flash at `.idle` before the cue jumps to `.seeded`.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 16/16 pass. Not run on a device or in the app.
    - Which states glow is unchanged: not-started and in-progress KYC (`glow: "none"`, `kyc_state: "not_done"`) still show no glow. The user first chose to turn those red too, then narrowed it the same day: "just make the red glow stay".
    - Co-travellers whose KYC isn't done show no glow because the server says so. The board in the booted simulator on 2026-10-17 sent the main profile `glow: "red"` with `kyc_state: "not_done"`. Every co-traveller got `glow: "none"`, no `kyc_state`, `kyc_complete: false`, and a "Connect Gmail to complete KYC for this traveller" banner. The user decided "if it's from server - no issues", so the client keeps mapping `"none"` to no glow.
    - Not mirrored to the app copy yet.
48. **Home agent boxes follow the Gmail rule (user's table, 2026-10-17).**
    - ACTIVATE pill: shown only when Gmail is not connected and the user has saved preferences for that agent (`hasData`, which means the tile has `updated_label`). If Gmail is connected, or there are no preferences, there is no pill. The pink invite glow and the orb ring keep their old rules. The glow shows on agents without preferences while KYC is incomplete or the tile has `activate`.
    - Gmail state: `ProfileV3BoardMapper.isGmailConnected` reads the profile whose `profile_kyc.required` contains `gmail_connect`. That is the main profile; co-travellers require only passport and selfie. It returns whether `gmail_connect` is absent from `missing`.
      - Every profile's home gets that value as `ProfileV3Home.isGmailConnected`, because Gmail is account-level.
      - With no such profile it is nil, and the card falls back to the tile's `activate` CTA.
      - The live board on 2026-10-17 sends no `profile_kyc`, so the fallback is what runs. The server already applies the table: `activate` (`{"label":"ACTIVATE","next":{"go":"agent",…}}`) comes only on tiles with `updated_label`, while the main profile's KYC banner still asks for `gmail_connect`.
    - Tap: `tapAgent` always calls `openAgent(…, requiresKYC: false)`, so the box opens the agent's preferences even behind a KYC gate. Analytics still reports the old gate in `requiresKYC`. The agent KYC redirect (`ProfileV3KYCRedirectView.Subject.agent`, `startKYC(resuming: .agent)`, and `openAgent`'s `requiresKYC: true` branch) is now unreachable from home but left in place.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests` (5) covers the Gmail state from `profile_kyc`, the pill table, the CTA fallback, a board shaped like the live payload, and the tap. Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 16/16 pass.
    - Not mirrored to the app copy yet.
49. **Agent form text fields grow with their text (user, 2026-10-17; dates excluded).** A first pass that day made `.text` sections full width. The user then asked for the width to follow the content, with the full row as the maximum.
    - `ProfileV3UnderlinedField(growsWithContent: true)` wraps the field in a private `ProfileV3ContentWidthLayout`. The width is the text field's ideal width (its text, or the placeholder when empty) plus `Sizes.underlinedFieldCaretAllowance` (4pt, so the caret never scrolls the text). It never goes below `width` (the 160pt `underlinedFieldWidth`) or above the row. It stays one `TextField`, so focus survives the resize. The dashed underline is an overlay in ATUIKit, so it follows the width.
    - `ProfileV3FormSectionView` turns it on for `.text` sections: plain text, and contacts such as the medical agent's emergency contact.
    - Date sections (`ProfileV3DateInput`) stay 160pt. A stored date the codec can't parse falls back to a text field in `ProfileV3AgentMapper.date`. That field carries `ProfileV3TextFieldValue.isDate` and also stays 160pt.
    - The 160pt floor was chosen here, not asked for: short values and empty fields keep the old look and tap target. Passing `width: nil` with `growsWithContent` would make short text hug too.
    - The first request's screenshot was the earlier hub image again, so the screen was inferred: the agent forms are the only place with both kinds of field.
    - Test: `ProfileV3UnderlinedFieldTests` hosts the field in a `UIHostingController` at a 345pt row. Empty and short text measure 160, longer text grows, and very long text stops at 345.
50. **The hub reloads its board after an agent form saves (user, 2026-10-17: ACTIVATE showed only after an app kill).**
    - Cause: `openAgent` presented the agent flow with no callback, so the tile kept its old `updated_label` and `activate` until the next launch.
    - `makeAgentFlow`, `ProfileV3AgentFlowHostingController`, `ProfileV3AgentFlowViewModel` and `ProfileV3AgentFormViewModel` take `onSaved` (default `{}`). The form calls it as soon as `saveAgentForm` succeeds, before the 1.2s saved state and the dismissal, and also when the task was cancelled after the write landed.
    - The hub passes `viewModel.requestRefresh()`, the refresh that `onAdded`, `onProgrammesChanged` and `onTravellersChanged` already use. It fetches a fresh board (`loadBoard`, which doesn't reuse a pending fetch), so the tile normally updates while the sheet is still closing. Whether the pill shows is still the server's call (Done item 48).
    - Not covered: a passport scan or ride-picker change inside the flow without a form save doesn't reload the hub.
    - Test: `savingAnAgentFormTellsTheHubBeforeTheSheetCloses` (fires on success before close, not on failure).
51. **Programme cards show the full membership number (user, 2026-10-17: "let's not hide the membership id").** `ProfileV3LoyaltyProgramme.displayNumber` is `membershipNumber ?? maskedNumber`. The programmes tile's number row, the tile's back, and the pass back-face `ProfileV3ProgrammeCard` show it, and the tile's `hasNumber` reads it. The masked "XXX" plus last 4 stays as the fallback for rows with only `last4`. The copy button already copied the full number. Test: `rowsShowTheFullMembershipNumber`.
52. **Permission section tags lose their pill (user, 2026-10-17: "what is this gray border").** `ProfileV3PermissionTag` drew `ProfileV3TagLabel` in a capsule filled with `Palette.background`, stroked with a hairline `settingsCardBorder`, and padded by a hairline. It is now the plain label at `tagHeight` and 50% opacity. Without the padding the label sits 1pt further left, level with the option titles, and the options move 2pt up.
    - Verified for items 49–52: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 19/19 pass. Not run on a device or in the app.
53. **The Information access shield carries a tick (user, 2026-10-19: "the atlys logo has been updated with a tick"; Figma frame "1880" `2633:31292`, layer `check` `4926:9054`).** `ProfileV3InformationAccessHero` drew the Atlys wordmark in the shield (48×21.2, centred). It now draws `ProfileV3ConnectorGlyph.shieldCheck` (`profile_v3_shield_check`), the Figma `check` SVG byte for byte: a rounded tick filled with a linear gradient (white → #5D64FF → #FF5959 → #FFE23D). It is 54×54 at Figma's shield-local origin (14, 26), which is 1pt left of and 0.5pt below centre: `Sizes.InformationAccess.check` and `checkOrigin` replace `wordmark`.
    - Figma's export carries a zero-size background-blur `foreignObject` (blur 0). iOS ignores it and draws the gradient tick; an `ImageRenderer` snapshot of the hero matched the Figma frame.
    - The wordmark asset stays: `ProfileV3TermsView` still draws it.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 19/19 pass. Package-only so far: vdcios is still on `fix/ios-missing-travel-dates`.
54. **Loyalty follows the 2026-10-18 backend note: credit cards have no membership number, catalogue art is never empty.**
    - Enter details (`/loyalty/enter/{program_id}`): the form is built from the served `fields` in order (only `membership_number` and `points` can be submitted), with the server's required flags. `canSubmit` needs the form loaded and every required field filled. Values reset per programme. A failed load shows the retry panel; it used to be ignored behind a hard-coded two-field form.
    - The submission carries `membership_number` only when the form has that field, so a credit card (one field, `points`) never sends one. `ProfileV3ProgrammeSubmission.membershipNumber` is optional. The mock serves the one-field form for `credit_cards` (`ProfileV3LoyaltyEntryForm.mock`).
    - Catalogue rows map `art_placeholder` and `text_overlay`. The picker card and the pass back-face card draw the programme name over placeholder art in the overlay colour (white on the no-art fallback). Order stays as served.
    - `displayNumber` is optional (`membershipNumber ?? maskedNumber.nonEmpty`). A row with neither draws no number line (tile, flipped tile, back-face card) and no tier divider.
    - Tests in `ProfileV3LoyaltyRowTests`: `cardRowsWithoutANumberShowNone`, `catalogueKeepsTheServerOrderAndPlaceholderArt`, `creditCardFormAsksOnlyForPointsAndSendsNoMembershipNumber`.
55. **Agent grid and forms follow the 2026-10-18/19 backend note (written in another session on 2026-10-19, reviewed and adjusted here).**
    - Tiles: ACTIVATE shows exactly when the server sends `activate`, with no bolt; the Gmail rule is now server-side, and `isGmailConnected` is still mapped and tested but no view reads it. Tiles with an unknown `agent_type` are kept, with the raw type as `id`. `orb_personal` maps to the personal agent art (`ProfileV3BoardMapper.iconURL`).
    - `tapAgent` follows `next`: `go:"agent"` opens preferences for the directive's `profile_id`, and `go:"travel_persona"` opens the persona sheet (item 57). Any other or missing `next` on a known agent opens its preferences, as before; the first version did nothing, so a cached board still carrying `kyc_gate` left dead tiles.
    - Forms: `display:"ft_in"` shows inches as feet and inches (71 → 5'11") through `ValueFormat.feetAndInches`. `format:"dd_mm"` (anniversary) shows day and month wheels and "14 Feb", keeps the date in year 2000 at local noon, and saves `2000-MM-DD`. A single-select preselects a plain `value` (organ donor's `yes`/`no`, or a legacy `true`/`false`).
    - Globe stats: `value` is optional (null shows "--"), and the distance tile appends the served `unit` (`mi`) in place of "km".
    - Tests: `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests` (13) covers server ACTIVATE, routing with the fallback, the persona tile, `ft_in`, `dd_mm`, the single-select preselect and globe stats.

The cars subtitle draws that run underlined in `neutralGrey800` (#333) as a tappable `AttributedString` link, and the agent form opens the regions sheet from it. Other note styles (`ProfileV3AINoteLine`) still draw a note without its link. `Car.subtitleWidth` is 381 (was 310), so the note fits one line as in the new frame.

The sheet sizes itself to its content with a `.height` detent.

57. **Travel persona screens (user, 2026-10-19; Figma `3309:68726` radar, `3309:69708` trait detail, `4778:5959` trait copy).**
    - Contract: `GET …/board/travel_persona?profile_id=` and `GET …/board/travel_persona/{trait}?profile_id=&page=`. Writes go to `/board/submit`, echoing the served `submit`, `button` or `bulk` object with its JSON types (`ProfileV3BoardAction` over `ProfileV3ScalarDTO`, which is now also `Encodable`), plus `score` for a drag. The directive gained `trait` and `page`, and `go:"persona_trait"` maps to `.personaTrait(trait:profileID:page:)`.
    - Sheet: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaHostingController` (page sheet, large detent, 44pt corners), opened by the hub router's `openTravelPersona`. The protocol's empty default is gone, so every router must implement it. The header matches the agent forms (orb, "Found N/10 traits" in two tones, subtitle), followed by the "Traveller profile" card and `ProfileV3PersonaRadar`. The card follows the frame: an H5 Denton Bold 20 title in a 25pt line box 39pt down, the radar 40pt below it and 314pt tall, and 69pt bottom padding (487pt in all).
    - Radar geometry was read from the frame through the plugin API (`use_figma`, read-only; the frame is too large for `get_design_context`, and `download_assets` stops at 20 SVGs).
      - Rings: five decagons at scores 0/25/50/75/100, radius 10 to 105.6 (#666 at 30%, 0.57pt), fitted to the frame's rings at 33.8/57.3/81.9/106. Spokes: #666 at 20%.
      - Layer order as in the frame: the three outer rings sit under the polygon, and the two inner rings and the spokes sit over it.
      - Each point sits at its score along its axis. Labels (B2/B1 Medium, #111827, up to 72pt wide) are anchored outside each vertex.
    - The polygon fill is the frame's own paint: white, #E5E6F7, #5D64FF, #FF5959, #FFE23D at 70%, in a 50% layer.
      - The layer also carries a 1.14pt gradient stroke, but the paint is hidden (`visible: false`), so no outline is drawn.
      - Figma maps a gradient to the shape's own bounding box, which `.fill(LinearGradient)` cannot do, so `ProfileV3BoxGradient` turns the gradient transform's first row into a `GraphicsContext` shading for any box. Both persona cards use it too (`ProfileV3BoxGradientCard`): the same transform gives a different angle at 487pt and at 328pt tall.
    - Handles are drawn in code, because iOS drops the SVG's shadow filter: a 15pt white dot, a 1pt rim outside it (#BBBBBC→#DFE1EA), and a 7pt blur at 15%.
    - Label wrap: the frame types "Culture &⏎History" with a hard break. A served "Culture & History" wraps as "Culture / & History", because iOS keeps a lone word off the last line.
    - Gestures: tap highlights and double-tap opens the trait. Dragging and saving changed in item 59: editing is off for now, and when it is on a drag only drafts the score until "save changes". The entrance follows the Figma motion (handles at 0.59s, labels staggered from 0.95s) and is skipped under Reduce Motion. VoiceOver: adjustable by ±5, and the default action opens the trait.
    - Trait detail: a score card (name, D2 score, description, and a read-only scale with the active third highlighted, using the midpoint and knob SVGs). Then "See how your score was calculated" with the bulk pill and the orb, and the rows:
      - an icon (Gmail logo, or "edited by you"), a #333→#999 title, and the delta, green when positive;
      - ignored rows greyed with the delta struck through, and an Ignore/Undo pill;
      - dotted connectors, paging on scroll, and the starting point row (flag).
      - New SVGs: `profile_v3_persona_gmail`, `_edited`, `_flag`, `_scale_mid`, `_scale_knob`.
      - Not drawn: the design's per-row mail/eye icons, which have no field in the contract.
    - Mock: `ProfileV3PersonaStore` applies the product note's rules: start at 50, apply rows oldest to newest, clamp after each, and a drag merges into your latest own row.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (7). This build replaced review item 4's "hide the persona tile"; the `isTravelPersonaEnabled` flag added for it was removed.
    - Verified for items 54–57: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 36/36 pass. `ImageRenderer` snapshots of the persona content, the trait content, the ride regions content and the cars subtitle were compared with the Figma frames. Package-only: vdcios is still on `fix/ios-missing-travel-dates`.
58. **App access under Connectors, UI only (user, 2026-10-19; Figma `3338:5444` and `3338:12183` page, `3338:5626` add credentials, `2782:35985` / `2782:37216` / `2782:37659` add new password with its loading and success states, `3338:12689` connectors panel, `3338:12923` edit).** There is no backend yet.
    - Gating: `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isAppAccessEnabled` is true in DEBUG and false in release. Off, Settings › App access still opens the old "Coming soon" page, now `ProfileV3AppAccessComingSoonView`.

59. **Travel persona: save with a button, and editing off by client config (user, 2026-10-19, after the drag saved on release and showed "That isn't available yet." with a success check).**
    - Client config: `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isPersonaEditingEnabled = false`. Both persona view models take `isEditable` (default: the flag), and the trait view model gets it from the persona one.
    - With editing off:
      - The radar handles keep only tap (highlight) and double-tap (open). There is no drag and no VoiceOver adjust (`ProfileV3PersonaAdjustable` leaves the action off).
      - `preview`, `adjust`, `save` and the trait page's `perform` do nothing.
      - The trait page hides its Ignore/Undo and "Ignore all" pills.
    - With editing on:
      - A drag or a ±5 adjust only writes `drafts`. A draft moved back to the saved score is dropped.
      - The sheet shows the agent forms' save bar (`ProfileV3SaveButton`, "save changes" → "Travel persona updated", new key `mo-aiprofile-agents-toast-persona-updated`).
      - `save()` submits each changed trait's `submit` with its score in turn, reloads, holds "saved" for `savedDismissMilliseconds`, then hides the bar.
      - A failure keeps the drafts and shows the reason in red above the button, as the agent save bar does (501 reads "That isn't available yet.").
      - The content gets 64pt more bottom padding, so the bar never covers the lowest labels.
    - `ProfileV3Toast` gained `isError` (red wash and badge with `priorityHigh`, plain grey text). The trait page uses it for action failures, so an error no longer shows a success check.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` now cover drafting and the save button, a refused save keeping its draft, dragging back to the saved score, and the config blocking every edit (8 in all).
60. **Past trips wired to the backend (user, 2026-10-19; backend note "WorldPass globe + past trips (2026-10-18)").**
    - Before: Live kept trips in an in-memory `ProfileV3PastTripsStore.shared`, so nothing reached the server and trips vanished on relaunch. The card's pills were hidden behind `isPastTripsEnabled = false`, and the expanded card merged locally added codes into the pass.
    - Card: `worldpass_card.globe.add_trips` and `trips_pill` decode into `ProfileV3WorldPass.tripsEntry` (`ProfileV3PastTripsMapper.entry`).
      - `.add` draws `ProfileV3AddPastTripsPill` with the server label.
      - `.pill` draws the served flags (≤5, latest first) and "+N" from `more`. The flags follow `next` (`go:"past_trips"`), and the + follows `add.next` (`go:"add_trip"`); they are separate tap targets now.
      - The server sending the pill is the gate, so `isPastTripsEnabled` is gone. Stat placeholders show only when stats are empty and the pill is served.
    - Routes and API: `.pastTrips(profileID:)` and `.addTrip(profileID:)`. `GET …/board/past_trips?profile_id=` and `GET …/board/past_trips/add?profile_id=`.
    - Writes: POST `/board/submit` through `ProfileV3BoardSubmitRequest`, which echoes the served `submit` and adds fields. `ProfileV3AnyCodingKey` is now internal for it.
      - add: `submit` + `country` + `year` + `month`;
      - edit: the trip's `edit.submit` + `year` + `month`;
      - delete: the trip's `delete.submit`.
      - Errors (422 `invalid_trip_month`, 404 `trip_not_found`, 502 `trip_save_failed`) show the server message, falling back to the local strings.
    - `ProfileV3PastTripsRepository` is now `loadPastTrips` / `loadAddTrip` (screens) and `addPastTrip` / `editPastTrip` / `deletePastTrip` (actions). Live calls the endpoints. The store and its fixtures are DEBUG-only and back the Mock, which serves screens shaped like the contract.
    - Screens:
      - The page title and delete-confirm copy come from `past_trips`; each trip's Edit/Delete labels and actions come with it; `name` and `month_label` replace the local lookups; `image_url ""` keeps the destination placeholder.
      - The add sheet uses `add_trip`: the country title, placeholder, CTA and list (searched on the client), and the duration title, labels, years, months, `max` (no month after it) and `default`. The page preloads it.
      - Edit opens straight at the duration step with the screen's `duration` ("Save"), since the server only edits year and month.
      - Local strings and a 80-year local duration are fallbacks only.
    - After any write, the page reloads (a new trip is matched by country/year/month to play its reveal) and the hub refreshes the board. The expanded card reads `latestPasses` (from `home.worldPasses`), so its globe and pill update without the old local merge (`mergingVisited`, `pastTripCodes` and `loadPastTripCodes` were removed).
    - Fixtures: the main pass serves the flags pill, and the co-traveller serves "ADD PAST TRIPS".
    - Tests: `ProfileV3PastTripsTests` (9) cover the card pills, the page and add screens, the `max` cut-off, the submit bodies, the routes and endpoints, and the add/edit/delete flows against a stub repository.
61. **App access stores credentials in the fort-knox vault, and agent saves can apply to household travellers.** This work was found uncommitted on 2026-10-19 at 21:28. Another session wrote it after this file was last saved at 18:14, and no peer session was active. It is recorded here from the code and was verified only by build and tests.
    - Vault client: `ProfileV3VaultClient`, owned by `ProfileV3LiveRepository`, calls `/api/fort-knox/v1` through 12 new `ProfileV3API` cases: public key, sites, credential create/read/update/delete, retest, pause and resume actions, device register and delete, and a reveal challenge plus reveal.
      - Live `loadAppAccess`, `saveAppCredentials`, `addAppPassword` and `disconnectApp` now go through it. On the Live path this replaces item 58's release `notImplemented` stubs.
      - The Mock serves no sites and throws `notImplemented` for the new vault calls.
    - Crypto (`ProfileV3VaultCrypto`):
      - A credential body is sealed with AES-GCM under a fresh 256-bit key, with the token uid as associated data. The key is wrapped with the server's RSA public key (PEM, OAEP-SHA256) and sent with `key_id`.
      - A reveal opens an AES-GCM box keyed by HKDF-SHA256 over an ECDH secret between the server's ephemeral key and the device key.
      - `ProfileV3VaultDevice` keeps a P-256 key in the Keychain (`WhenPasscodeSetThisDeviceOnly`), stores the enrolled device id (`AfterFirstUnlockThisDeviceOnly`), and signs the reveal challenge.
    - UI: `ProfileV3AppAccess` gained `vault` and `usesVault`. `ProfileV3VaultViews` adds the vault list and a site picker.
    - Household:
      - The agent screen's `save.household` maps to `ProfileV3AgentForm.household` (`ProfileV3HouseholdPrompt`: eyebrow, icon, title, travellers with pass numbers, confirm label).
      - Save then opens `ProfileV3HouseholdApplySheet` so the user can tick travellers. `saveAgentForm(_:profileID:profileIDs:)` sends them and returns the server's done label.
      - A 502 `preferences_write_failed` maps to `ProfileV3Error.partialSave(message:failedIDs:savedIDs:)`, which the sheet shows. Without `household` the save is direct, as before, and `onSaved` (item 50) still fires on success.
    - Errors: `ProfileV3ErrorEnvelopePlugin` stores the parsed envelope (message, `code`, ids) in `ProfileV3ServerFailureBox.shared`, and `ProfileV3APIErrorMapper` consumes it. The backend `code` therefore reaches `ProfileV3Error` again (see Known trade-offs). Any 401 whose message mentions "re-authentication" maps to `server(code: "reauth")` with "confirm it's you" copy. Other 401s stay `unauthorized`.
    - Agents header: an empty `heading` now shows only the emphasis. `ProfileV3TwoToneTitle` drops the empty lead, and the local `agentsHeadingLead` fallback is no longer used there.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultCryptoTests` (3) checks that a sealed body opens only for the token uid, that a reveal opens with the phone key, and that a conflict keeps the existing credential id. Three new `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests` cover the empty heading, the household sheet submitting every ticked traveller, and a save without household staying direct.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 59/59 pass. Not run against the fort-knox backend, on a device, or in the app.
62. **Saved login detail and delete, per the fort-knox app guide (user, 2026-10-20, "fix all the open questions which answers").** The guide answers how the password is shown (sections 3, 5, 6) and how to disconnect (section 5). It does not answer what the Connected button shows, so that is unchanged.
    - Detail: tapping a vault row still opens the `.vaultEdit` credentials sheet, which is now the guide's detail screen. It shows the website from `url` read-only with the open-link accessory (`ProfileV3CredentialField.isEditable`), the full username from `?include_username=true`, and the password as black dots (`prompt`, `ProfileV3VaultStrings.passwordHidden`) with the eye.
    - Reveal: the eye still runs the Face ID reveal. The revealed password is kept apart from what the user types, so it doesn't count as a change, the eye hides it again, and the 30-second and background hides no longer wipe a new password the user typed. Saving sends only the fields that changed.
    - Clipboard: while the stored password is showing, a copy of it is replaced on the pasteboard by a `localOnly` item that expires after 30 seconds (`ProfileV3VaultClipboard`), so iOS clears it even if the app is suspended.
    - Delete: "Delete login" under the note swaps the sheet for `ProfileV3ConfirmationView.deleteLogin`, then calls `DELETE /credentials/{id}`, closes the sheet and reloads the list. A refusal returns to the form with the server message.
    - Removed: the `detail` path in `ProfileV3AppAccessModel` (reveal, copy, retry, delete, pause/resume), which no view showed. The repository calls for retest, pause and resume remain.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (7) covers the detail fields, reveal and hide with an edit, an unchanged reveal sending only the username, delete after confirming, a refused delete, sheets that can't delete, and the clipboard expiry.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 66/66 pass. Rendered the sheet and confirm with `ImageRenderer`. Not run against the fort-knox backend, on a device, or in the app.
63. **Reveal failures show in the sheet (user, 2026-10-20: on an iPhone the eye made no request, stayed crossed, and the password stayed dots).**
    - Cause: on a phone that isn't registered yet (`ProfileV3VaultDevice.deviceID()` is nil), registering needs a sign-in in the last 10 minutes (guide 6a.2). For an older session the client stops with the re-auth message before any request. The message went to `onFailure`, which reaches Ayl's `showStatusMessage`, a TODO that only logs, and would sit behind the sheet anyway. So nothing showed.
    - The 10-minute check now runs before Face ID, so the user isn't asked for Face ID only to be refused. It logs `[ProfileV3] vault_reveal stopped: …` through `ATCoreLogger`.
    - The sheet shows a spinner in place of the eye while revealing and the failure in red under the password field (`revealError`). A cancelled Face ID shows nothing. `confirmItsYou` now says to sign in again and show the password within 10 minutes.
    - Still missing: the guide's "Confirm it's you" step. `ATAuthKit.start` returns the current user at once when a non-anonymous session exists, and ATAuthKit has no re-authenticate call, so a fresh sign-in needs ATAuthKit work. Until then, signing out and in, then revealing within 10 minutes, registers the phone; later reveals don't need a recent sign-in.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` gained two (a failed reveal explains itself in the sheet, a cancelled Face ID leaves no error).
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 68/68 pass. Not run on a device or against the backend.
64. **Secure credential store, from the password vault boards (user, 2026-10-20; Figma `3664:16930` settings, `3664:13919` store sheet, `3664:14278` / `3664:14681` / `3664:15084` add, `3664:15491` / `3664:15900` saved login, `3664:16317` delete; "if any ui is missed, add it").** The boards make the vault its own entry in Settings, separate from App access.
    - Settings: `ProfileV3CredentialStoreCard` sits under the Connectors card (30pt gap). It has the dark 219.7° gradient, the 2.37pt inset bottom edge, two `ProfileV3ShieldShape` fills (the Figma SVGs carry drop-shadow filters that asset catalogs don't draw, and the outline is the same shape) and the lock art, bundled as `profile_v3_vault_lock` (`ProfileV3ConnectorGlyph.vaultLock`). Gated by `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isCredentialStoreEnabled` (DEBUG only, like App access).
    - Store sheet: `ProfileV3CredentialStoreSheet`, presented by `.profileV3CredentialStore(_:)` on the settings root and driven by `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel`. Silver shield with the lock, title, subtitle, one 86pt row per saved login (globe, `display_name`, chevron, a spinner while it opens), and "+ Add credentials". Rows carry no status badge, as on the boards (the guide also holds badges until the login check ships), so the 60-second status poll is gone.
    - Add: mode `.vaultNew`, the existing "Add new password" sheet (website, username, password, Continue, spinner, "Password added successfully"). There is no site picker on the boards, so the typed website is matched to `GET /sites` by host (`ProfileV3VaultSite.matching`, `www.` ignored, subdomains match) and sent as `site_slug`. Otherwise it goes as `label` (bare host) plus `url`. A 409 opens the saved login. `.vaultSite`, `.vaultCustom`, the label field and `ProfileV3VaultSitePicker` / `ProfileV3VaultList` are removed.
    - Saved login (`.vaultEdit`): no shield, title or note. Fields start 134pt down, with the website read-only and shown without the scheme (`displayURL`). A dashed divider 164pt below, then the red "Delete account" row (`ProfileV3SettingsRow`, which gained `iconSlot`). "save changes" shows only once something changed; it keeps its space so the sheet height doesn't jump. Save and delete failures show in red under the password field (`inlineError`, renamed from `revealError`).
    - Delete confirm: `ProfileV3ConfirmationView.deleteLogin(subject:)` with `Metrics.subject`. "Are you sure you want to delete your password credentials?", the website in 14pt at 50% black on one line, "Proceed to delete" / "Don't delete".
    - App access goes back to apps only: `ProfileV3AppAccess` lost `vault` / `usesVault`. Live `loadAppAccess`, `saveAppCredentials`, `addAppPassword` and `disconnectApp` serve `ProfileV3AppAccessStore.shared` in DEBUG and throw `notImplemented` in release, as before item 61. The vault list is the new `loadVaultCredentials()`. The Mock serves `ProfileV3VaultStore` fixtures (myhdfcbank.com, abc.com) for previews and tests.
    - Strings: six new catalogue keys (`mo-aiprofile-agents-label-credential-store`, `-description-credential-store`, `-description-credential-store-sheet`, `-button-add-stored-credentials`, `-button-delete-credentials`, `-confirmation-delete-credentials`); add them in Ditto. "Delete account" on the saved-login row is the boards' copy. Unused `ProfileV3VaultStrings` and the status badge mapping are removed.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (14) now runs through `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel`: listing and opening, the save button appearing on change, reveal cases, delete and refused delete, a known site sent by slug, an unknown website sent by host and address, a 409 opening the saved login, host matching and display addresses, and clipboard expiry.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 72/72 pass. Rendered the card, store sheet, add sheet, saved login (before and after an edit) and delete confirm with `ImageRenderer` and compared them with the boards. Not run on a device or against the backend.
65. **Website picker before adding, and App access shows "Coming soon" (user, 2026-10-20: "present the picker once the user clicks on add cta and fill the website link there"; "keep the build demo page but for now let's show coming soon").**
    - "+ Add credentials" opens `ProfileV3VaultSitePicker` first (no board; styled like the store rows): title "Add credentials", a Website search field, one row per `GET /sites` site sorted by name (filtered by name or address in `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel.sites(matching:)`), and a last "Other website" row (new key `mo-aiprofile-agents-button-other-website`).
    - Picking a site opens "Add new password" (`.vaultNew(site:)`) with the website filled from its `login_url` (shown without the scheme). Saving sends `site_slug` when the website still matches that site's host, with no second `/sites` call. "Other website" opens it with whatever was typed in the search (`.vaultNew(website:)`), and saving matches against `/sites` as before, else sends `label` plus `url`.
    - The store sheet presents one `.sheet(item:)` routed by `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel.Sheet` (`.picker` / `.editor`), so the picker swaps straight to the add sheet. `closeEditor` became `closeSheet`. The picker and sheet header share `ProfileV3VaultSheetHeader`.
    - App access: `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isAppAccessEnabled` is now `false` in every build, so the Connectors card shows `ProfileV3AppAccessComingSoonView`. The demo page, its model, fixtures and tests are kept.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (15): the add flow now goes through the picker (sorting, filtering, a picked site filling the website and saving by slug), closing the picker adds nothing, and "Other website" carries the search text.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 73/73 pass. Rendered the picker and the add sheet after a pick with `ImageRenderer`. Not run on a device or against the backend.
66. **Face ID before the vault opens (user, 2026-10-20: "While opening the vault - we should ask faceid and then open it").**
    - `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel.open()` now awaits an injected `unlock` (default `ProfileV3VaultDevice.unlockVault()`) before it sets `isPresented` or loads anything. `isUnlocking` blocks a second tap. A cancel leaves the vault closed with nothing fetched.
    - `unlockVault()` evaluates `.deviceOwnerAuthentication` (Face ID, with the passcode as the system fallback) with reason `ProfileV3VaultStrings.unlockVault`. When the policy can't be evaluated (no biometrics and no passcode, as on most simulators) it returns `.unlocked`, since there is nothing to check against.
    - Revealing a password still runs its own biometric prompt and Secure Enclave signature (guide 6b), so opening the vault and then revealing asks twice. Reusing the unlock for the reveal would loosen the guide's "one Face ID per reveal" and is left for the backend team to decide.
    - Ayl: `NSFaceIDUsageDescription` now reads "Enable $(PRODUCT_NAME) to use Face ID to open your saved passwords and show them." (`plutil -lint` OK).
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (17): nothing loads before the unlock, the sheet opens and loads after it, a double tap asks once, and a cancelled unlock keeps the vault closed. Every other store test injects `unlock: { .unlocked }`.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 75/75 pass. Not run on a device.
67. **Delete confirm is pushed, like the settings sheets (user, 2026-10-20: "presenting the delete bottom sheet from the opened vault creds entry is a bit awkward - let's use the push flow we have used in the settings flow").**
    - `ProfileV3AppCredentialsView` wraps the sheet in a `NavigationStack` whose path is derived from `isConfirmingDelete`. "Delete account" pushes `ProfileV3ConfirmationView.deleteLogin`, and its back button or "Don't delete" pops (`cancelDelete`). This replaces item 63's in-place opacity swap. A failed delete pops back with the error under the password; a successful one closes the sheet.
    - The detent still follows the visible screen. As in `ProfileV3SettingsPremeasure`, a hidden copy of the confirm in the container's background measures it before the push, so the sheet shrinks in step with the slide. `onDisappear(perform: cancel)` stays on the outer container so the push doesn't cancel work.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 75/75 pass. `ImageRenderer` can't draw a `NavigationStack`, so the push animation is unverified until it runs on a device.
68. **Settings felt slow: hidden work removed (user, 2026-10-20: "while using the settings module the app is so slow and sluggish").**
    - Measured on the simulator before changing anything:
      - One frame of the WorldPass card globe (`KYCGlobeLand`, 40k samples) took 2.6 ms of main-thread drawing, 3.3 ms with rasterising.
      - Per render, a string accessor costs 2.5 µs, `typoGraphy`'s font metrics 0.23 µs and `Color(at:)` 0.18 µs, so those are not the problem.
      - Ditto overrides (a regex per lookup) never load in Ayl, because nothing calls `configure`/`refresh`.
    - Main cause: settings and the programmes page are presented `.overFullScreen`, which keeps the hub mounted underneath. Its card globe (`TimelineView(.animation)`) and the card hover kept redrawing every frame behind an opaque page.
      - `ProfileV3ScreenCover.shared` counts covering pages. `ProfileV3PageHostingController` and `ProfileV3ProgrammesHostingController` begin on `viewDidAppear` and end when they start leaving (`isLeavingScreen`), or on `viewDidDisappear`. Each uses a `ProfileV3ScreenCoverToken`, so the count stays balanced.
      - `ProfileV3HubRoot` passes `profileV3PausesAnimations` into the environment. The card globe and hover fold it into their existing running switch, so the globe rests at its angle and resumes from it. The detail globe and the pulse rings pause their `TimelineView`.
      - The hub resumes as the page starts to slide away.
    - `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel` is now `@Observable`; `sheetHeights`, `router` and tasks are `@ObservationIgnored`. The page and sheet use `@Bindable`.
      - Before, sheet navigation (`sheetEntry`, `sheetPath`) re-rendered the whole settings page, including its hidden sheets. Now only views that read a changed property re-render.
      - `ProfileV3SettingsAccountScreen` holds the settings model as a plain `let`, because it only calls `pop`.
    - The three hidden "premeasure" sheets (logout, delete account, permissions) were laid out on every page render.
      - Each is now measured once and then dropped (`ProfileV3SettingsPremeasure`).
      - Permissions is measured only after it loads. Before, a hidden copy span a loading spinner forever when the menu had no permissions row, and played an error haptic when permissions failed.
    - The menu loads once per settings presentation (`loadMenuIfNeeded` on appear). Before, every return from Information access or App access refetched the menu and the citizenship field and re-rendered the page mid-transition.
    - `clearDeletionError()` no longer publishes on every keystroke while there is no error.
    - Not changed: the Information access / App access hero (two large blurs plus a masked shadow) costs some GPU time while scrolling. Profile it on a device before reworking it.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3SettingsRenderTests` (4): balanced cover count, the menu loading once, sheet navigation not notifying the page's reads, and no publish while typing.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 79/79 pass. Not yet profiled on a device.
69. **Delete confirm is its own sheet, presented like the add sheet (user, 2026-10-20: "for the delete login account flow - let's just present it as we are doing it for the add cred sheet. sliding is also awkward").** This replaces item 67's push.
    - `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel.Sheet` gained `.deleteConfirm(editor)`. The vault sheet's one `.sheet(item:)` swaps the saved login for `ProfileV3DeleteLoginSheet`, the same way the site picker swaps to the add sheet: the saved login goes down and the confirm comes up. The sheets are never stacked.
    - Routing follows the editor's existing `isConfirmingDelete` through `onConfirmingDeleteChange`. "Delete account" opens the confirm. Back or "Don't delete" returns to the same saved login (`model.editor` is the same view model, so edits and errors survive).
      - A refused delete returns to the saved login with the error under the password.
      - A successful delete closes it and reloads the list.
      - Swiping the confirm down closes it, as swiping any vault sheet does. It can't be swiped while deleting.
    - `ProfileV3AppCredentialsView` lost its `NavigationStack`, the hidden measuring copy of the confirm and the confirm detent. `ProfileV3DeleteLoginSheet` fits its own height (confirm plus bottom inset), with the same 40pt corners.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3VaultLoginTests` (17). The delete test now checks the sheet swaps both ways. A new test covers a refused delete returning to the saved login, and closing the confirm closing both.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 80/80 pass. The sheet swap animation is unverified until it runs on a device.
70. **Release `1.0.1`: README version line and `CHANGELOG.md` (user, 2026-10-20: "commit and tag 1.0.1").** `1.0.0` had been tagged on `07d0531` without the version line and changelog that item 40 asks for, and the README still said there was no release tag.
    - `README.md`: "**Version 1.0.1**", versions described as git tags on `master`, and the dependency line `from: "1.0.1"` in place of `branch: "master"`. "What the host must still provide" gained `NSFaceIDUsageDescription`: the credential store prompts before opening and before a reveal, and iOS ends an app that prompts without the key.
    - `CHANGELOG.md`, in the AI-KYC changelog's shape: `1.0.1` covers Done items 62–69 (credential store, site picker, Face ID unlock, delete sheet, reveal errors, App access back to "Coming soon", the settings performance work), the Info.plist key, and the seven new string keys for Ditto. `1.0.0` gets a one-paragraph summary.
    - Tag: `1.0.1` is annotated, like `1.0.0`.
    - Verified before tagging: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 80/80 pass.
    - Committed with Done items 62–69 as `955ecaf` (2026-10-20, "Add credential store, Face ID unlock and settings speedups", 45 files), and tagged `1.0.1` there. Neither is pushed: `master` is one commit ahead of `origin/master` (`07d0531`), and the tag exists only locally until `git push origin master 1.0.1`.
71. **Client config to skip the vault's 10-minute sign-in check (user, 2026-10-20: "let's have a config and bypass this check alone - the 10 minute check").**
    - `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isVaultRecentSignInCheckEnabled = false`. While it's off, `ProfileV3VaultClient.signedInRecently()` returns `true`, so neither client gate stops an unregistered phone: the early one before Face ID in `revealPassword` and the one before `POST /devices` in `enrolledDeviceID`. The reveal then goes Face ID, `POST /devices`, challenge, reveal.
    - The server can still enforce guide 6a.2: a 401 "re-authentication required" from `POST /devices` maps to the same `confirmItsYou` message under the password. Turning the flag back on restores the `lastSignInDate` check (which is account-wide and cached, not the token's `auth_time` the server reads).
    - After the `1.0.1` tag, so not in it.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 80/80 pass. Not run on a device or against the backend.
72. **Traveller profile "processing" card, built but not wired (user, 2026-10-20; Figma `3309:68726`, card `3309:69428`, loader `5052:11874`: "There are some cases where the spidy sense data will take time to process. Till that time, we need to show this component. For now, let's just build it.").**
    - `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard(title:traits:animates:)` is a drop-in for the persona radar card: the same `personaCardGradient` card and title/`radarTop`/314pt shell, with `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingDial` in place of the radar and "Decoding your travel choices, won’t take long..." (new key `mo-aiprofile-agents-status-persona-processing`, add it in Ditto) under it with the board's static grey-white shimmer gradient. Nothing shows it yet: there's no backend signal for "still processing". When there is, `ProfileV3TravelPersonaContent` can swap it in for `radarCard`, passing `persona.profileTitle` and `persona.traits.map(\.name)`.
    - Dial: `ProfileV3PersonaLoader` draws the 172pt loader natively from the node data (the SVGs carry filters and a `foreignObject` conic gradient). It has a white fill, the 7.8pt `F0EEEF`→`989898` rim, the hidden 2.5pt ring, the 65pt knob (`DAD8D8`→`9D9D9D`, white→`747373` edge, eroded multiply drop shadow), and the 270° round-capped arc stroked with the angular gradient at 70% (start 29.84°, from the export's matrix). Over these sit the inset top shadow and the drop shadow, all at 80% opacity.
    - Motion (`get_motion_context`, one 4s loop): one `TimelineView` drives the arc's 360° linear rotation and a colour wave (`111827` → `6E3DE0` → `D62A9C` → `F5A6D1` → `D62A9C` → `111827` over 15.75% of the loop) that runs clockwise through the ten labels from each label's board start (`ProfileV3PersonaProcessingSlot.waveStart`, interpolated in sRGB). The board also restarts each label's fade-in every loop; that is played once, as the radar's entrance, with the same 0.95s + 70ms stagger and 0.55s curve, because restarting would blank the labels every 4s and hide the wave crossing the loop boundary. Reduce Motion (and `animates: false`) shows the resting frame.
    - Labels sit at the board's ten slots (`ProfileV3PersonaProcessingSlot.all`, edges measured from the dial's centre, clockwise from Adventure), 72pt wide like the radar. "Culture & History" breaks as "Culture / & History", as on the radar.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingTests` (3): the wave matches the board's keyframes, wraps across the loop (Social and Outdoors at 0%, within one colour step), and the ten slots run clockwise.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 83/83 pass. Rendered the card and loader with `ImageRenderer` and compared them with the board; the motion is unverified until it runs on a device.
73. **Debug menu in Settings for the persona processing card and loader (user, 2026-10-20: "let's add a debug menu under the settings for presenting this ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard & ProfileV3PersonaLoader"; the Xcode preview had crashed inside `PreviewsInjection`'s `_typesThatConform`, before any package code ran).**

Rows "Persona processing card" and "Persona loader" open `ProfileV3DebugShowcaseSheet`: the card with the fixture trait names, or the loader spinning on the 4s loop (`ProfileV3DebugSpinningLoader`). Two buttons: "Show still frame / Animate" (the card's `animates`; the loader pauses at rest), and "Replay", which remounts the content with `.id` to replay the label entrance and restart the loop.
