74. **No box behind connected email addresses on Information access (user, 2026-10-20, device screenshot: "Please remove the grey boxes in the mail id").** `ProfileV3ConnectedEmailChip` lost its white capsule background and its 2pt leading / 8pt trailing padding, so the Gmail logo lines up under the Email card's mail icon. The 2pt vertical padding stays, so the card height doesn't change. `InformationAccess.chipTrailing` is removed as unused. Verified: `xcodebuild … build`, 0 errors, 0 warnings. Not run on a device.
75. **Seven-point list (user, 2026-10-20): debug KYC states, empty coupons, no client fallbacks, flip and reorder hints 5 times, `worldpass_label`, "traveler".** The user chose "server-field fallbacks only" and "`worldpass_card.worldpass_label`" when asked.
    - **Debug KYC states.** `ProfileV3DebugKYCScenario` (whole file `#if DEBUG`, plain English labels) has Live, No passport, No KYC and Re-KYC, stored in `UserDefaults` (`profileV3.debug.kycScenario`). `ProfileV3BoardStore.decode` applies it to every board it maps, fetched or restored. Only the main traveler changes: their pass wherever it appears and their home's KYC fields. No passport: not started, 2/3 progress, capture steps `[passport]`. No KYC: not started, steps passport, selfie and Gmail. Re-KYC: expired, red glow, line "KYC expired", steps passport and selfie. `ProfileV3SettingsDebugSection` (DEBUG) sits under the settings rows. Picking a state posts `didChange`, and `ProfileV3HubView` answers with `requestRefresh()`. Re-KYC means expired, not the Gmail reconnect banner (`needsKYCReconnect`). The backend's `/kyc/v1/test/control` hook (used by the Ayl debug Console's Reset KYC) may have matching scenarios, but only `{profiles: "none"}` is known here.
    - **Empty coupons.** `ProfileV3CouponsListView` shows `ProfileV3EmptyState` with the `localOffer` icon and "No coupons available." (`mo-aiprofile-loyalty-empty-state-no-coupons`, `ProfileV3Strings.noCoupons`) when the list has loaded with no coupons.
    - **Server-field fallbacks removed.** A label the server can send is shown only when it does; otherwise the element hides. Mappers give `""` or `nil`. Items without their label are dropped: permission options, relationship and add-traveller options, trip actions, the add-trips pill, relationship entries and the concierge card. Views hide empty titles, buttons and rows. Shared pieces hide on empty too: `ProfileV3ConfirmationView` (title and each button), `ProfileV3SettingsCardHeader`, `ProfileV3OfferDetailScaffold` (terms title and terms), `ProfileV3NudgeCard`, `ProfileV3KYCGlow`, `ProfileV3KYCRedirectOverlay` (status), `ProfileV3BenefitCardStack` ("view all").
    - Covered: settings, permissions, connectors and the disconnect flow, coupons, benefit terms (now `termsTitle` from `terms_title`), past trips and their duration labels, terms, travelers, the KYC status, tag and banner, the agents heading, airport pickup, the agent tag, picker titles and the household done label, loyalty entry fields, passport screens, the medical lock toggle, and the programme insight pill. Also removed: `ProfileV3KYCStatus.title/subtitle`, `ProfileV3RelationshipEntry.title(for:)` and `ProfileV3Strings.fieldLabel(fromKey:)`; connector names are no longer normalised.
    - Kept as client copy: errors and toasts, accessibility, input placeholders, the "—" value placeholder, country and month names, the "Traveler" name for a nameless record, the sources-pill count, and static headers that never read a server field (Edit travelers, the Connectors deck, `kycRegainAccess`).
    - 52 `ProfileV3Strings` accessors became unused, or were used only by fixtures and previews. They are deleted along with 64 catalogue keys and their generated accessors, `...-worldpass-name` among them. DEBUG fixtures and previews now use literals.
    - **Flip demo, 5 times.** `ProfileV3HubHints.flipDemoShows` (limit 5, key `profileV3.flipDemoShows`) replaces `hasSeenFlipDemo`. The demo plays only on the main traveler's card (`flipDemoPassID`), and `markFlipSeen` counts one visit. The hold hint is due once a demo has shown, and it waits while the demo is pending on the selected card.
    - **Reorder discovery, 5 times.** `programmeReorderHintShows` (limit 5) replaces `hasSeenProgrammeReorderHint`. A show is counted when the hint appears; "Got it" and Edit only hide it.
    - **WorldPass label.** `worldpass_card.worldpass_label` maps to `ProfileV3WorldPass.brand`. The header (card and detail) reads "%1$@ worldpass" (`...-worldpass-branded`), trimmed, so it shows "worldpass" when the label is missing. Fixtures use "atlys".
    - **Traveler.** 13 catalogue values and their generated fallbacks, plus fixture copy, now say "traveler". Keys, identifiers, routes, JSON keys and analytics are unchanged.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3DiscoveryHintTests` (9 tests). Three relationship tests now expect untitled options to be dropped. Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 91/91 pass; Release build, 0 errors, 0 warnings. Image renders of the empty state, debug section and branded header were checked. Not run on a device.
76. **Settings globe letter falls back to the `/board` main traveller (user, 2026-10-20: "when the name we are getting from the app is nil - then let's use the main traveler's name from the profile's /board").**
    - Order: the host's `account.snapshot().firstName`. If that is nil or blank, the cached board's `mainProfile.name`. If no board is cached, `loadMonogramIfNeeded()` on appear fetches it (`loadLatestBoard()`, which shares an in-flight fetch). The letter is still the lowercased first character (`ProfileV3SettingsViewModel.monogram(from:)`).
    - Ayl's host gives the Firebase sign-in display name, which phone and email sign-ins usually leave empty; vdcios gives the main traveller's first name.
    - `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel` takes an optional `board: (any ProfileV3BoardRepository)?`, and `monogram` is now observable state. `ProfileV3SettingsSheetPresenter` gained `board:`, which the hub host and `ProfileV3EntryPoint.presentAddTraveller` pass as their repository.
    - A failed fetch leaves no letter and retries on the next appear. `cancelReads` stops it.
    - `ProfileV3SettingsDependencies.preview` gained `firstName:`.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3SettingsMonogramTests` (4): the app's name wins; nil or blank falls back to the cached board; with nothing cached the board is fetched; with no board there is no letter.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors, 0 warnings, 95/95 pass. Not run on a device.
77. **Programme tile at the card's aspect ratio, Figma text placement, and the insight prompt into AI chat (user, 2026-10-20, device screenshots: "We should respect the aspect ratio of the card being rendered - because the card is being cut if not honoured"; Figma frame `4635:2441`, tile `4635:2479`: "make the placements of the text accordingly"; "let's use the prompt we get in the request and pass it to open the ai chat view"; "view insights button is missing in the card … keep this one in client").**
    - Aspect: `ProfileV3ProgrammeTile` was a fixed 253pt tall and drew the art height-first. Wider art ran off the right edge, which cut the Star Alliance badge on Air India and United faces. The tile is now `.aspectRatio(cardAspect, .fit)`, width-driven. `cardAspect` is the loaded image's own ratio, then the server's `art_aspect` (now decoded on `ProfileV3ProgrammeRowDTO`, mapped to `ProfileV3LoyaltyProgramme.artAspect`), then `Programme.artAspectRatio` (1140/720). The art simply fills, so `renderedArtWidth`/`artOffset` are gone. `ProfileV3ProgrammeCard` (pass back face) also prefers `art_aspect` before its image loads. `ProgrammeTile.height` and `Programmes.reorderStep` are removed: the skeletons use `tileWidth / artAspectRatio`, and the reorder demo measures the first two tiles (`demoTileHeights`).
    - Placement, per Figma: row 1 is the name (no art or placeholder art) and "POINTS" (T1 Bold 11, uppercase, `CardShade.label` = 50%). Row 2, 6pt below, is tier │ membership number ⧉ and the points value (H4 Semibold 24). The number is Inter Bold 13 with 1.04 tracking (`Typography.programmeNumberSize/Tracking`). Row 3, 6pt below, is expiry (`shade.faint`) and the updated line (`shade.secondary`, trailing, wraps). The insight follows 20pt below, then the pill, 24pt from the bottom (`pillBottom`), with at least 20pt above it (`bottomRowReserve`). The insight takes whatever height is left (`layoutPriority(-1)`) and truncates. At 361pt wide that is about 2 lines; Figma's 380pt frame fits 3. The lowercase "points" under the value is gone.
    - The user first asked for the updated line "at the bottom" and picked bottom-right on the pill row. That shipped briefly, then the Figma request superseded it: it is back on row 3 under the value.
    - Insight pill: the tile's pill reads the client "View insight" (`mo-aiprofile-general-button-view-insight`, restored after item 75 deleted it). It shows whenever the programme has `insight` or `insightDirective`; it no longer waits for a server label.
    - Insight prompt → chat: the insight response's `continue.next` is `{"go": "ai_chat", "prompt": "…"}`. `ProfileV3DirectiveDTO` decodes `prompt`, `ProfileV3Directive.prompt` carries it, and the route is `.aiChat(prompt:)`, `.unsupported` when the prompt is blank. `ProfileV3ProgrammeInsight.continueDirective` keeps `continue.next`, and `chatPrompt` reads it. The insight view's Continue shows when `onContinue` is set and the label isn't empty. `ProfileV3ProgrammesHostingController.continueAction` sets it: for a prompt with a chat-capable host, it dismisses the insight (the programmes page stays) and calls `events.onOpenChat(prompt)`, after logging the CTA. Otherwise it falls back to the old flight-agent path, still behind `isProgrammeInsightContinueEnabled`.
    - Host API: `ProfileV3AppHost` gained `opensChat` and `openChat(prompt:)`, defaulting to `false` and a no-op, so the vdcios host (`AppIntegration/VDCProfileV3AppHost`) compiles unchanged and never shows the chat Continue. Ayl's `AYLProfileV3AppHost` returns `true` and calls `ChatDeepLinkPresenter.present(ChatDeepLinkRequest(text: prompt, context: nil, sessionId: nil))`, which opens the chat sheet over the top-most screen and sends the prompt. Ayl needs a released AI-Profile with this API before that change can merge.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3ProgrammeInsightChatTests` (3): the prompt is read and trimmed; a blank or absent prompt opens nothing; `art_aspect` is decoded and zero is ignored. Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 98/98 pass, including the pill-label change. Debug and Release package builds: 0 errors, 0 warnings. Ayl builds against the local package (arm64 simulator; the default two-arch simulator build fails to link TwilioVoice for x86_64, unrelated). Tile layout checked with an `ImageRenderer` render at 361pt wide (no art). Not run on a device.
78. **Backend board notes of 2026-10-20 (user paste: delete account served, 503 retry, board cache, passport back only for Indian passports), plus the home-airport picker icon (user, screenshot: "only when icon is there show this!").**
    - Delete account is server-driven now. `go: delete_account` → `.deleteAccount` loads `GET …/board/settings/delete-account` (`ProfileV3DeleteAccountReasonDTO` → `ProfileV3DeleteAccountReason`: title, subtitle, field placeholder, submit label). `go: delete_confirm` → the new `.deleteAccountConfirm` loads `…/delete-account/confirm` (`ProfileV3DeleteAccountConfirmDTO` → title, warning, proceed and cancel labels, picked by key `proceed`/`cancel` or by `action: delete_account` / `next.go: back`). The order follows the server: reason first (full-height sheet), then confirm (measured sheet). It was confirm then reason before.
    - `ProfileV3AccountSettingsModel.tapDeleteAccount` pushes the reason and loads both screens with `ProfileV3PhasedLoad`, so the confirm is pre-measured (`ProfileV3SettingsPremeasure` now observes the account model) before the user gets there. Each screen shows `ProfileV3SettingsLoadState` with Retry until it loads. `submitDeletionReason` keeps the reason and pushes the confirm. Proceed (`confirmDeletion`) is the app's existing native deletion, unchanged: `logAccountDeletionReason`, `requestAccountDeletion` (the Slack-alert endpoint), `markAccountDeletionRequested`, flash, dismiss. Nothing is posted to `/board/submit`. A failed deletion now shows a failure flash; there is no inline error anymore. Cancel pops back.
    - Removed the built-in copy that named "Atlys": `mo-aiprofile-account-confirmation-delete`, `…-deletion-reason-title`, `…-deletion-reason-description`, `…-deletion-details`, `…-input-reason`, and their accessors. `ProfileV3DeleteReasonView` takes the server screen and hides empty pieces. `ProfileV3ConfirmationView.deleteAccount(_:isDeleting:…)` takes the confirm content. The `typingADeletionReasonPublishesNothing…` test went with `clearDeletionError`.
    - The 503 `kyc_upstream_unavailable` already maps to `ProfileV3Error.transient` (502/503/504), and the new `ProfileV3Error.isRetryable` reads that. Past trips: a first load that fails already shows the Retry panel. A reload or delete that fails with a transient error now offers "Retry" in the alert (`canRetryNotice` / `retryAfterNotice`), which re-runs the same reload or delete (`delete(_:)` was split out of `confirmDelete`). Persona Save and ignore/undo already leave their button enabled with the error shown, so tapping again is the retry. A 404 is still the generic server error.
    - Board cache: the cache keys on `X-Atlys-Client`, `X-Platform` and `X-App-Version`. ATCore's `ATCoreClientHeadersPlugin` is on every provider `ATCoreMoyaProviderFactory` builds, so the package's client already sends all three (`ATCoreClientHeaders.client = "ayl"` in Ayl). vdcios leaves `client` unset, so the backend falls back to Atlys. No client change.
    - Passport back (user, later the same day: "back required true - front + back. back required false then only front"): `ProfileV3PassportScanResponseDTO` decodes `side`, `back_required` and `nationality`. `uploadPassportScan` now returns `ProfileV3PassportScanResult` (field + `backRequired`; `needsBack` was false only for an explicit `false`, so a missing field still asked for the back; since item 87 only an explicit `true` asks for it). `ProfileV3PassportScanVerifier` calls `onBackNotNeeded` after a front upload that says no back. ATCaptureKit's `IDCVerificationOutcome` can't end a flow early, so `ProfileV3PassportScanLauncher` ends it itself: after `Motion.passportFrontOnlyFinishMilliseconds` (600ms, inside the kit's 900ms verified beat, before the flip to the back) it dismisses the capture it presented on `host` and finishes. The request's `onFinish` runs exactly once (`hasFinished`), so the usual reason screen follows. The host's own completion never fires on that path. `true` or missing keeps the single front→back flow with the flip. No ATCaptureKit or host-adapter change was needed: both Ayl and vdcios present the capture from the given presenter. The mock repository returns `backRequired: true`.
    - Home-airport picker: `ProfileV3OptionPickerRow` draws the grey logo circle only when `option.logoURL` exists (options without `image_url`, like airports and languages, get just the checkbox and label).
    - Tests: `ProfileV3DeleteAccountTests` (4: both mappers, the split routes, and proceed running the native deletion with the typed reason), plus `aBrieflyUnavailableDeleteOffersRetry` in `ProfileV3PastTripsTests`. `ProfileV3PassportScanTests` (4) covers the response decoding, `needsBack`, and the verifier skipping the back only after a front with `false`. Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 106/106 pass; Release build, 0 errors, 0 warnings. The early dismissal itself (UIKit) was not exercised; not run on a device.
79. **Glove box (travel inbox) flow, built and reachable from the Settings debug section only (user, 2026-10-21; Figma `5052:13945`, `5142:15574`, `5142:15775`, `5142:15898`, `5142:16416`, `5142:16939`: "implement this flow and wire it up with the debug menu to present it").**
    - Screens: an intro card for locked ("Your travel inbox is ready to unlock" + Complete KYC) and activate ("Activate your email address" + Complete KYC or Activate now). Then the inbox: address pill, Copy, about text, "Emails forwarded to …", and a white sheet with recent emails, or the "Nothing in the Glovebox yet" state. Tapping an email opens a dimmed bottom sheet with sender, summary, status, the CHANGED badge, the agent's note with `ProfileV3AtlysOrb(.still, .insight)` (Figma's orb is the same 68/57/40), and "Tell me more".
    - Files: `Domain/ProfileV3GloveBox.swift` (`ProfileV3GloveBox` with an `.intro`/`.inbox` stage; intro, inbox and email models), `Presentation/ProfileV3GloveBoxViewModel.swift` (stage switch, copy with a 1.5 s "Copied", open/close email, `onIntroAction`/`onFollowUp` closures), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxView.swift` (root + `ProfileV3GloveBoxIntroCard`), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxInboxView.swift` (inbox, rows, logo, meta), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxEmailDetail.swift`, `Components/ProfileV3GloveBoxBlobs.swift` (blob fill + `ProfileV3GloveBoxActionButton`), `Support/ProfileV3Theme+GloveBox.swift` (layout, palette, gradients, motion, `ProfileV3MotionTrack`, `ProfileV3Reveal`).     - Figma's blob and glow SVGs use Gaussian-blur filters and luminosity blending, which asset-catalog SVGs don't render. The colour blobs are drawn in SwiftUI from the SVGs' own ellipses, colours and 25pt blur. The glow is the SVG's circle, gradient stops and 50pt blur. The folder SVG is bundled with its `foreignObject`/blend markup stripped and drawn `.grayscale(1)`. The envelope is bundled as a 600px PNG (`profile_v3_glovebox_envelope`), since ImageKit has no copy (404); it could move there. The "needs attention" diamond is bundled (ATUIKit has no `emergencyHome`); the other status icons are ATUIKit's `mail`, `visibility`, `calendarToday`, `doneAll`.

The pill pops in at screen centre with a bounce, then rises 272pt into place, while two mirrored blob layers drift and settle. Copy, the about text and the forwarding line fade up. The sheet slides up 464pt, then rows fade up 0.32 s apart. A `TimelineView` evaluates `ProfileV3MotionTrack` keyframes (cubic-bezier 0.22/1/0.36/1 and ease-in-out, exact Figma times) for 4.7 s, then pauses. Reduce Motion shows the final frame, and `animatesEntrance: false` does the same for renders.
    - Debug entry: `ProfileV3SettingsDebugSection` gained "Debug · Glove box" with Unlock, Activate (KYC pending), Activate now, Inbox and Empty inbox, presented as a `fullScreenCover`. `ProfileV3GloveBoxDebugFlow` drives it: Complete KYC → Activate now → the inbox, with its entrance. "Tell me more" closes the sheet, since there's no destination yet. Sample data is in `ProfileV3GloveBoxFixtures` (DEBUG); the Emirates and Marriott logos are the media.atlys.com loyalty logos. Only the Emirates detail note is Figma copy; the Marriott and VFS notes are placeholders.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3GloveBoxTests` (5): the pill track, reveal order and rest, intro actions, copy only on the inbox, and email open/close/follow-up. Reading `UIPasteboard` in a test hung the run (every main-actor test timed out), so the copy test doesn't read it back. Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 111/111 pass; Release build, 0 errors, 0 warnings. `ImageRenderer` renders of the unlock, empty, rows and detail screens were compared with the Figma frames (the subtitle and row widths were tuned to Figma's line breaks). Not run on a device; the entrance animation was not watched live.

With the number gone and `Globe.sphereVerticalInset` cut from 22 to 8, the sphere takes the slot's height, ~213pt on a 331pt card. It is still capped at `sphereWidthRatio` of the width. 81. **Disconnect email: no dead space under the terms, and the progress sheet uses the shared bottom sheet (user, 2026-10-21, device screenshots: "There is more space in the bottom - fix it. lets use the shared bottom sheet - seems like this is not reusing the ones from profile module").**
    - The footer is a `safeAreaInset`, so the scroll view already stops above it. The extra `Disconnect.scrollBottom` of 180pt left a ~175pt gap under the consent row. It's now 16.
    - "Disconnecting your account…" was a hand-built overlay inside `ProfileV3DisconnectEmailView`, with its own scrim, 44pt top corners and no grabber. It is now `ProfileV3DisconnectProgressSheet` (own file), presented with `ProfileV3SheetWindow.present` like settings, logout and past trips: the shared corner radius, backdrop, `ProfileV3SheetGrabber`, white background and `ctaBarBottom` + bottom inset. The backdrop doesn't dismiss it; only Stop or the result does.
    - `ProfileV3ConnectorsRouting` gained `presentDisconnectProgress()` / `dismissDisconnectProgress()`. `ProfileV3ConnectorsSettingsModel` calls them on confirm, stop, success and failure. `ProfileV3SettingsSheetPresenter` guards with `isDisconnectProgressPresented`, because `ATBottomSheetWindowPresenter.dismiss()` pops whatever window is on top and an unmatched call would close the settings sheet. On success the progress sheet pops before the confirm screen and the path change.
    - The confirm view no longer takes `onStop`. Removed `Progress.panelRadius`, `Disconnect.footerBottom`, `Motion.progressScrim` and `progressScrimOpacity`. `Progress.linkTop` is the old 67pt panel top minus the grabber, so the artwork sits where it did.

82. **Glove box intro card entrance (user, 2026-10-21; Figma `5202:19844`: "we have animation for this frame too, but it's not playing").**
    - Item 79 animated only the inbox; the intro card was static. Figma loops a 2.4 s timeline. The app plays its entrance once (1.4 s), like the inbox, and leaves out the loop's fade-out (1.4→2.15 s).
    - The card frame (surface, shadows and envelope + glow) fades 0→1 by 0.65 s and settles from a (3.8, 21.97) offset and 0.98 scale by 0.75 s, all ease-out. The title, subtitle and button aren't children of the card in Figma, so each gets its own fade by 0.65 s and a 16pt rise by 0.75 s, ease-out, rather than inheriting the card's transform. The subtitle's 0.5 rest opacity stays in its colour.
    - The shimmer is a card-sized white gradient (`GloveBox.shimmerGradient`), clipped to the card. It starts at −470/380 of the width and travels 1050/380 between 0.3 s and 1.35 s, ease-in-out. Its opacity goes to 0.65 at 0.55 s, holds until 1.1 s and is gone by 1.4 s. The offsets are fractions of the card width, so it scales with the card. Figma's "White impact flash" is at opacity 0 the whole time, so it isn't drawn.
    - Code: `ProfileV3GloveBoxIntroCard` uses the inbox pattern: `TimelineView(.animation(paused:))`, a `.task` that pauses after `Motion.Intro.delay + duration`, `animatesEntrance:`, and Reduce Motion shows the final frame. The tracks are `ProfileV3MotionTrack`s in `GloveBox.Motion.Intro`. `ProfileV3GloveBoxView` puts `.id(intro)` on the card, so Complete KYC → Activate now plays it again; `ProfileV3GloveBoxIntro` is now `Hashable` for that.
    - Source of truth: the Figma SVG export of the frame (user: "this svg has the animation - handle it properly"). Its CSS keyframes match the values above. Card `Frame_1991431241`: `translate(3.8, 21.97)` + `scale(0.98)` about the card centre → 0 / 1 at 31.25% of 2.4 s, ease-out; opacity → 1 at 27.08%. Shimmer: translate −470 → +1050 from 12.5% to 56.25%; opacity 0 → 0.65 → 0 at 12.5 / 22.92 / 45.83 / 58.33%. Text and button: translateY 16 → 0 at 31.25%, opacity → 1 (subtitle 0.5) at 27.08%. 58.33 → 89.58% fades everything out for the preview loop. CSS `ease-out` and `ease-in-out` are written as explicit beziers (0, 0, 0.58, 1) and (0.42, 0, 0.58, 1).
    - Layering, matching the SVG: the copy isn't inside the card group, so it doesn't share the card's drift and scale. `stack(showing:)` builds the same VStack twice in a ZStack. The `.card` pass shows the surface, shimmer, glow and envelope and takes the card's fade, scale (about the card centre) and offset. The `.copy` pass shows only the title, subtitle and button, each with its own fade and rise. The hidden half of each pass keeps its layout, and the `.card` pass has hit testing and accessibility turned off.
    - `Motion.Intro.delay` (0.4 s) holds the first frame, with the card invisible, before the timeline runs. The debug flow opens the glove box in a `fullScreenCover`, and without the hold most of the 0.75 s ease-out rise played while the cover was still sliding up, so only the shine was visible. A stage switch (Complete KYC → Activate now) crossfades for 0.35 s, so the hold covers that too.
    - Revised twice the same day. The user first asked "The card should also come from a few px below and settle alone with the shine", and the card's offset and scale were stretched to 1.4 s with the copy riding on the card. The user then sent the SVG, so both went back to the SVG timing and layering above; the start hold is what makes the rise visible.
    - Verified: `theIntroCardRisesInAndTheShimmerSweepsOnce` (hold, start values, settled at 0.8 s, shimmer window) plus the suite, 112/112; Release build. `ImageRenderer` frames at 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.9, 1.2 s and at rest show the card's diagonal drift, the copy's straight rise, the shine crossing, and the layers lining up at rest. Not watched live on a device.
83. **The hub keeps the selected traveller when it comes back on screen (user, 2026-10-21: "When switching between the profiles and going to settings or going back to chat view or any other view - the profile is being reset to the start. fix this.").**
    - Cause: `ProfileV3CentredCarousel` saved the selection from its first layout in `restoreTarget` and scrolled to it in `.task(id: restoreTarget)`. SwiftUI cancels a `.task` when its view leaves the screen and starts it again when the view returns, so every return scrolled the carousel back to the pass selected when the hub first appeared: the main traveller. That programmatic scroll doesn't write the `scrollPosition` binding, so the view model kept the co-traveller, and the main card sat centred over the co-traveller's content.
    - What takes the hub off screen: switching tabs in Ayl (the hub's back button sets `model.tab = .home`, and the TabView removes the Profile tab's views), and pushes on the hub's navigation controller (KYC onboarding, including the connector onboarding that settings starts). Settings itself is `.overFullScreen`, which keeps the hub mounted. Opening and closing it didn't reset the carousel in the probe below; leaving the Profile tab or starting onboarding from settings did. Settings → Loyalty programmes still moves the hub to the main traveller on purpose (`tapMainProgrammes`, from the app's original code): settings belongs to the main traveller, and the programmes page it opens is theirs.
    - Fix: `restoreTarget` is gone. The carousel's positioning task is keyed on `width > 0`; it scrolls to the live `selection`, then reveals. It runs at first layout as before, and again whenever the carousel comes back on screen, where it changes nothing unless the selection moved while it was away. The duration wheel, the airport pickup cars and the add programme picker share the carousel and get the same behaviour.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3CarouselSelectionTests` hosts the real carousel in a window and reads which card is centred from the cards' frames, because the bug moved the scroll view without touching the binding. A tab switch and a push/pop keep card "c" centred, and a selection made while on another tab is centred on return. All three fail on the old carousel.
    - Verified: a scratch app built with `swiftc` (the carousel copied in and embedded like Ayl: SwiftUI `TabView` → representable `UINavigationController` → hosting controller → vertical `ScrollView`) logged the scroll offset after each flow on a throwaway simulator. Old carousel: the tab switch and the push reset to the first card. Settings `.overFullScreen` (also with a full-screen modal over it, and with the tab bar hidden and shown under it), sheets and full-screen modals didn't. Fixed: every flow kept the card. Then the suite, 115/115 (the new suite 5 iterations in a row), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
84. **The hub reloads the board after a Gmail disconnect, and a pass that turns red plays the red glow (user, 2026-10-21: "Are we refreshing the board after the gmail disconnect flow? Expected the red glow but no refresh. We are getting the green flow once we connect the gmail though - this is working."; then, with the Xcode network log: "Only these were made during the disconnection flow - fix it").**
    - Before: a disconnect made two calls, `POST …/board/submit` (`{"action":"disconnect_connector","connector":"gmail"}`, answered in the request with `ok`, `revoked` and `watch_stopped`) and `GET …/board/settings/connectors` (Gmail `connected: false`). The submit invalidates `ProfileV3BoardStore`, but the hub keeps its own board and nothing asked it to reload, so the card stayed green. Connecting works because the capture flow ends in `refreshAfterCapture`.
    - A reload alone wouldn't have played the red either. `passIDsNeedingKYCGlow` only queued passes that became complete or are new, and `ProfileV3KYCGlow` replays only when its trigger (`kycGlowPlays`) moves, not when the tone changes.
    - Now: `ProfileV3ConnectorsSettingsModel.finishDisconnect` reports a success through the new `ProfileV3ConnectorsRouting.connectorDidDisconnect(_:)`. `ProfileV3SettingsSheetPresenter` collects the keys and, when settings closes straight back to the hub, hands them to the new `onConnectorsDisconnected` (the add-traveller entry point passes nil). When settings closes into a follow-up (reconnect capture, onboarding, KYC, restart) it doesn't: those flows end in their own refresh, and a red cue behind them would fire the warning haptic mid-flow.
    - `ProfileV3HubViewModel.refreshAfterDisconnecting(_:)` shares `syncKYC` with `refreshAfterCapture` (the task and generation are now `kycSyncTask` / `kycSyncGeneration`): the KYC tag shows "updating", every loaded board is applied, and it polls on the refresh backoff until the board reflects the change. For Gmail that means the main home's `isGmailConnected` is no longer true; other connectors settle on the first load. The disconnect sync loads at once (`ProfileV3Backoff.poll(startsImmediately:)`) because the server finishes the disconnect inside the request. The capture sync keeps its initial 1 s delay.
    - Glow rule: a pass also cues when its resolved tone turns `.negative` (from positive or hidden). As before, the selected pass plays at once and any other pass plays when it is selected; the red then stays on the card (item 47). The red needs the board's help: after the disconnect the main pass has to come back with `worldpass_card.glow` "red" (or an expired KYC state). A `not_done` state with no glow resolves to hidden, and the card just loses its green.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3GmailDisconnectTests` (4): the connectors model reports a successful disconnect and not a failed one; the hub reloads after a Gmail disconnect and plays the red cue on the main pass; and when the first board still shows Gmail connected, the sync keeps polling and cues only once the board changes.
    - Verified: the suite, 119/119, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (6.5 s warm; the two connectors tests wait out the real 5 s disconnect grace, and a cold first run on a new simulator took 22 s across every suite). With the "turned negative" rule taken out, both hub tests fail on the glow assertions only, so the reload and the rule are each needed. Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. The presenter hand-off is untested: building `ProfileV3SettingsSheetPresenter` in a test needs account, coupons, app and session host fakes that don't exist. Not run against the backend, in Ayl or on a device.
85. **Settings shows the monogram letter straight away (Slack, Vijay Kumar, 2026-10-21: "Latency to show user first later of name is very high. Sometime its taking 5 to 10 sec."; user: "Yes, let's fix it").**
    - Cause: `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel` took the letter from the app's first name, else the board store's in-memory board, else a `loadLatestBoard()` fetch on appear. Ayl's first name is the first word of `ATAuthKit.currentUser.displayName`, which is nil when sign-in set no display name. Every write clears the store's snapshot (`store.invalidate()`): connector changes, KYC capture steps, profile field edits, permissions, programme add and remove, agent forms, traveller add, edit and delete, passport and co-traveller uploads, persona actions. Until the next board fetch lands, the letter waited for the whole board, 5–10 s for them. The settings response carries no name.
    - Fix: the hub keeps its own board when the store is cleared. `ProfileV3HubViewModel.mainProfileName` (new, `board?.mainProfile?.name`) goes from `ProfileV3HubHostingController.makeSettingsPresenter` through the presenter's new `profileName` to `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel(profileName:)`. Order: the app's first name, the hub's main traveller name, the store's cached board, then the fetch on appear. The add-traveller entry point passes nil; it never shows the settings page.
    - Tests: `theHubsMainTravellerFillsTheLetterWhenNothingIsCached` (no first name and an empty cache still give "p" from " Priya Sharma "; the app's first name still wins) and `theHubHandsSettingsItsMainTravellersName`.
86. **The relationship editor opens on the traveller's current relationship (user, 2026-10-21, device screenshot: "3. Previous selected relationship not highlighted on edit traveler flow").**
    - Cause: the chips highlight `relationshipSelection`, which was seeded from `ProfileV3EditField.selectedValue`: an option the server marks `selected`, else `current` only when it equals an option value exactly. The edit-traveller row's own `relationship` (item 37) was never consulted, so on device nothing lit up. The form's Relationship row had the same gap: it showed an option title only after the editor had loaded once that session, else the "Select" placeholder. Item 37's humanised fallback was never built.
    - Fix: `ProfileV3EditField.option(matching:)` compares values and then titles by `matchKey` (lower-cased letters and digits only), so `father_in_law`, `Father-in-law` and `FATHER_IN_LAW` agree. `selectedValue` uses it for `current`, and `selectedValue(fallingBackTo:)` falls back to the row's `relationship`; a server-marked option still wins. `ProfileV3TravellersSettingsModel` seeds the selection that way and keeps it as `initialRelationship`. Confirm enables only when the selection differs from it; comparing with `selectedValue`, as before, would have enabled Confirm on open whenever the value came from the fallback. The form row shows the cached option title (keyed by `matchKey`), else the row value with underscores as spaces and a capital first letter.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3TravellerRelationshipTests`): `currentMatchesAnOptionWhateverItsCaseOrSeparators`, `theTravellersRelationshipIsSelectedWhenTheEditorMarksNone`, and `theEditorOpensOnTheTravellersRelationshipAndSavesOnlyAChange`, which stores "Sibling" in the mock session: the form shows "Sibling" before the editor loads, the editor opens on "sibling", and Confirm is off until the selection changes and off again after going back to it. With the old matching restored, that test fails on all three.
    - Not confirmed against the live payload: which of `selected`, `current` or the row value the server actually fills for the relationship editor. The "Confirm edit" bar over the last chip row in the screenshot is the shared clear-to-white CTA fade at the list's top scroll position; the list scrolls above it.
    - Verified for items 85 and 86: the suite, 124/124, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl, against the backend or on a device.
87. **A passport front scan without `back_required` asks for the front only (user, 2026-10-21: "passport - back_required - nil - then only ask for front page.").**
    - `ProfileV3PassportScanResult.needsBack` is now `backRequired == true`. A missing or null `back_required` ends the settings re-scan after the front page, the same as `false` (`ProfileV3PassportScanVerifier` calls `onBackNotNeeded`, and the launcher finishes the scan as in item 78). This reverses item 78's default, where a missing field still asked for the back. Only the settings passport re-scan reads `back_required`; the KYC capture flow's upload result carries no back signal and is unchanged.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3PassportScanTests`): `onlyAnExplicitTrueAsksForTheBack` replaces `onlyAnExplicitFalseSkipsTheBack`, and `aFrontThatNeedsNoBackEndsTheScan` now covers nil as well as `false`.
    - Verified: the suite, 124/124, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run against the backend or on a device.
88. **Content fades under fixed headers on every screen that cut it sharply (user, 2026-10-21, device screenshot of a traveller's edit form with the name labels sliced under "EDIT TRAVELERS": "Previously we made a change for the header - to prevent the sharp cut at the bottom for the traveler list. Now this screen still has it, also analyse whether anyother screens are there to address.").**
    - Shared: item 45's private list fade is now `ProfileV3HeaderFade` in `Support/ProfileV3ScrollOffset.swift`, applied with `profileV3HeaderFade(_:gap:color:)` on the scroll view and `profileV3HeaderFadeAnchor(_:)` on its content. `ProfileV3HeaderPin.offsetBinding` replaces the per-screen bindings. As before, the fade comes in over the first 24pt of scroll (`headerRevealDistance`) and is invisible at rest, so no resting layout moves. `gap` (default `Sizes.headerFadeGap`, 12) is a solid band above the 61pt gradient. It stands in for item 45's 12pt chrome gap on screens whose chrome ends flush with the title, without moving their content; padding the shared `ProfileV3SheetChrome` instead would have grown every measured sheet. The list keeps its real gap and passes `gap: 0` (`Traveller.listHeaderGap` now reads `headerFadeGap`). `color` defaults to white; the passport screens pass `Palette.background`, the top colour of `passportScreenGradient`.
    - Applied (header stacked above the scroll view, so content clipped flush against it): `ProfileV3FocusScroll`, which covers the edit traveller form, contact info and add traveller; the relationship picker; permissions (its scrolling branch); passport reason and previous passports; the agent option picker's list; terms; section sources; ride regions; passport how-used. Programmes overlays an opaque white header block (`topBarBackdropHeight`) on a full-height scroll view, so its fade sits at the block's foot with no gap, driven by the same pin.
    - Audited and left alone: the header scrolls away with the content (the add programme sheet, app access, information access, household apply, the benefits and coupons lists); a fade already exists (settings page, past trips, airport pickup, agent form, travel persona, persona trait, hub); controls float over the content with no header block, so content passes behind the buttons but there is no cut line (programme sources, programme insight, offer detail, app credentials, the vault sheets, disconnect email); the glove box inbox clips at its own panel's rounded top (debug-only).
    - Tests: `ProfileV3HeaderFadeTests` (2): the pin's progress across the reveal distance through `offsetBinding`, and a rendered fade that is solid through the gap, clear at its foot and invisible at rest.
    - `ProfileV3HeaderPin` is now `@MainActor`: `offsetBinding`'s closures capture it, and as a plain class that drew two Sendable-capture warnings. Every user is a view.
    - Verified: the suite, 126/126, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. A throwaway `ImageRenderer` probe drew the edit form's chrome over name fields pushed up under the clip line: without the fade they cut flush under "EDIT TRAVELERS", with it they fade out below a 12pt white band. Scrolling itself was not exercised (ImageRenderer can't scroll), and nothing was run on a device or in Ayl.
89. **The WorldPass back face no longer draws empty programme slots (Slack thread on a device screenshot of a two-programme pass with the grey block scribbled over, grukhs: "We can remove it"; user, 2026-10-21: "handle this with a client config now").**
    - The back face stacks programmes in three fixed slots (`Programme.cardWidths` with `BackFace.stackTops`, or the single-programme `singleStackWidths`/`singleStackTops`). Slots without a programme were drawn as `Palette.emptySlot` (#F5F5F5) rounded rectangles, the back one at 60% opacity (`backSlotHeight` tall in the single stack). Two programmes left one grey block above the cards; one programme left two.
    - New client flag `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isEmptyProgrammeSlotEnabled = false`. While it's off, `ProfileV3WorldPassBackFace.programmeCards` starts its `ForEach` at the first real card, so only the placeholders go; the cards keep their slot widths and tops. Setting the flag to `true` brings the old look back. No programmes still shows the empty-state art (`emptyContent`); three or more never had empty slots.
    - Verified: the suite, 126/126, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. A throwaway `ImageRenderer` probe drew the back face with one, two and three programmes: no grey slots, cards in their usual places. Not run on a device or in Ayl.
90. **The citizenship lock and the citizenship row's flag come from `GET …/board/settings` (user, 2026-10-21: "let's use the citizenship_country_code from board api and lock it", then the backend contract: "GET /board/settings now has a top-level citizenship_country_code (main traveller, ISO-2, e.g. "IN"). It's null when the main traveller has no passport on file. Key is always present. Re-fetch /settings after a citizenship edit.").**
    - Before: the lock was the host's `account.snapshot().passportNationality` (item 44's "unchanged" lock). Ayl hard-codes it to nil, so the lock never engaged there; the Atlys app filled it from `UserData`'s saved passport. The row's flag came from a separate `GET …/settings/edit/citizenship` preload (`preloadCitizenshipIcon`, item 44).
    - Now `ProfileV3SettingsMenuDTO` decodes `citizenship_country_code` through `ProfileV3LossyDTO<String>` (null, missing or mistyped never fail the menu) into `ProfileV3SettingsMenu.citizenshipCode`, upper-cased. When the menu loads, `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel` calls `fields.applyCitizenship(code:)`, which sets both `passportCitizenship` (the lock and the overlay's country) and `citizenshipIconCode` (the row's flag). Non-null means locked, as the backend notes it is null without a passport. A code missing from `ProfileV3Countries` still locks, as a bare `ProfileV3Country(code:name:)` (generic cover, flag by code). `preloadCitizenshipIcon`, its task, and `ProfileV3SettingsDependencies.passportCitizenship` are gone. `ProfileV3AccountSnapshot.passportNationality` stays public for the passport mappers (`accountCountry(for:)`).
    - Re-fetch after an edit: a citizenship save already waits for the write, closes settings and restarts the app (`restartAfterSaving`; Ayl's `restartApp()` presents `AYLRestartCover`), so the next settings open loads `/settings` afresh. No extra call.
    - Mock: `ProfileV3MockRepository(settingsCitizenship:)` (default nil) serves the code; the "Settings · citizenship locked" preview uses `"IN"`.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3CitizenshipLockTests` (4): decoding (code, null, missing, mistyped), a loaded menu with "IN" locks the row (the tap plays the overlay and pushes nothing), no code leaves the editor reachable, and an unlisted code still locks.
    - Verified: the suite, 130/130, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run against the backend, in Ayl or on a device.
91. **The ITR step has a real screen in Ayl (user, 2026-10-21: "let's wire up the ITR connect flow"). No package change.**
    - The package side was already complete: Settings → Information access → ITR → Connect runs `startConnectorCapture("itr")` into `ProfileV3KYCCaptureViewModel`'s `.itr` phase, whose actions post PAN + password (`itrCredentials`), the PDF (`uploadITR`), skip, finish and back (`leave`). Ayl's `AYLProfileV3KYCHost.itrConnectView` returned `AYLProfileV3PlaceholderScreen("Connect ITR")`. The Atlys app draws it with its in-tree `KYCITRConnectView`, which AI-KYC carries but kept internal.
    - AI-KYC (local `main`, fast-forwarded to 1.0.4 first; unreleased, uncommitted): new public `KYCITRConnectScreen` wraps `KYCITRConnectView` the way `KYCGmailConnectScreen` wraps the Gmail view. It has a `Content` (state, copy, looking-for rows, skip, `Findings`), progress, busy, and upload / connect / skip / done / back callbacks, and reuses the Gmail screen's `State` and `LookingFor`. `KYCITRConnectView` gained an optional `onBack`, shown on the options and the failed card. It had no other caller. README and CHANGELOG ("Unreleased") are updated.
    - Ayl (uncommitted): `itrConnectView` maps `ProfileV3KYC.ITRConnectScreen` into `KYCITRConnectScreen.Content` and forwards the five actions. The README's profile-host paragraph is updated. `AYLProfileV3PlaceholderScreen` now has no callers.
    - Verified: AI-KYC builds and its 4 tests pass on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator. Ayl builds (`BUILD SUCCEEDED`, no new warnings) through a scratch workspace that overrides AI-KYC with the local checkout, with AI-Profile local through the project's own reference. Not run in the simulator, against the backend or on a device. Ayl needs an AI-KYC release (1.0.5) and a pin bump before this ships.
92. **A client flag turns every profile globe off, for a GPU test (user, 2026-10-21: "Let's use a client config and not show the globe at all - testing gpu performance hit.").**
    - `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isGlobeEnabled = false` is checked at the three call sites, so each globe's whole subtree is never built: its `TimelineView`, its `Canvas` and its land-`prepare` task. The three are the WorldPass card front (`ProfileV3CardGlobe` with its `ProfileV3GlobeOrbits`, on every card in the hub carousel), the hub's card-to-detail hero (`ProfileV3HeroGlobe`), and the expanded detail (`ProfileV3InteractiveGlobe`, orbits included). `ProfileV3HubStage.expand` also skips `ProfileV3InteractiveGlobe.prepare(using:)`. Every other `prepare` call lives inside those views.
    - Layout is unchanged. The card keeps its fixed frame and draws black under the wash, the KYC glow and the chrome. The detail keeps its flexible spacer, which still reports the sphere frame that nothing draws to. The hero's state and timings run as before. Setting the flag to `true` restores the globes.
    - Verified: the suite, 130/130, a Release build (0 errors, 0 warnings), and Ayl builds through the scratch workspace. Not run on a device.
    - Measured the same day (user: "How much CPU does the globe take to render and maintain"). A throwaway AI-KYC XCTest timed `KYCGlobeLand` through `ImageRenderer` at @3x on an M4 Pro (iOS 26.5 simulator, Release), 120 frames each. Card density: 10,427 points (3,093 coast), about 7,940 drawn per frame, built once in 25 ms. Card frame: 2.17 ms, of which path building is 0.46 ms and the land tint about 1.4 ms (0.77 ms without the tint). Detail globe at 345pt: 10,447 points, 1.52 ms a frame. A lighter card density (16,000 samples, keep 0.6, coast every 2°; 3,538 points): 0.46 ms. Drawing a pre-rendered 456×456 px snapshot: about 0.1 ms, 812 KB per frame. In Debug, path building is about 4× slower (2 ms). Everything ticks at most 60 Hz: Ayl doesn't set `CADisableMinimumFrameDurationOnPhone`. Per-frame CPU rasterising also re-uploads the Canvas bitmap: about 0.8 MB a frame for the card and 4.3 MB for the detail.
93. **The globes draw about 2,000 land dots instead of about 10,000, and spin at 24 fps (user, 2026-10-21: "the globe has around 10k, let's try to reduce it to 2k and cap the fps to 24", after stopping a Metal land renderer midway).**
    - `ProfileV3Theme.Globe.landDensity` (4,000 samples, keep 0.5, a coast dot every 2°, nothing south of -60°) is now both appearances' density. It replaces the card's 40,000 / 0.8 / 1° (10,427 points) and the detail's 60,000 / 0.5 / 0.9° (10,447). With Ayl's KYC land it builds 2,003 points: 1,621 coast, 382 interior. Throwaway AI-KYC renders of both globes chose it: options that spent the 2k on interior with a coast every 3–5° (1,976–2,150 points) broke the continents' outlines up, most visibly on the detail globe. Sharing one density means one build, and the hero flight crossfades the same dots.
    - `Globe.frameInterval` (1/24 s) is the `minimumInterval` of the card's and the detail's `TimelineView`. A drag still redraws on every touch, because each move changes state; the cruise and a fling's coast tick at 24 fps, so a fast fling steps visibly. The hero flight is not capped: its animated `progress` redraws the Canvas every frame anyway.
    - Measured with item 92's harness (Release, @3x, M4 Pro, iOS 26.5 simulator, 120 frames): card 2.30 ms of process CPU a frame before, 0.74 ms now (main thread 0.93 → 0.37 ms); detail 1.33 → 0.41 ms (0.92 → 0.21 ms). Counting the frame cap, a spinning card goes from about 138 ms of CPU a second at 60 fps to about 18 ms, the detail from about 80 to 10.
    - The globes are on again: the user set `isGlobeEnabled = true` (item 92) while doing this. The stopped Metal work is not in the tree: AI-Profile's part is in the user's `stash@{0}`, and Ayl's `AYLProfileV3GlobeRenderer.land(_:)` was taken out (it needs the stashed `ProfileV3GlobeLand`).
    - Verified: the suite, 130/130, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl or on a device; the look at 2,003 dots was judged only from the throwaway renders.
94. **Less GPU work outside the globes: the hub redraws at most 24 times a second at rest, freezes under its own covers, and stops re-rendering a shadow and a blur every frame (user, 2026-10-22: "apart from globe - what other optimizations can be done to reduce the GPU utilization? Do all those with the major effect!").**
    - One ambient clock. `ProfileV3AmbientSchedule` (`.profileV3Ambient(paused:)`) is a `TimelineSchedule` whose ticks sit on a fixed grid of `Motion.ambientFrameInterval` (1/24 s) since the reference date, so every timeline using it updates in the same frame. Before, each card's hover ran its own `.animation` timeline at the display rate (60 Hz) and the globe ticked on a separate 24 fps cadence, so the carousel region was recomposited about 60 times a second. The card hover, both globes (replacing item 93's `Globe.frameInterval`), the past-trips pulse rings and the hold-to-explore nudge now share the clock. The nudge was a `repeatForever` animation (`Motion.hintNudge`); it is now computed from the clock with the same ease-in-out swing over `Motion.hintNudgeSeconds` (1.2 s). A throwaway hosted-window probe counted 24.5 updates a second per timeline, and three timelines shared all 49 of their dates.
    - The hub freezes under its own full-screen covers. `ProfileV3HubView` sets `profileV3PausesAnimations` on its scroll content while `hidesHub`: the pass detail once settled (`stage.isDetailSettled`, which turns false at the start of the collapse), the benefit or coupon detail, the all-benefits and all-coupons lists, and past trips. The pass detail and the lists draw a full-screen `.ultraThinMaterial`, which had to re-blur every frame while the cards hovered behind it. The overlays themselves keep animating; only the hub underneath stops. Settings and the other pages already paused it through `ProfileV3ScreenCover`.
    - A paused hover now freezes where it is instead of dropping to rest. Each pause starts and ends while the hub is covered, so the jump to the current phase on resume is hidden. Before, the cards snapped down when a pause began, which would have shown at the start of these overlays.
    - The past-trips pill's shadow is on its capsule only. It was on the whole button, pulse rings included, so the shadow was re-rendered every frame while the rings grew. The pill sits on the pass's black card, where the rings' faint shadow was never visible.
    - The podium is flattened once (`.drawingGroup()`, padded by `Podium.renderSpill` (48pt) so the blurred shadow ellipse isn't clipped). Its blur and multiply blend are no longer redone each time the hovering cards above them change. A throwaway `ImageRenderer` comparison on white at @3x: the real podium is pixel-identical, and with a stand-in disc image for the remote art the largest difference is 2/255. Multiply over the opaque white page gives the same result inside the group as outside it.
    - Audited and left alone: the hint pill's `.ultraThinMaterial` (small, new users only), loading spinners and orb spins (transient), `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard` (only its preview uses it), the glove box screens (debug-only), and the static shadows and blurs elsewhere, which render once.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3AmbientScheduleTests` (3): timelines started apart share their ticks, a paused schedule holds its frame, and the clock is 24 fps.
    - Verified: the suite, 133/133, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. GPU load itself was not measured (the simulator can't show it), and nothing was run in Ayl or on a device.
95. **Glove box redesign: a glass cabinet intro, then an email, phone number and virtual card home with list and detail sheets (user, 2026-10-22; 22 Figma frames in `M8nsox6XKRV5p1Ysc8byuL`, among them `5202:18962` locked, `5202:19209` warming up, `5202:18474` home, `5241:11294` sample home, `5241:10167`/`5241:9137` card closeups, `5241:7680`/`5241:8804`/`5241:10547` lists and `5202:19412`/`5241:10892`/`5241:11118`/`5298:13207` details: "this is the updated flow for the glove box, update it.").** Still debug-only, from Settings (until item 115 added the Settings card).

The home has three section cards under the cabinet: Email (address + copy, about, forwarding line, the latest three emails, View all), Phone number (flag, number + copy, about, the latest call with its audio pill, View all) and Virtual card (card art, two stat tiles, three facts, How you pay with two radio options, Link card chip or Add card + secure note, the latest three transactions or the empty state, View all). View all opens a list sheet; a row opens a detail sheet (title, summary, status or audio pill or amount, logo, CHANGED badge or Sample tag, the agent's note with the orb, Tell me more). Back pops one sheet; the dim closes all. Tapping the card shows "Cards details are protected".

Section badges are `ProfileV3GloveBoxBadge` (`.live` green gradient, `.pending` grey). Rows: `ProfileV3GloveBoxEmail` (attention line + meta), `ProfileV3GloveBoxCall` (`.audio(duration:speed:)` or `.missing(message)`), `ProfileV3GloveBoxTransaction` (`.approved`/`.blocked`). All copy is model data; no new strings.
    - View model: `sheets: [Sheet]` (`.list(Section)`, `.detail(Entry)`), `copy(_:)` for the address, number and trip number (only copyable values; check icon for 1.5 s and a success haptic), `selectPayOption`, `linkCard`, `showCardNotice`/`hideCardNotice`, `sampleLabel(for:)`, and `onIntroAction`/`onFollowUp`/`onAddCard`. `show(_:)` clears the sheets and the notice.
    - Files: `Components/ProfileV3GloveBoxCabinet.swift` (the cabinet), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxView.swift` (root, header, intro + `ProfileV3GloveBoxIntroContent`, home + `ProfileV3GloveBoxHomeContent`, section card, email and phone sections), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxCardSection.swift` (card section, `ProfileV3GloveBoxVirtualCard`), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxRows.swift` (rows, meta, attention, audio pill, waveform, badge, sample tag, View all, copy value, preview card), `Views/ProfileV3GloveBoxSheets.swift` (list and detail sheets). Removed `ProfileV3GloveBoxInboxView.swift`, `ProfileV3GloveBoxEmailDetail.swift`, the intro card and its motion, and the envelope and folder art. `ProfileV3GloveBoxBlobs` + `ProfileV3GloveBoxActionButton` stay for Complete KYC.
    - Cabinet: drawn in the 440 × 562.31 Figma frame and scaled to the width (`Layout.cabinetScale(for:)`, capped at 1). Three layers so only the glow redraws: a `Canvas` for the box (sheen, side strips, bevel, two plus-lighter blurred shines), the glow, a `Canvas` for the rings and the three dials (gradient discs, 12 dots, ATUIKit mail/call/credit-card icons), plus the label, pill and border. The stand (shelf, seam, highlights, legs, shadows) is one `Canvas` under the white fade. Intro and home differ as in Figma: the intro moves the label 5.91pt and the dials 3.43pt down and adds the glow and pill; the home fade is 104pt reaching white at 60%. The shelf gradient was fitted to sampled Figma pixels: the CSS 166° export spans corner to corner over 130pt, but Figma's render is the same direction over about 238pt, which keeps the left end grey. Figma's leg backdrop blur was left out; sampled leg colours matched without it.

The warming title fades in by 0.65 s and rises 16pt by 0.75 s, ease-out, once; Figma's loop then fades it out for 4 s, which the app leaves out. The home sheet rises 464pt and fades in on the settle curve (0.2–0.82 s) the first time it appears. List rows fade up 16pt, 0.08 s apart (Figma's list keyframes collapse to one instant, so the stagger is the app's).

Figma's noise texture on the card and glows is not reproduced. The card's plus button is ATUIKit's `add` in a black circle. Section icons are mail, call and credit card; Figma uses mail for all three.

The debug flow goes Complete KYC → warming up → the sample home; Add card links a sample "HDFC ***5818" card (its logo is the Figma PNG as a data URL, like the app access fixtures); Tell me more closes the sheets. Fixture copy is Figma's, typos included ("Marriot", "Actives on trip"). The audio pill is visual only; there is nothing to play.

96. **New error state for a failed profile load and a failed settings load (user, 2026-10-22; Figma `5432:14062` hub and `5433:14525` settings: "These are the new error states - handle this. one for the main profile api failure and one for the settings.").**

Let’s give it another shot" (B2 SemiBold 12, #787878, 207pt wide) and a text Retry button (ATUIKit `refresh` 18pt + B1 SemiBold 14 in `backgroundDarkTheme`, with a 12pt hit inset that doesn't move the layout). Spacing is Figma's (`ProfileV3Theme.Sizes.ErrorState`).

    - Settings: a failed menu shows the block in a 421pt card (`backgroundLightTheme` #F9FAFB, 1pt #E6E7E8 border, 40pt radius, 44pt below the avatar, content dropped 11pt as in Figma), then Logout and Delete account 47pt below. Those two rows used to vanish with the menu; they are client rows now (`ProfileV3SettingsViewModel.failedMenuRows`, `logout_confirm` and `delete_account` directives, the existing Logout and Delete account strings), routed through `tap(_:)` like the server rows, so logging out and deleting the account still work. The header title falls back to "Settings" until the menu arrives. `ProfileV3SettingsRowList` now takes rows instead of the menu. Loading still shows the spinner.

The Delete row keeps the existing "Delete account" string (Figma writes "Delete Account").

A throwaway probe hosted the real `ProfileV3SettingsView` and `ProfileV3HubView` on `ProfileV3FailingRepository` in a 440×956 window and snapshotted them with `layer.render` (`drawHierarchy` is blank in a window without a scene, and `ImageRenderer` can't draw the settings `ScrollView`). Settings matched the frame: card 295–716, Logout at 783, Delete at 852. The hub block is centred in the safe area, about 9pt below Figma's. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
97. **The glove box list and detail sheets use the shared ATUIKit bottom sheet (user, 2026-10-22: "for the sheets used - can we use the atbottomsheet?").** Item 95 drew them as an in-view overlay (own dim, `.move(edge: .bottom)`, own 44pt background).
    - `ProfileV3GloveBoxSheetPresenter` (Presentation) presents one `ProfileV3SheetWindow` (`ATBottomSheetWindowPresenter`, edge to edge, no ATUIKit grab handle) when `viewModel.sheets` becomes non-empty and dismisses it when the stack empties. `ProfileV3GloveBoxView` drives it from `.onChange(of: viewModel.sheets.isEmpty)` and dismisses it `.onDisappear`, so closing the debug cover can't leave a window behind. A backdrop tap closes the window, and its `onDismiss` calls the new `sheetsDidClose()` (clears the stack without animating).
    - One window for the whole stack: `ProfileV3GloveBoxSheetStack` shows `sheets.last`, crossfading between list and detail while the ATUIKit wrapper animates the height. It keeps showing the last sheet while the window animates out, so the sheet doesn't collapse to the wrapper's minimum on the way down. Back pops one level; Tell me more and a backdrop tap close all.
    - The sheets lost their own background and the list's `ViewThatFits`/`ScrollView` (the wrapper scrolls past its 0.92 max height, as for settings). They take `bottomInset` like the other shared sheets: the list adds it under the rows, the detail under the CTA (`profileV3CTABar(bottom: ctaBarBottom + bottomInset)`) or the empty bottom. The window keeps the design's look through `ProfileV3SheetWindow`'s parameters: 44pt corners (`GloveBox.Layout.sheetRadius`) and the Figma dim (`GloveBox.Palette.dimOpacity` 0.8, replacing the `dim` colour); the page under it still blurs 4pt. Removed `Layout.sheetTopGap` and the private sheet background.
    - Tests: `closingTheSheetWindowClearsTheStack` (12 glove box tests).
    - Verified: the suite, 141/141, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build. A throwaway `ImageRenderer` render of the stack (a call detail) and the transactions list, clipped as the wrapper does, checked the bottom inset under the CTA and the rows. The window itself can't be presented in the test bundle (`ATBottomSheetWindowPresenter` needs a connected window scene), so the presentation, the height animation between list and detail and the backdrop dismissal were not seen running; not run in Ayl or on a device.
98. **A lit rim around the WorldPass card globe (user, 2026-10-22; Figma `5482:14797`: "Let's add this circle around the globe to make the globe more prominent").**
    - Figma's globe is one raster (`5482:14805`), so the "circle" was read off pixels of the 2× frame. The faint arcs about 1.6 globe radii out at the equator are already `ProfileV3GlobeOrbits` (1.55×, the same ±10° band), and a scan of rings from 1.1 to 1.7 radii found nothing else. What the app lacked was the sphere's own edge: a blue-grey rim just inside the limb (#8EACC3 at its brightest, about 1pt, with a glow fading over about 5–10pt inward, black right outside), bright at the right, medium at the left and upper left, dim at the top, gone along the bottom. The host renderer only brightens land dots towards the limb (`edgeGlow`), so the ocean edge never showed.
    - `ProfileV3GlobeRim` (Components): a 2pt `strokeBorder` and a 10pt stroke blurred 5pt at 0.5, both with `Globe.rimGradient` (an `AngularGradient` of `Palette.globeRim` by angle), clipped to the circle. It is static, so the 24 fps globe redraw doesn't touch it.
    - The card overlays it on `ProfileV3CardGlobe` at the sphere's size (`Sizes.worldPassGlobeSphere` = `worldPassGlobe` × `cardSphereRatio` × 2, 139.8pt). At first the hero flight faded it with `1 - progress` and the open (interactive) globe had none; see the revision below.
    - Tuned against the frame: brightness sampled every 30° just inside the limb of a throwaway `ImageRenderer` render of the card and of the Figma 2× image. The rim is within about 20 of 255 at most angles; at 30°, 90° and 210° it is 20–40 brighter, where Figma's raster dips over land and cloud and a static rim keeps a smooth profile.
    - Verified: the suite, 141/141, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, and a Release build. The package renders its placeholder land, so the rim was not seen over Ayl's real land dots; not run in Ayl or on a device.
    - Revised the same day (user: "let's increase the rim width to 5 from 2. Also add this for the expanded globe too"). `Globe.rimWidth` is 5; the glow is unchanged, so the profile tuning above was done at 2pt and the 5pt band is brighter overall. `ProfileV3InteractiveGlobe` overlays the rim at its own sphere (`sphere(in: size)`, like its orbits), over the ocean fill, vignette and shade it draws. With a rim at both ends, the hero flight now draws it at full opacity the whole way instead of fading it with `progress`. Verified: renders of the card (`ImageRenderer`) and of the interactive globe hosted in a window (`layer.render`, since its sphere size arrives through `onGeometryChange`), then the suite, 141/141, and a Release build. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
    - Revised again the same day (user: "it shouldn't come into the globe - it should be outside."). The rim now sits outside the sphere: the 5pt band runs from the limb outwards (`strokeBorder` with `padding(-rimWidth)`), and the glow is a 10pt band outside it (`padding(-rimGlowWidth)`, blurred), so the glow halos outwards. A mask (a rectangle `rimMaskOutset` (30pt) past the frame with the sphere's disc cut out by `.destinationOut`) keeps the blur from spilling onto the globe. The view's frame is still the sphere, so the card, the hero flight and the interactive globe needed no change. This drops the "black right outside the limb" reading from the Figma pixels above: the user's call. Verified with the same two renders (the dots and the ocean disc end where the band starts), then the suite, 141/141, and a Release build. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
99. **Ambient timelines no longer use `.profileV3Ambient` (user, 2026-10-23: "`TimelineSchedule` has no member `profileV3Ambient`" after a stash onto master).** `TimelineSchedule` now lives in SwiftUICore, and leading-dot lookup on `TimelineView` does not see a same-module `extension TimelineSchedule where Self == ProfileV3AmbientSchedule`. The six call sites (card globe, interactive globe, hover, hold-to-explore hint, glove box glow, add-past-trips pill) pass `ProfileV3AmbientSchedule(isPaused:)` instead. The static helper is removed. Committed with Done item 101 as `89bcda1`.
100. **Glove Box email and shared meal-card height, committed as `94614d6` (2026-10-22 22:26, "Wire Glove Box email and show every meal", 25 files).** `origin/master` is at this commit. It also contains Done item 98.
    - Jaqen lives under `/gateway/jaqen/v1`: `GET/POST /me`, `PUT /forwarding`, `PUT /forwarding-address`, `GET /messages`, `GET /messages/{id}`, and `GET /messages/{id}/attachments/{index}`. `ProfileV3GloveBoxClient` loads the address, inbox page, forwarding state, and attachments. The account carries `address`, `previousAddresses`, `forwardingEmail`, `forwardingPaused`, `pendingEmail`, and `eligible`.

101. **First-run tips on the travel persona radar, knob dragging reworked, and persona editing turned on.** Found uncommitted on 2026-10-23 after a stash was applied; it was written outside this session and is recorded here from the code.
    - Coach (`Presentation/ProfileV3PersonaCoach.swift`): it picks the highest-scoring trait whose axis points up (`cos` of its angle at least `Coach.targetMinimumRise`, 0.3), else the highest overall. Its steps are drag (when editing is on and the trait has `submit`), then tap (when the trait has `detail`). `startCoach()` runs 2.3 s after the content appears (`Motion.personaCoachDelayMilliseconds`). It shows once per install (`ProfileV3HubHints.hasSeenPersonaCoach`, key `profileV3.personaCoachSeen`), and not while a knob is selected, a drag is running or a trait is open. Releasing a drag on the tipped knob moves to the tap tip. Touching another knob, or opening a trait, ends the tips.
    - Bubble (`Components/ProfileV3PersonaCoachBubble.swift`): 131pt wide, the app-access hint gradient and shadow, a pointer notch that follows the knob, and step dots. It sits 23.3pt below the knob, 12pt clear of the card edges, scales in from the tip, and a tap advances it. Two new keys need adding in Ditto: `mo-aiprofile-agents-hint-persona-drag` ("Drag the circle to edit values") and `mo-aiprofile-agents-hint-persona-tap` ("Tap to see how score is calculated").
    - Radar: a tap selects a knob, and tapping the selected knob opens its trait, replacing the double-tap. A drag moves the knob along its axis from where it started (by translation, not finger position) and stays local in the radar. The score is drafted only on release (`beginDrag` / `endDrag`), and scrolling and swipe-to-dismiss are off during the drag. `canEdit(_:)` is per trait: editing on, no save running, and the trait has `submit`. The selected knob grows from 15 to 21pt on a spring and shows `ProfileV3PersonaRipple`, two hairline rings growing from 21 to 57pt on a 2 s loop (opacity 0 → 0.1 → 0.05 → 0). The ripple runs on its own `.animation` timeline, not the 24 fps ambient clock (Done item 94). A selected score no longer brightens.
    - `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isPersonaEditingEnabled` is now `true`. This reverses Done item 59, which turned editing off after a save came back 501 "That isn't available yet." With it on, drags draft and the save bar shows.
    - Tests: six new in `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (14 in all): the tips play once on the highest upper trait; dragging the tipped knob moves to the tap tip and a tap opens it; another knob ends the tips; a knob touched before the tips keeps its selection; with editing off the tips only teach the tap and knobs don't drag; and the ripple's rings and loop.
    - Verified with Done item 99: the suite, 149/149, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator, 0 errors. A Release build was not run. Not run on a device, in Ayl or against the backend.
102. **Glove box inbox: delivery status, the attention line, the footer and sender logos from `GET /gateway/jaqen/v1/messages` (user, 2026-10-23, pasting the backend's note). Written in a separate worktree off `94614d6` while `master` was in use, then moved onto `master` uncommitted ("don't commit it yet").**
    - Contract: `status` is `forwarded` ("Sent to your inbox"), `handled` ("Handled for you"), `added_to_thread` ("You’re in the thread"), `not_forwarded` (shown without a tick; the reason is explained once at the account level), or null while it is processed (about 2 s). Only `forwarded` and `not_forwarded` happen today. `attention` is null or `{type, title, detail}` (title on the row, detail on tap); `action` is the footer text ("change caught", "Added to trip"); both come from Signals and are null for now. `sender_logo_url` is the BIMI logo or the site icon served through our API; null means initials from the sender's name. Before this, the mapper read an older status set (`processing`/`paused`/`stored`/`failed`), turned `failed` into an attention line and a badge, and showed a blank circle without a logo.
    - Figma has no frames for these states (a text search of "🟢 Profile V 1.2" and "Glove box" found only "Needs attention", "change caught", "Added to trip" and the forwarding line), so they reuse the redesign's row: attention line, then the meta line (glyphs, label, date).
      - Meta line: a tick (ATUIKit `check`, new `ProfileV3GloveBoxGlyph.tick`) for every status but `not_forwarded`, the mail glyph for an old address, then the label: `action`, else "Sent to an old address", else the status line. A row with no label shows the bare date; the "·" separator used to dangle.
      - Attention: `ProfileV3GloveBoxEmail.Attention(title, detail)`; an attention with no title reads "Needs attention", and an empty object is dropped. The detail sheet shows `detail` under the attention line (B2 Medium 12 at 70%, 187pt wide).
      - Logo: `ProfileV3GloveBoxLogo(url:initials:)` draws initials (T3 Bold 9 on grey-100) until the image loads and keeps them if it fails; once loaded the grey fill sits behind it as before. `ProfileV3GloveBoxEmail.initials`: the first letters of the first two words, or for a one-word sender the first label of its domain (`booking@marriott.com` → "M", `goindigo.in` → "G").
      - Banner (`ProfileV3GloveBoxEmailSection.forwardingNotice`): when any loaded email is `not_forwarded`, the email section shows an info box under the forwarding line: forwarding paused, a forwarding email waiting for confirmation, or none set. It disappears once forwarding is on; those rows stay without a tick.
    - Robustness: `attention` and `action` decode through `ProfileV3LossyDTO`, so a different shape drops the field instead of failing the inbox. Only absolute http(s) logo URLs are used: the package's base URL is a placeholder the host rewrites, so a relative path, or a logo behind our auth header, would need loading through the API client like attachments; until then such rows show initials.
    - Detail merge: `loadGloveBoxMessage` now takes the list row (`_ listed:`) instead of an id. Before, the live repository rebuilt a stand-in row from the detail response, so the logo (and anything only the list carries) was lost when an email opened. `detail(_:listItem:)` keeps the row's logo, attention, status and meta and only fills what the row lacked. The view model's `append` (paging) now rebuilds the section through the mapper, so the banner follows newly loaded pages.
    - Copy lives in `ProfileV3GloveBoxCopy` like the rest of the glove box (not the string catalogue). The fixture's "Needs attention" is an `Attention` now; the design rows are otherwise unchanged.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3GloveBoxTests` (17): the inbox test reads `forwarded` (tick, mail glyph, old address) and decodes the detail from JSON, plus four new ones: every status with attention, footer, logo and initials (including a relative logo path and malformed `attention`/`action`); an untitled attention and the initials rules; the banner for paused, pending, none and active forwarding; and a detail keeping the row's header or filling it when the row had none.
    - Verified on `94614d6` plus this change: the suite, 147/147, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. A throwaway hosted-window probe rendered the email section (paused forwarding with the banner; forwarded, handled with attention and footer, not forwarded and added-to-thread rows with initials) and an opened email with the attention detail. Remote logos were not loaded, and nothing was run against the live endpoint, in Ayl or on a device. `89bcda1`/`b516e2d` touch none of its code files; only this file overlaps. Then on `b516e2d` (`origin/master`) plus this change, the tree `master` holds now: the suite, 153/153, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings.
103. **Client flags hide the glove box Phone number and Virtual card sections (user, 2026-10-23: "let's have two client config for hidding the phone number forwarding and the virtual card component. let's hide it now.").**

With them off, `ProfileV3GloveBoxHomeContent` leaves out `ProfileV3GloveBoxPhoneSectionView` and `ProfileV3GloveBoxCardSectionView`, so the home sheet holds only the Email section. The calls and transactions lists, their details and the card notice open only from those sections, so they are unreachable too. The debug glove box flow follows the same flags.
