104. **The travel persona sheet loads behind the processing dial and reveals only at the end of a loop (user, 2026-10-23, two screenshots of `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard` in the sheet: "For the travel persona alone - let's use this as a loading screen - once the full animation completes then if the API loaded then show the response or continue the animation").**
    - View model: `isDecoding` turns on when `load()` starts with no content (the first load, or a retry after a failure; reloads after a save or a trait change keep the content and skip the loader). `finishDecodingLoop()` turns it off only once the request has settled (`phase != .loading`), so a response that lands mid-loop waits for the loop to end, and a slow one keeps the dial turning loop after loop. A failure also waits for the loop end, then shows the usual load-state panel with Retry.
    - View: while decoding, `ProfileV3TravelPersonaLoader` replaces the content: the persona orb (`ProfileV3PlaceholderArt.agentPersonal`, the art `orb_personal` maps to) at the content's `orbTop`, then `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard` 50pt below, in a scroll view. Its `.task` calls `finishDecodingLoop()` every `Motion.personaProcessingLoop` (4 s) from when it appears, which is also when the dial's own clock starts, so the reveal falls on a loop boundary. The swap is a crossfade (`Motion.contentSwitch`), and the radar then plays its own entrance. Under Reduce Motion the dial is still, so the loader reveals as soon as the request settles.
    - The persona response isn't cached, so the loader shows for at least one 4 s loop on every open.
    - Copy: the dial needs a title and ten trait names before the API answers, so they are client strings in the dial's clockwise slot order: `mo-aiprofile-agents-title-traveller-profile` ("Traveller profile", the screenshot's spelling) and `mo-aiprofile-agents-label-persona-adventure`, `-new-places`, `-outdoors`, `-social`, `-nightlife`, `-pace`, `-local`, `-culture-history`, `-comfort`, `-planning` (`ProfileV3Strings.personaLoaderTitle` / `personaLoaderTraits`). Add them in Ditto. The radar itself still shows the server's names and title.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (16): the loader holds a response until a loop ends and skips reloads with content; a failed load waits for the loop and a retry decodes again.
105. **Email detail: the note wraps only beside the orb, and attachments are chips (user, 2026-10-23, device screenshot of a long email body squeezed into a narrow column: "the text is wrapping up. This is happening due to the image on the right side. but let's try to over come once the image has ended. let's make sure that the text is wrapping only when there is image."; then Figma `5298:13207`: "Got an update for rendering the attachments inside a mail").**
    - Note: SwiftUI `Text` can't flow around a shape, so `ProfileV3FlowingText` (Components) wraps a non-editable, non-selectable, non-scrolling `UITextView` with a text-container exclusion rect at the top-trailing corner. The detail sheet reserves the insight orb (68pt) plus 16pt, and keeps lines beside it to the design's 260pt column (`besideWidth`, so a 440pt screen matches Figma's 52pt gap before the orb). Lines below the orb's 68pt use the full width. The orb is an overlay; the view's height is at least the orb's. Typography mirrors ATUIKit's `.H7InterMedium16` (font, tracking, line spacing from `TypographyStyle`). Calls and transactions use the same note block.
    - The email's own hard line breaks remain (plain-text parts are often wrapped at about 72–76 characters, e.g. "…hotel booking for the" / "application."); nothing reflows them.
    - Attachments (`5654:13458`/`5654:13477`): `ProfileV3FlowLayout` chips, 16pt apart and 41pt under the note: `Palette.preview` fill, 1pt `Palette.sectionBorder`, 20pt corners, 6/12/6/16 padding, a 20pt ATUIKit `image` icon for `image/*` (`ProfileV3GloveBoxAttachment.isImage`) or `attachFile` otherwise, and the filename in B2 SemiBold 12, all in `neutralGrey700` (#4D4D4D, the SVGs' fill). Long names truncate in the middle. While a file opens, its icon becomes a mini spinner and every chip is disabled. Before, attachments were a list of blue filename links.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3GloveBoxTests` (18): a long note is shorter than the same text in a fixed column, taller than it at full width, and a short note keeps the orb's height; `isImage` by content type.
    - Verified for items 104 and 105: the suite, 156/156 (with a throwaway hosted-window probe, removed afterwards), on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. The probe rendered the device screenshot's email (three lines in the column, then full width), the design's short note (the same five line breaks as `5298:13207`) with both chips, and the persona loader 2.6 s into a 60 s mock load (orb, "Traveller profile", the ten labels with the colour wave, the dial and the shimmer). Not run in Ayl or on a device.
106. **Saving an agent form keeps the sheet open (user, 2026-10-23: "everytime u save changes in agent the screen should not shut and go back to profile").**
    - Before: `ProfileV3AgentFormViewModel.submit` showed "saved" for `savedDismissMilliseconds` (1.2 s) and then called `finish()`, which runs the flow's `dismissAgentFlow()`. Closing the household "apply to" sheet after a save also called `finish()`.
    - Now a save moves the baseline the save bar compares against (`savedValues` is a `var`, set to the saved snapshot), shows "saved" for the same 1.2 s, then returns to `.idle`, so the bar hides and the form stays. A household save closes only its sheet after that pause; closing it by hand during the pause does the same. `onSaved` still fires straight after the save, so the hub refreshes behind the sheet. Only Back (`tapBack`) closes the agent now.
    - After a save the form re-fetches itself (`loadAgentFlow` with `form.agentID`) so server-derived parts such as the "Found N/M … preferences" header are current. The fresh form is applied only if nothing was edited since the save, so it never overwrites the user. `cancelPendingWork` cancels it. The mock repository stores saved forms, so mock builds behave the same way.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests` (19): a save keeps the form open, returns to idle with no save bar and only "saved" reported (Back still closes); a household apply closes only its sheet, whether it times out or is closed by hand.
    - Verified: the suite, 158/158, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
107. **The loyalty picker shows each card's name under it (user, 2026-10-23; Figma `5606:12507`: "we will be having an extra key called "name" in the json for each entry of cards").**
    - Data: each entry in the pick screen's `programs` already decodes a required `name` (`ProfileV3ProgrammeRowDTO`; an entry without one is dropped) into `ProfileV3ProgrammeCatalogueItem.name`, so the backend's `name` needs no new decoding, assuming it is that top-level key. Before, the name only appeared drawn on cards with no art or placeholder art.
    - `ProfileV3ProgrammePickerCard` is now the card plus its name: 20pt below (`Picker.nameTop`), 142pt wide, centred, at most two lines. The focused card's name is B1 SemiBold 14 in black; side cards get B2 SemiBold 12 at 40%, lifted by `sideNameLift` (151 × (1 − 170/190) ≈ 16pt) so it follows the shrunken card's edge, as in the frame (655 vs 641). Size, colour and lift animate with the existing selection animation. The on-card name for art-less or placeholder cards is kept, so those cards show the name twice.
    - `Picker.carouselHeight` (302 + 20 + 38 = 360) is the carousel's height and the loading, error and empty states' height, so switching states doesn't jump.
    - Verified with item 108: the suite, 158/158 (with a throwaway hosted-window render of a focused and a side card, removed afterwards), on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), and a Release build, 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
108. **Information access cards are white, and the connected email chip is grey (user, 2026-10-23; Figma `2633:31985`: "remove the grey bg in the container and give the gray for the gmail chip.").**
    - In the frame (`1880`) all six connector cards are white with the 1pt #E6E7E8 border, 30pt corners and a 5% black shadow (0, 4, blur 30, spread −2). `profileV3SettingsCard` gained a `fill` parameter (default `Palette.background`, the old grey), and this page uses `profileV3AccessCard()`: white fill plus the shadow (radius 15, y 4; SwiftUI has no spread). The email card and the other connector cards share it; the rest of Settings keeps the grey cards.
    - `ProfileV3ConnectedEmailChip` now sits on a `Palette.background` (#F9FAFB) capsule with 2/2/2/8 padding, as in the frame; the Gmail logo, B1 SemiBold 14 and #808080 text are unchanged.

Its only reader is `ProfileV3SettingsView.swift:89`, so the Settings page now shows the Secure credential store card under Connectors in Release too. 110. **Ditto synced with the local strings, and hard-coded copy moved into it (user, 2026-10-24: "clean up the strings in the ditto - sync the local ones we have now to ditto and also some strings are directly used, migrate those too into ditto", with the toolkit at `/Users/aravindh/Downloads/ditto-migrate-toolkit-main`).** See Architecture decisions › Client strings come from Ditto for the setup.
    - Before: Ditto's `ai-profile` project and the `profilev3strings` folder each held 333 strings, identical to each other. Against the local catalog (309 keys, all used), 68 were stale, 44 were missing, and 12 still said "traveller".
      - The stale ones were keys deleted when copy moved to the server (items 75, 78, 80 and others). None of them appeared in release code, and 45 have a matching `be-aiprofile-*` string in `ai-profile-board`.
      - The missing ones were the keys earlier items marked "add in Ditto".
    - Ditto changes: the user chose to delete the 68 from both the project and the library. The 44 missing keys were created in both. The 12 texts were patched to "traveler" in both.
    - Hard-coded copy moved into Ditto as 57 new keys, in a new `glovebox` library subfolder and in `agents`:
      - `ProfileV3GloveBoxCopy`: every string, the "Email" section title, "Active on trip", the card's "Expires", "CVC" and "Name", and the eight error messages in `ProfileV3GloveBoxMapper.message(for:)`.
      - `ProfileV3VaultStrings`: every string except the `••••••••` mask.
      - The household sheet's "Confirm" fallback in `ProfileV3AgentMapper`.
      - Three glove box strings reuse existing keys with the same text and meaning: Activate (`general-button-activate`), Try again (`general-button-try-again`), Needs attention (`account-status-needs-attention`).
      - The phone headline is stored as three keys without its joining spaces.
      - The two copy enums keep their names and members; each member is now a computed property over `ProfileV3L10n`, so call sites and tests are unchanged. The mapper's placeholder `phone` and `card` sections are computed too, so runtime overrides reach them.
    - Left as literals: DEBUG fixtures, previews and the debug KYC menu; logs and `fatalError`s; wire keys, analytics names and server-message matching; date formats; the brand names in `ProfileV3ProgrammeCatalogue`; `ProfileV3Country.india`, which code compares against; the `"--"` stat placeholder; the placeholder number `+1 847-757-XXXX`.
    - Rebuilt the catalog and the generated Swift with the toolkit's `ditto-pipeline.sh --skip-scan`: 366 keys, every accessor used, none removed or renamed.
      - The 44 formerly local-only keys now carry the Ditto notes as their comments.
      - Two values lost their curly apostrophe in the export: the persona loader caption and the error-state subtitle.
      - The generated file has SwiftGen's `///` notes and `swiftlint` lines again, as item 27 expected.
    - Verified:
      - A fresh export of the project matched the expected 366 keys exactly, and the library matches the project.
      - The toolkit's detector flags only the known `({{centimetres}}cm)` false positive.
      - `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted afterwards): 158/158, 0 errors, 0 warnings. A Release build passed too.
      - Not run in Ayl or on a device.
    - Follow-up the same day (user: "some of the keys and values still have double l in traveler - fix it and update ditto too"):
      - Ten keys renamed from `traveller` to `traveler` in place with `newDeveloperId`, in both the project and the library, so their Ditto history stays. Examples: `mo-aiprofile-account-settings-button-add-traveler`, `-agents-title-traveler-profile`.
      - The persona loader title is now "Traveler profile". Item 104 had kept the screenshot's "Traveller".
      - The 11 notes that said "traveller" now say "traveler".
      - The generated accessors were renamed with the keys, and `ProfileV3Strings` and `ProfileV3SettingsStrings` call the new names. The Swift wrappers keep their names (`editTraveller`, `travellerAdded` and the rest).
      - The catalog and generated file contain no "travell".
      - Not changed: the library components' display names, such as "Account Error Traveller Add Failed". `PATCH /v2/components` returns success for `name` but ignores it, so those change only in Ditto's web app. No export uses them.
      - Verified: 158/158 on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build.
111. **Agent form section sources show "N sources" (user, 2026-10-26, airport logistics screenshot and Figma `2548:22231`: "The sources flow has text too. add it.").**
    - Figma row `4778:5948`: an HStack with 4pt spacing. Two 16pt round logos overlap by 2.67pt. The caption is 10.67pt after them, B2 SemiBold 12 in grey-400 (#999). Then a 20pt `chevron_forward` in the same grey. The row used to be one 20pt badge icon and a 24pt black chevron, with no text.
    - Data: the field `source` was built against `{icon, next}` (vdcios `5b0c8cf48`), with no count. Asked where the number comes from, the user chose "server sends a count". `ProfileV3FieldSourceDTO` now also reads `count`, `label` and `icons`, the same shape as the form's top-level `sources`.
      - Each entry in `icons` can be an object with `image_url` or a bare URL string. Entries that aren't http(s) URLs are dropped, and an empty list falls back to the single `icon`.
      - `ProfileV3SectionSource` holds `icons`, `count` (nil unless above 0) and `label`, replacing `iconURL`.
      - The backend hasn't confirmed these keys; until it sends a count, the row shows the logos and the chevron only.
    - `ProfileV3SectionSourcesRow(source:action:)` and `caption(for:)`: with a count and a label it reads "3 sources" (`sourcesCount(_:label:)`). With a count and no label it falls back to the existing "%1$@ Sources", capital S, as on the form header pill, because there was no Ditto token for a lowercase key. `ProfileV3SourceIconStack` gained a `.logo` style for the 16pt logos; the header pill keeps `.badge`.
    - The flight fixture's airlines source has two logos and a count of 3. The form header pill (`isAgentSourcesPillEnabled`, still `false`) is unchanged.
    - Test: `fieldSourcesCarryTheServerCountLabelAndLogos` (`ProfileV3AgentBoxTests`, 20).
112. **The past trips add/edit sheet has a close button (user, 2026-10-26: "Add close dismiss for the past trips country add flow + the existing tap outside to dimiss the sheet").** No Figma frame. `ProfileV3AddPastTripSheet` takes `onClose` and overlays a 40pt `ProfileV3CircleIconButton.close` top-trailing, 16pt in, centred on the title row (`TripSheet.closeTop`, from the 40pt title top and the 14pt tag line). The presenter passes `ProfileV3PastTripsViewModel.dismissSheet()`, which a backdrop tap already reached through `sheetDidDismiss`. The button is disabled while a save runs. Test: `closingTheAddSheetAddsNothing` (`ProfileV3PastTripsTests`, 11).

113. **Travel persona redesign: no card background, header in the loader, and an edit hint (user, 2026-10-26: "Updated delight loading screen", Figma `3309:68873` in frame `1913` `3309:68726`, then "updated spidy sense design", Figma `5574:2234`).**
    - Both frames share one layout. The subtitle is 244pt wide (`Persona.subtitleWidth`; it was the agent forms' 310pt). The card starts 34pt under it (`Persona.cardTop`, was 50) and has no fill: the `personaCardGradient` background is gone from the radar card and the processing card. The trait detail card keeps its gradient. The radar, its labels, the scores at 60%, the dial and its 4s motion are unchanged (`get_motion_context` matches item 72).
    - The orb, title and subtitle are a shared `ProfileV3TravelPersonaHeader`. The user chose "fill in when data lands":

      - When the response lands mid-loop, the real title, subtitle and sticky-header tag fade in (`Motion.contentSwitch`) while the dial keeps turning on the same clock.

It is B2 SemiBold 12 with the frame's 131.87° grey gradient (#C2C2C2 → #838383 → #C2C2C2, `personaHintGradient`) and wraps at 240pt. It sits at the frame's card y 446 (28pt under the radar, 9pt bottom padding). The first-run coach bubbles (item 101) stay.
    - New key `mo-aiprofile-agents-hint-persona-edit` (`ProfileV3Strings.personaEditHint`). It was added by hand to the catalog and the generated Swift in the toolkit's format, because this session had no Ditto token; the user chose that. It was added to Ditto later that day (item 118).
    - Verified with item 114: the suite, 161/161, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings. A throwaway hosted-window probe (440×956, `layer.render`, removed afterwards) took four snapshots on the mock with a 1.5s delay: the loader before the response, the response landing mid-loop, the dial still turning, and the radar after the swap. They matched both frames: the card title lands where the dial card's title was, so the swap changes only the card. Not run in Ayl or on a device.

114. **The loyalty card opens the programmes list when Gmail needs reconnecting (bug report pasted by the user, 2026-10-26).**
    - Cause: with `needs_reauth`, the board sends `worldpass_card.kyc_complete: false`. `ProfileV3HubViewModel.tapOpenProgrammes` called `gatesKYC(pass.programmesDirective, fallback: !pass.kycStatus.isComplete)`. The card's `{go:"programs_page"}` isn't one of the routes `gatesKYC` lists, so it fell back to the KYC state and opened the add-programme KYC redirect. Android gates only on a KYC `go` (`loyaltyCard?.next?.go.isKycGate()`).
    - Fix: the fallback there is `false`. Only a server KYC step gates the card: `kyc`/`kyc_gate`, KYC capture or co-traveller capture. A missing directive opens the list too, as on Android. `addProgrammeRequiresKYC` and the agent tap keep their fallbacks.
    - Test: `loyaltyCardOpensTheListUnlessTheServerSendsAKYCStep` (`ProfileV3AgentBoxTests`). With `kyc_complete: false`, `programs_page` opens the list and `kyc_gate` opens the KYC redirect. `AgentRouterSpy` now records both calls.
    - Verified with item 113 (161/161). Not run against a `needs_reauth` board, in Ayl or on a device.
115. **The glove box opens from a Settings card, and the debug entry is gone (user, 2026-10-26, Settings screenshot and Figma `2633:30879`, card `5574:5255`: "new entry point for glove box - remove the debug one").** The glove box now opens in release builds too. Items 79 and 95 had it reachable from the DEBUG section only.
    - Card: `ProfileV3SettingsGloveBoxCard` is the first card in the Settings panel, above Accounts center, at the usual `cardSpacing` (25; the frame uses 28 between all its cards).
      - Content: "Glove box" (H7 SemiBold 16), "Your virtual email" (B1 Medium 14 at 80%), and the two-line description (B2 Medium 12, grey-400). The line break is in the string, as in the frame.
      - Styling: white, `profileV3AccessCard()` (now internal, was private to Information access), so 30pt corners, the #E6E7E8 hairline and the 5% shadow.
      - Size: the texts get Figma's 20/19/32pt line boxes, so the card is 380×186 at Figma's width.
      - Dials: three, credit card, call and mail, drawn top-trailing and clipped by the card. They come from the cabinet's dial drawing, now `ProfileV3GloveBoxDial.draw(_:centre:diameter:in:)`, scaled from its 62.69pt dial; the Figma dials are the same art at 47.12, 62.69 and 55.28pt.
      - Pill: `ProfileV3GloveBoxPill` (moved out of the cabinet) with the bolt and a spectrum border (`GloveBox.pillSpectrum`: black → #5057EA → #EF4646 → #EDD758, drawn with `ProfileV3BoxGradient` from the frame's stroke transform). The cabinet keeps its black hairline.
    - State: the frame only shows ACTIVATE, so the pill shows only while the account has no address. `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel.loadGloveBoxStatusIfNeeded()` runs on appear and calls `loadGloveBoxCard()` (`GET /gateway/jaqen/v1/me`). While that is loading or after it fails there is no pill, and without the pill the card is 145pt. The card shows only when the settings repository is also a `ProfileV3GloveBoxRepository`.
    - Tap: logs `settingsRowClicked(rowKey: "glove_box")` and presents `ProfileV3GloveBoxLiveFlow` as a `fullScreenCover` on the settings root. Closing it re-fetches the status, so an address created inside hides the pill.
    - Removed from `ProfileV3SettingsDebugSection`: the "Debug · Glove box" sample cases, the "Live" button and both covers. The KYC-state section stays. `ProfileV3GloveBoxDebugFlow` stays for the glove box previews, and `ProfileV3GloveBoxFixtures.Start` lost its debug titles and conformances.
    - Mock: `ProfileV3MockRepository` now conforms to `ProfileV3GloveBoxRepository` through a DEBUG `ProfileV3GloveBoxStore` (no address by default; creating one uses the fixture address; messages are the fixture emails; attachments throw `notImplemented`). Previews and mock-based settings therefore show the card.
    - Copy: two hand-added keys, `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-label-virtual-email` and `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-description-settings-card`, added to Ditto later that day (item 118). ACTIVATE reuses `general-button-activate`.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3SettingsGloveBoxTests` (3): ACTIVATE shows until the account has an address and hides after the flow closes; an active account shows no pill; without a glove box repository there is no card.
    - Verified with item 116: the suite, 164/164, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. A throwaway hosted-window probe (`layer.render`, removed afterwards) rendered the card with and without the pill and the Settings page on the mock; they matched the frame, apart from the Upload pill under the avatar, which wasn't asked for. Not run against the jaqen backend, in Ayl or on a device.
116. **The globe rim is off behind a client flag (user, 2026-10-26: "remove the gradient added to the globe - a thin border. move it under a client config").** `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isGlobeRimEnabled = false`. `ProfileV3GlobeRim` (item 98) draws nothing while it's off, which covers the card, hero and expanded globes. Setting it to `true` brings the rim back unchanged.
117. **Travel persona autosaves when a drag ends (user, 2026-10-26, screenshot of the radar: "when user has finished dragging - we need to do auto save just for this agent!").** Only the persona radar changes; the agent forms keep their save bar.
    - Before: a drag only drafted the score (item 59, reworked in item 101), and a "save changes" bar (`ProfileV3PersonaSaveBar`) submitted the drafts. While a save ran, and for the 1.2 s "saved" state after it, every knob was locked.
    - Now `endDrag` drafts the score and saves straight away. The submit and reload path is unchanged: each changed trait's `submit` with its `score`, then a fresh `GET …/travel_persona`.
      - Saves run one at a time. A knob released while a save runs is drafted, and the save loop picks it up next (`savesAgain`). Knobs are never locked; `canEdit` is only the flag plus a served `submit`.
      - A draft is cleared only if it still holds the value that was sent, so dragging the same knob again mid-save is kept.
      - If the reload fails after a successful save, the saved scores are applied to the local copy.
      - VoiceOver ±5 steps draft at once and save 0.8 s after the last step (`Motion.personaAdjustSaveMilliseconds`), so a run of steps is one request.
    - Feedback: a `ProfileV3Toast` at the bottom, in the trait page's style. "Travel persona updated" (`personaUpdated`, the old saved title) shows for 1.2 s. A failure shows the server's reason as an error toast for 3 s, and the knob snaps back to the server's score. Before, it kept the draft for the button to retry, and there is no button now.
    - Removed: `ProfileV3PersonaSaveBar`, `savePhase`, `saveError`, `showsSaveBar`, `hasChanges`, `save()` and `Persona.saveBarClearance`. While editing is on, the content keeps a bottom clearance for the toast (`Persona.toastClearance`) so it never covers the edit hint. `isPersonaEditingEnabled` still turns all of it off.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (18). Five replace the three save-bar tests: a released drag saves at once (clamped, toast, drafts cleared); a refused save snaps back with the reason; a drag released during a save is saved next; VoiceOver steps save once after they stop; and dragging back to the saved score sends nothing. The config test now also tries a drag.
    - Verified: the suite, 166/166, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. Not run against the backend, in Ayl or on a device, so the toast and the knob snapping back weren't seen running.
118. **The strings that existed only locally are in Ditto (user, 2026-10-26: "let's sync the local strings to ditto - the one's which are not uploaded yet", using the toolkit at `/Users/aravindh/Downloads/ditto-migrate-toolkit-main`).** No repo change.
    - Before: a pipeline build into a scratch folder matched the repo, except that it dropped the three hand-added keys from items 113 and 115. Nothing else was missing in either direction, and no text had drifted.
    - Library first, as the toolkit says: components `agents-hint-persona-edit` (folder `agents`), `glovebox-label-virtual-email` and `glovebox-description-settings-card` (folder `glovebox`). Each is named as its id in title case and has the catalog comment as notes, Draft status and no tags, like its siblings. `ditto_check_component_issues.py profilev3strings` then flagged only the known `(180cm)` false positive.
    - Project items `mo-aiprofile-…` were built from those components with the toolkit's `build_item` and `post`, for the three keys only. `ditto_copy_folder_to_project.py` copies whole folders, and every other key in `agents` and `glovebox` was already in the project.
    - Verified: the project and the library each hold 369 strings, matching the catalog. A pipeline build from Ditto into scratch is byte-identical to the repo's catalog and generated Swift.
119. **Four AYL regression bugs from QA-1675 (user, 2026-10-26, pasted Linear issues with device screenshots).**
    - "NEW PASSPORT" header not centred (passport reason screen, "What happened to your old passport"). The title was centred: 194.5 of 390pt in the screenshot and in a probe. The defect was the two step bars drawn across the bottom of "PASSPORT". `ProfileV3BackTagHeader` placed the `ProfileV3StepIndicator` overlay with `.alignmentGuide(.bottom) { $0[.top] - stepTop }`, which SwiftUI didn't apply, so the bars sat on the title's bottom edge. They are now offset by `Passport.stepTop + stepBarHeight` (13pt), so they sit 10pt under the title as intended. The title stays centred on the back button. Only the reason screen passes `step`.
    - "How this is used" sheet not fitted, with two grabbers (same sheet, opened from the reason screen's pill). `ProfileV3AgentFormView` presented `ProfileV3PassportHowUsedView` with `.presentationDetents([.medium, .large])` and no drag-indicator setting. The sheet opened at half height, so the steps scrolled under the Scan bar, and the system grabber sat over the view's own `ProfileV3SheetGrabber`. The new `ProfileV3PassportHowUsedSheet` feeds the view's existing `onHeightChange` (grabber + content + Scan bar, 581.7pt on the mock) into a `.height` detent, `.medium` until it measures. It also hides the system indicator. That is what the UIKit `ProfileV3PassportHowUsedHostingController` already did for the pill on the passport scan screen, which had neither bug.
    - Past trips cards truncated long names ("Bonaire, Sint Eusta…"). `ProfileV3PastTripCard`'s name now wraps to two lines (`PastTrips.nameLines`), centred, keeping the 0.75 minimum scale as a last resort. A wrapped name grows the block upwards: the card measures the name against a hidden one-line `Text` and lifts the flag, name and month by the difference. The month stays where it was, clear of the faded year. The screenshot was the Android app; iOS truncated the same way.
    - Verified: the suite, 166/166, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. A throwaway hosted-window probe (removed afterwards) showed the header before and after, the Bonaire and China cards side by side with "September" level with "October", and the how-used view's measured height. The sheet presentation itself (the fitted detent and the single grabber) can't run in the test bundle, so it wasn't seen. Not run in Ayl or on a device. In the probe, "BQ" has no `ATCountry`, so the Bonaire card shows the grey flag placeholder; the Android screenshot has a flag.
120. **Five UAT changes (user, 2026-10-27, with screenshots of the vault site picker and the glove box virtual card).**
    - Persona delight loader, five times only. The processing-dial loader (item 104) now shows for the first five persona loads with no content on this install (`ProfileV3HubHints.personaLoaderShows`, limit `personaLoaderShowLimit` 5, key `profileV3.personaLoaderShows`). After that, the sheet shows the usual `ProfileV3LoadStateView` spinner. A show is counted when the loader starts, a retry after a failure included. The check is client-side only.
    - Opening a dragged knob waits for its save. A drag saves on release (item 117). Tapping the same knob straight away opens the trait page, which used to fetch `…/travel_persona/{trait}` alongside the save and could show the old rows. `open` now starts any unsaved drafts (`flushDrafts`, which also cuts short a pending VoiceOver debounce) and loads the trait only after the save loop, including its persona reload, has finished. The trait page shows its loading state meanwhile. If the trait page is closed first, nothing loads.
    - Vault site picker header. "Add credentials" was the first line of the scrolling content, under the close button (`pickerTitleTop` 104). `ProfileV3VaultSheetHeader` gained an optional `title`, drawn in H5 and centred on the close button's line. The picker puts that header above its scroll view, so it stays put. The list passes under it with the settings fade (`profileV3HeaderFade`, item 88), which is invisible at rest. The search field now starts 24pt under the header (`pickerSearchTop`, was 40 under the title). The store sheet's header (close only) is unchanged.
    - Glove box virtual card, no empty gaps. The card section padded empty `stats`, `facts` and `payOptions` anyway. That left 76pt between the card art and the first divider, and 56pt after "How you pay". Each now drops out with its spacing when empty, giving 32pt and 28pt.
    - Sample entries for empty backend responses. When the inbox is empty (`emails` empty and no next page), the email section shows two sample emails in `ProfileV3GloveBoxMapper.section`. The phone section shows one sample call and the card one sample transaction, since the backend sends neither yet. Each block carries the existing Sample tag (`sampleLabel`; the card section gained one, and the transaction card adds `sampleClearance` 12pt so the tag clears the amount). Sample details show the tag and no "Tell me more" (item 95). Sample emails have no View all, never fetch `GET /messages/{id}`, and give no forwarding notice. A pause, resume or page rebuild keeps them flagged (`realEmails`). Any real email replaces them.
      - Copy is Figma's sample home (`5241:11294`) and its detail frames, with "Marriot"/"Mariot"/"early checking" corrected, through `ProfileV3GloveBoxSamples`: the Emirates delay with "change caught" and Changed, the Marriott upgrade with Needs attention and "Added to trip", and the Marriott early check-in call with a 1:23 audio pill and Changed. The SOHO dinner transaction is "-$12.00", Approved.
      - The Figma detail frames reuse one seat-upgrade note on every entry. Only the Emirates email keeps it; the Marriott email, the call and the transaction have short notes written to match them.
      - The design's "25 Jun, 2026" dates are left off, so samples don't carry a stale date.
      - The logos are the media.atlys.com Emirates and Marriott loyalty logos.
      - 18 new keys were hand-added in the toolkit's format: `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-label-sample`, `-badge-changed` and 16 `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-sample-*`. No Ditto token here. They were added to Ditto on 2026-10-27 (item 125).
      - The phone and card flags (`isGloveBoxPhoneNumberEnabled`, `isGloveBoxVirtualCardEnabled`) are still `true` (see Upstream drift), so all three sample blocks reach release users.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` (20) adds the five-show cap and a trait fetch that waits for the drag's submit. The two loader tests now use fresh hints. `ProfileV3GloveBoxTests` (20) adds the samples mapping (an empty inbox gives samples, a real email replaces them, calls and transactions are samples) and a live view model on an empty-inbox stub: a sample email never reaches the repository, and a forwarding pause keeps the samples.
    - Verified: the suite, 170/170, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. A throwaway hosted-window probe (removed afterwards) rendered the site picker (title and close on one line), the glove box home on an empty inbox with all three sample blocks, and the virtual card section. Scrolling under the picker header wasn't exercised. Not run in Ayl, against the backend or on a device.
121. **An edited past trip is scrolled back into view (QA-1675 sub-issue pasted by the user, 2026-10-27: "After editing country card, user should return to same card position — currently scrolls to top").**
    - An edit sends year and month, then the page reloads (item 60), and the server returns the trips newest first. The edited card can move to a new place in that order. Its row also gets a new identity, because `ProfileV3PastTripCard` is keyed `.id(trip.revision)`, and the revision includes the date. Nothing brought the user to where the card went.
    - A hosted-window probe didn't reproduce a jump to the top: with the list at the bottom, the offset stayed there while the edited card moved up out of view. The reported jump may come from the app's presentation. Either way, the user lost the card.
    - Now, when the reload finds the trip the draft describes (`matches`, the same lookup that plays the reveal), `ProfileV3PastTripsViewModel` publishes `focus` (trip id plus a request count, so the same trip can be focused twice). `ProfileV3PastTripsView` wraps the list in a `ScrollViewReader`, gives each row an outer `.id(trip.id)`, and scrolls that card to the centre (`Motion.pastTripsFocus`, 0.4 s ease-in-out; none under Reduce Motion). Adding a trip focuses the new card the same way, so its reveal is on screen. Deleting focuses nothing.
    - The scroll runs on the next main-queue turn (`DispatchQueue.main.async`). `focus` changes in the same update as the reordered trips, and the reorder has to be laid out first.
    - Tests: `anEditedTripIsScrolledBackIntoViewWhereverItLands` (`ProfileV3PastTripsTests`, 12): an edit that moves the last trip to the top focuses that trip. `TripsRepositoryStub` now applies edits and takes its trips in `init`.
    - Verified: the suite, 171/171, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. A throwaway probe (removed afterwards) hosted `ProfileV3PastTripsView` on the mock with five trips. It scrolled to the bottom, edited the last trip to January 2026, and found the list scrolled to that card at the top. Animated scrolls don't advance in a test window: a bare `ScrollViewReader` moved only without animation. So the probe ran once with the animation set to `nil`, which was then restored. The animated scroll and the in-app presentation weren't seen. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
122. **Previous passports come from the server, past trips can be undated, and the passport screens match Figma (backend notes pasted by the user, 2026-10-27; Figma `1578:53347` frame "1856" with its passport section `1578:53778`, and `1578:53927` frame "1857", Previous Passports).**
    - Previous passports, contract: the personal agent's `passport` card sends `previous {label, title, next: {go: "passport_previous", profile_id?}}` only when an older passport is on file. `GET …/board/passport/previous?profile_id=` (omitted for the account main) returns `{title, labels: {number, issue_date, expiry_date}, passports: [{title?, name?, number?, issue_date?, expiry_date?, status}]}`: the current passport first (`active`), then older ones (`expired`).
    - Mapping: `ProfileV3AgentPassportDTO.previous` becomes `ProfileV3PassportField.previous` (`ProfileV3PassportPrevious`: label, title, next), kept only with a label and a `next` that routes to the new `.passportPrevious(profileID:)`. It replaces `hasHistory`, which nothing live set. `ProfileV3API.passportPrevious(profileID:)` and `loadPreviousPassports(profileID:)` (was `loadPassportHistory`, which threw `notImplemented` on Live) map to `ProfileV3PreviousPassports` (title, `ProfileV3PassportLabels`, passports).
      - Each row keeps its own name, number, dates, status and printed `title` for the country strip.
      - A row without a title takes the account's passport nationality for the main traveller, as the card does, except India: the server titles every Indian passport, so an untitled row isn't one.
      - Labels and titles on this screen come only from the server (item 75). The agent screen's card keeps the client labels (`ProfileV3PassportLabels.card`), since its payload has none.
    - Behaviour: VIEW PREVIOUS shows the served label and loads the directive's own `profile_id`. The screen's header is the response's `title`, or the card's `previous.title` while loading. After a passport replacement (the reason screen submits, or a scan completes with no reason step) the form re-fetches its agent screen through the existing `refresh()`, so VIEW PREVIOUS appears in place. A scan result without `previous` keeps the card's.
    - Design, from hosted renders at 440pt compared with both frames and pixel-sampled against a 1x Figma crop of the card:
      - The card lost its grey top shade (`passportHeaderShade`); Figma is a flat #F6F7F7 there. It gained the two stacked page edges under it (`upperPageEdge`/`lowerPageEdge`, 7pt, white with a 20% black-to-clear shade), so the component is 300pt tall.
      - Dates sit 44pt apart (were 20), with a 16pt `calendarToday` on the active card only; the frame hides it on expired cards.
      - The passport section's card starts 28pt under its header (`Passport.sectionTop`; other sections stay 24), VIEW PREVIOUS has 8pt before its chevron (was 4), and "Upload new passport" is the compact pill (32pt, 12pt semibold) in grey-800 (#333; Figma #323232).
      - The previous screen is white, the rule after the current passport is dashed (black 20%, 2/2), cards are 30pt apart (`listSpacing`, was 38 without page edges), and `listTop` is 40, so the title-to-card gap is Figma's 58pt. The whole screen sits 8pt higher than the frame because it keeps the passport screens' shared back-button header; the frame has no back button.
      - Not matched: the date underlines use the shared dashed line where the frame's date fields use #9D9D9D, and the page edges are up to about 5 levels darker than Figma's at their darkest pixel (SwiftUI interpolates the gradient premultiplied).
    - Past trips, contract (deployed 2026-10-27; reaches AYL builds once `AYL_ADD_TRIPS_MIN_IOS_VERSION` is set): an undated country comes as `{trip_id: "country:<ISO-2>", code, name, image_url, delete}`, after the dated trips, sorted by name. A country-only add posts `submit` and `country` with no `year` or `month`.
    - Mapping: `ProfileV3PastTrip.date: ProfileV3TripYearMonth?` replaces `year` and `month`. A row with both is dated, a row with neither is undated, anything else is dropped. Server order is kept.
      - Undated cards draw no year and no month line, and VoiceOver reads just the country. The year's line is kept blank so the bezel matches dated cards: a dated card's year overflows the 342pt frame and stretches its bezel to about 357pt.
      - Edit shows only when served, and undated cards don't get it, so the actions sheet shows Delete alone. That sheet now fits a single row (`actionsSingleHeight`, 116pt).
    - Country-only add: `ProfileV3AppHost.addsPastTripsWithoutDate` (public, default `false`) reaches the editor through the hub and past-trips view models.
      - When it's true, or when `add_trip` serves no `duration` block (which used to leave the duration step without a button), the sheet has one step. The country step's CTA (`country.cta_label`, "Done") submits `ProfileV3PastTripsMapper.addRequest`, which leaves out `year` and `month` for an undated draft. The reload reveals and focuses the new `country:<ISO>` card.
      - A country already on the list, dated or undated, gets the duplicate message and isn't sent, since the server wouldn't list it again.
      - The default is false because the Atlys app never gets undated trips; vdcios's host keeps it. **Ayl needs `var addsPastTripsWithoutDate: Bool { true }` on `AYLProfileV3AppHost`**, plus a package release with this API, before AYL iOS adds by country. Until then Ayl keeps the month and year step, which the server still accepts.
      - The mock store mirrors the server (undated ids `country:<ISO>`, hidden when a dated trip covers the country), and the fixtures include an undated Italy.
    - Strings: `ProfileV3Strings.viewPrevious` and `previousPassports` are removed, as the server sends that copy now. Their keys `mo-aiprofile-documents-button-view-previous` and `mo-aiprofile-documents-header-previous-passports` were left unused in the catalog and generated Swift. They were deleted from Ditto on 2026-10-27, and both files were rebuilt without them (item 125).
    - Tests: `ProfileV3PreviousPassportsTests` (7): the card's entry only when served, the screen's rows and empty fields, the untitled-row fallback, the endpoint's query, the tap loading the traveller's passports, nothing without an entry, and the re-fetch after a replacement. `ProfileV3PastTripsTests` (17) adds undated mapping, the add body without year and month, the one-step add with a Delete-only sheet, a duplicate country, and an add screen without a duration.
    - Verified: the suite, 183/183, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build, on a tree that also holds the GPU work below. A throwaway hosted-window probe (removed afterwards) rendered the passport section, Previous Passports, a dated and an undated trip card, and the single-row actions sheet. Not run against the backend, in Ayl or on a device.
123. **Deep links to add a traveller and add a loyalty programme (user, 2026-10-27: "let's add the present add traveler and add loyalty program deeplinks", for Ayl).** Before this, Ayl's only profile link was `ayl://profile`, which switched to the Profile tab; the package had no way to take a link into the hub.
    - Package API: `ProfileV3EntryPoint.open(_:)` (main-actor) takes the new public `ProfileV3Destination` (`.addTraveller`, `.addLoyaltyProgramme`) and leaves it in `ProfileV3PendingDestination.shared` (an `ObservableObject`; a newer post replaces an older one, and `take()` clears it). It never presents anything itself.
    - Hub: `ProfileV3HubView` observes the holder (`destinations`, injectable for tests). It sets `isSettled` once `stage.enter` returns uncancelled, which is after the entrance on first visit or after the load check on a return, and clears it in `onDisappear`. While settled with a loaded home, it takes the destination and calls `ProfileV3HubViewModel.open(_:)`. A link that lands while the hub is off screen (another tab) waits for it to come back.
    - `open(.addTraveller)` calls the router's `openAddTraveller()`. `open(.addLoyaltyProgramme)` selects the main traveller's pass, then calls `openAddProgramme(requiresKYC: addProgrammeRequiresKYC(for: main), profileID: main.id)`, so an incomplete KYC gets the hub's usual redirect. Neither logs a CTA event, and the hub's screen event still says `entrySource: chat` (`makeHubSurface` hard-codes it).
    - `ProfileV3HubHostingController.openAddTraveller()` now runs through `afterSettings`, so with Settings open it closes Settings first rather than replacing `settingsPresenter` with a second presenter. Both sheets present in their own `ProfileV3SheetWindow`, and the scan loader flag is off, so anything else over the hub doesn't block them.
    - Ayl (AI-Native, uncommitted): `ProfileRoute` gained `.addTraveller` and `.addLoyaltyProgramme`. `ProfileFeature.route(for:)` maps `ayl://profile/add-traveler` and `ayl://profile/add-loyalty-program` (also `?action=` on `profile`, the Atlys app's form `atlys.com/profile?action=add-traveler`; also `atlysapp://` and `https://link.atlys.com/ai/profile/...`). Anything else under `profile` is still `.root`. `AppCoordinator.navigate` calls `ProfileFeature.open(route)` after `profile.show(route)`, which brings the tab forward. The README's deep-link section lists both links. Ayl's `AylTests/DeepLinkTests.swift` isn't in any target (the project lists three test files), so the routes have no Ayl test.
    - Same session, Ayl only, no package change: a custom-token sign-in (the debug console, a Branch auto-auth link) goes through `AppSession.signIn(withCustomToken:)`, and a different uid reloads the app under `AYLRestartCover` (moved out of `AYLProfileV3AppHost.swift` into `Ayl/Features/Root/Splash/`). The profile board needed nothing, because `ProfileV3BoardStore` keys its snapshot and disk cache by `currentUserID`.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3DestinationTests` (4): the loyalty link opens for the main traveller after a co-traveller was selected, the traveller link opens its sheet, the holder hands a destination out once, and a hub hosted in a `UITabBarController` opens a pending link once it has settled, keeps a link posted while its tab is hidden, and opens it on return.
    - **That last test fails** (`theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`, found 2026-10-27 with item 126). It fails every time, both on `e94a37e` alone (a clean export, that suite only) and with the working tree. Lines 68–69: a link posted 300 ms after `tabs.selectedIndex = 1` is opened anyway, so the hub still counts as settled while its tab is hidden. Either `onDisappear` doesn't clear `isSettled` in a tab switch inside a test window, or the hidden-tab guard doesn't work. Not investigated yet. The other three tests in the suite pass.

124. **The pinned KYC notch follows the scroll instead of a timer (user, 2026-10-27, three frames of a screen recording: "it should transition smoothly - this is happening when scrolling fast").**
    - Symptom: after a fling back from the collapsed hub, the pinned "KYC INCOMPLETE" notch showed over the re-opening pass, 30pt and then 12pt below its pinned spot, while the content's own KYC tag was visible too.
    - Cause, from the frames and the code: `ProfileV3HubPinnedBlock` showed the notch from a boolean (`collapse >= 1 && isKYCTagUnderBackdrop`) with `.animation(Motion.selection, value: showsNotch)`. The card re-opens with the scroll, but the notch faded over 0.2 s. The value-based animation also animated the notch's position in the scroll content in the frame the flag flipped, so it trailed the pin by that frame's scroll jump. The card in the frames sits exactly where the pin puts it (with the hero origin at the 59pt safe area: tab 75.7, notch 99.7, card top 90 − 14.3 × collapse), so the pin itself doesn't lag.
    - Now `ProfileV3HubScroll.notchReveal` is the smaller of `ramp(collapse, collapseTabFadeStart, 1)` and the content tag's progress through the backdrop fade (`backdropFadeBottom` → `backdropSolidBottom`). The notch's opacity is that value, with no animation, and VoiceOver skips it until it's fully shown.
    - The tag's maxY goes to the scroll model (`kycTagBottom`, not observed), so HubView no longer re-renders when the tag crosses the backdrop; the `isKYCTagUnderBackdrop` state is gone. In steady state the tag crosses the fade between collapse 0.906 and 0.976 (tag bottom = 588 − 455 × collapse), so the notch fades in with the tag going under, and is gone before the card visibly opens on the way back.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3HubNotchTests` (4): a fling back hides the notch in the same update, the notch cross-fades with the tag, a hidden tag still waits for the card to fold, and no KYC tag means no notch.
    - Verified: the suite, 187/187, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. A throwaway probe (removed) hosted the hub on the KYC-not-done fixture with a "KYC INCOMPLETE" label and captured settled frames at collapse 0.80–1.4: no notch at 0.80 and 0.90, faint at 0.93, stronger at 0.96, full from 1.0. The glitch itself couldn't be reproduced there: without a window scene there's no display link, so SwiftUI animations finish at once, and `layer.render(in:)` draws neither 3D transforms nor presentation values. Not seen on a device or in Ayl.
    - Committed in `e94a37e` with items 122 and 123 and the GPU changes below.

A pipeline build into scratch was then byte-identical to the repo.

Both already had a no-title layout (dial or radar 39pt below the card's top). That layout is now the only one, as `Persona.radarTop` (39). `cardTitleTop`, `cardTitleLineHeight` and the old `radarTop` (40, title to radar) are gone. The dial and the radar still use the same offset, so the swap at the loop's end changes only the card (item 113). The radar now sits 65pt higher than with a title, 73pt under the subtitle. No Figma frame for it.
