The one failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen` (see there). Not seen rendered, in Ayl or on a device.
127. **SwiftUI review fixes from sections 1, 2 and 4, plus the number bug from section 3 (user, 2026-10-27, pasting the review's findings).**
    - Board work off the main thread: `ProfileV3BoardStore.fetch` and `restore` are `@concurrent`. Under `ApproachableConcurrency` a `nonisolated async` function runs on its caller's actor; a compiled probe showed it on main with the flag and off main without. So the board's JSON decode, mapping and disk write ran on the main thread on every load and refresh.
    - The hub view model is `@Observable`. Its 24 `@Published` properties are plain `private(set) var`s, and the tasks, the router and the pending-overlay closure are `@ObservationIgnored`. `ProfileV3HubView`, the second view in that file and `ProfileV3HubChrome` hold it as `let`; there were no `$` bindings or Combine uses. `stackBenefits` and `board` stay tracked because views read values derived from them. A view now re-renders only for the properties it reads.
    - Persona trait paging: the rows are a `LazyVStack`. The spinner is keyed by the row count, so its `onAppear` fires again for each page as it scrolls into view. Before, it sat in a plain `VStack`, fired once when the rows were built, and never loaded page 3. A failed page sets `loadMoreFailed`, and the footer shows Try again (`retryLoadMore()`); before, the spinner kept turning. A full reload clears it.
    - Glove box "View all": the email list is a `LazyVStack` with the same footer: `hasMoreEmails`, a spinner calling the now-internal `loadMoreEmails()`, and Try again after `emailPagingFailed`. Before, opening the list loaded exactly one more page and dropped errors. ATUIKit's sheet wraps content in a SwiftUI `ScrollView` (`ATBottomSheetWindowPresenter.swift:98-112`), so the lazy stack pages on scroll.
    - `profileV3FittedDetent(_:until:)` (`Support/ProfileV3FittedDetent.swift`) replaces the five copies of the measured-height detent. They are in `ProfileV3RideRegionsView`, the two sheets in `ProfileV3AppCredentialsView`, `ProfileV3VaultViews` and `ProfileV3PassportHowUsedView`. Each site keeps its fallback (`.large` for the two credential sheets, `.medium` for the rest) and its own corner radius.
    - `ProfileV3Toast` posts an `AccessibilityNotification.Announcement` when it appears and when its message changes.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3ReviewFixesTests` (3): a failed trait page waits for Try again and then keeps paging; a failed inbox page offers Try again and then appends; and a view reading only the hub's `phase` isn't notified when `notice` changes.
    - Second batch (user pasted the section 3 number bug and the section 4 items):
      - `ProfileV3ScalarDTO.stringValue` keeps fractions: `Int(exactly:)` or `String(value)`. Before, `rounded(.towardZero)` turned 3.5 into "3". `ProfileV3AgentValueDTO.doubleValue` reads the scalar's own `doubleValue` instead of parsing `stringValue`, so a stored 72.5 slider value loads as 72.5. It also now reads "1,200" as 1200. `intValue` still truncates, as before.
      - The glove box mapper's two ISO parsers and its label formatter are cached behind `OSAllocatedUnfairLock`s, the same pattern as `ProfileV3BoardMapper` (item 24). Before, they were created for every row. The output and the UTC time zone are unchanged; the UTC date bug from the review was not part of this batch.
      - `ProfileV3PersonaRipple` and `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard` tick on `ProfileV3AmbientSchedule` (24 fps) instead of `.animation` (display rate), with the same pause conditions. `ProfileV3PersonaProcessingCard` also carries item 126's uncommitted edits from another session.
      - `ProfileV3FormSectionView` is `Equatable` on the section, the avatar URL, the selected tab and which optional actions are present, and `ProfileV3AgentFormView` applies `.equatable()`. A keystroke now re-renders only the edited section; before, the closures made every section look new. The conformance stays main-actor isolated, because `nonisolated` can't read the closure properties.
      - `profileV3Card` is back to its pre-`e94a37e` body, with the shadow on the whole clipped card. It now matches `85dba88` byte for byte. The GPU runs showed no gain from the change (GPU section below).
      - PostHog replay in Ayl: the user chose to leave it unchanged. It stays a finding for whoever owns analytics.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3ReviewFixesTests` (6) adds two:
      - fractional numbers keep their fraction (scalar strings and agent values, including "1,200" and a Bool);
      - inbox dates parse with and without fractional seconds and give the same labels.
      - The hub test now uses `tapViewAllBenefits()`. Observation doesn't notify when a value is set to the same value, so `clearNotice()` on a `nil` notice proved nothing.
    - Verification: `xcodebuild … test` on throwaway iOS 26.5 simulators (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings; 195 of 196 tests pass. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`, which also fails on `e94a37e` alone (item 123). A Release build wasn't run, and nothing was run in Ayl or on a device. The lazy paging footers, the toast announcement and the fitted detents weren't seen rendered.
    - Committed with items 125 and 126 as `c8afbc3` (2026-10-27). It has since been pushed, with no tag.
128. **The persona loading caption uses the edit hint's gradient (user, 2026-10-27, screenshots of "Decoding your travel choices, won’t take long..." and of the hint's Figma fill: "lets not show like this. lets use the other linear gradient. a common one for both").**
    - The caption had `personaProcessingShimmer`, a horizontal C2C2C2 → E0E0E0 → white → E0E0E0 → C2C2C2 gradient (item 72), which washed its middle words out to white. It now uses the edit hint's fill (item 113), renamed `ProfileV3Theme.personaCaptionGradient`: C2C2C2 → 838383 → C2C2C2 at 5.4/46.2/94.6%, with the same box-relative start and end points. The edit hint uses it too.
    - The points were fitted to the hint's 240×32 box (131.87°). On the one-line caption (about 300×16) the same points tilt to about 20°, and the box's middle still lands at the 838383 stop, so the caption reads grey at the ends and darkest in the middle.
    - Renamed or removed: `personaProcessingShimmer` and `Palette.personaShimmerMid` (E0E0E0) are gone. `personaHintGradient` is now `personaCaptionGradient`, `Palette.personaShimmerEdge` is `personaCaptionEdge`, and `personaHintMid` is `personaCaptionMid`.
    - Verified: the working tree didn't build at the time because another session's analytics rework was half done (`ProfileV3Analytics` members missing). `c8afbc3` plus only these four files built with 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not seen rendered, in Ayl or on a device.
129. **Profile analytics follow the event spec (user, 2026-10-27, pasted spec of about 69 events plus 7 to remove).** The user's answers to the open questions: no default properties (`userId`, `userResidenceCode`, `lib`) for now; send the personal data as specified; ignore the second `aiFlightBenefit`; use the doc's agent names; report text edits when editing ends; spell it `settings_connector_permission_changed`; keep the live events the spec doesn't list; send the KYC uploads through the host.
    - Fixed:
      - The hub's `screen_view` (`aiProfile`) fires from `viewDidAppear`. From `viewDidLoad` it fired once per hub instance, so Ayl, which builds the hub once, never re-sent it when the Profile tab came back. `entrySource` is still always `chat` (`makeHubSurface` hard-codes it).
      - An agent sends one `screen_view`, named after the agent. It used to send `aiProfileAgent` on load and `aiProfileAgentForm` when the form arrived.
      - Ayl's `AYLProfileV3KYCHost.track` was empty, so the ITR events never reached analytics.
    - Removed: `profile_pass_viewed`, `profile_agent_card_viewed`, `profile_benefit_viewed`, `profile_coupon_viewed`, `profile_programme_viewed`, `profile_settings_row_clicked` and `profile_pass_expanded`, with the impression plumbing that only fed them (`onImpression` on the usage rail, agent grid and benefits list, the stack's `onFrontImpression`, `trackProgramme`). The KYC nudge impression stays. Glove box, credential store and concierge taps now send no row event.
    - Renamed:
      - `aiProfileSettings` → `aiSettings`.
      - `profile_benefit_clicked` → `profile_benefits_clicked`, adding `travellerId` and `benefitTitle`.
      - `profile_traveller_*` → `settings_traveller_*`.
      - `profile_field_saved` → `settings_residence_updated`, `settings_citizenship_updated` and `settings_currency_updated`, with new and old values. They fire after the server write.
      - `profile_contact_saved` → `settings_contact_info_updated`, with new and old `{email, phone}`.
      - `profile_logout` → `settings_logout_clicked` (the row) and `settings_logged_out` (when the logout is handed to the host).
      - `profile_agent_option_toggled` → `profile_agent_preference_changed`.
      - `passId` → `travellerId` on `profile_pass_switched`, and `connectorKey` → `connectorName` on the three disconnect events.
      - Extra properties events already had (`status`, `origin`, `isActive`, `passportChanged` and so on) are kept.
    - Hub:
      - `profile_worldpass_tapped`: `tapOn` is the face showing before the flip. Flip-demo flips don't count.
      - `profile_card_connect_clicked`: `card` from the back face's +, `connect_more` from the programmes page.
      - `profile_view_benefits_clicked` fires only from the stack's View all. The programmes page's benefits action now calls `showAllBenefits()`, which doesn't log.
      - `profile_benefits_screen_viewed` fires once per open, after the list loads, with the titles shown.
      - `profile_connector_added_card_clicked` fires from the back face's cards (`tapAddedCards`), not from Settings › Loyalty programmes.
      - `profile_agent_clicked` adds `travellerId`, `agentIndex` and `agentName`.
    - Names (`ProfileV3Analytics`):
      - `agentName(_:)`: `flight_agent` … `itinerary_agent`, `personal` → `personal_info_agent`, the persona tile or route → `travel_person_agent`, anything else `<key>_agent`.
      - `benefitScreenName(_:)` matches words in the benefit ID, falling back to `aiProfileBenefit`. The server's IDs aren't listed anywhere here. The ones seen in earlier sessions map as: `fast_track_immigration` → FTI, `baggage_protection_500` and `flight_delay_200` → insurance, `flight_credit_50` → flight, `hotel_credit` → hotel credit, and `zero_forex`, `slot_priority`, `airport_hotline`, `esim` and `activities` to their own names. `visas_approved_minutes` falls back. Confirm the list with the backend.
    - Loyalty:
      - `connectorType` is `all`, `cards`, `hotels`, `flights` or `travel_passes`. Connected rows now carry `ProfileV3LoyaltyProgramme.category`, from the row's `kind`, else the local catalogue.
      - Picker scroll depth: the carousel's binding now goes through `selectItem`, so swipes and taps both count. Each new 25% step is sent once per filter, and a jump sends one event with the deepest step. The first card never counts.
      - `profile_connector_card_details_entered` fires on the submit tap. `loyaltyDetails` has `points` and `membership_number` only for the fields the form has.
      - Insight viewed fires as the insight opens, with its body text, else its summary. Copying the number counts on the programmes tile and on the insight screen's tile.
    - Settings:
      - Account Center open and close come from `isAccountExpanded`'s `didSet`.
      - Row clicks come from the route. Information access, App access and a server `connectors` row send `settings_connector_clicked`.
      - Permission changes fire after the save succeeds. A section ID containing "connector" sends the connector event, any other the action event, and `ask_some` reads as `ask_sometimes`.
    - Agents:
      - Preference changed fires after each edit: options, a car when it changes, baggage, a slider on release, a date when a wheel settles, the opt-out and the lock-screen toggle.
      - A text field reports on focus loss, and on Save or Back, only when its text differs from what was last saved or reported (`commitText`).
      - `changedValue` uses the server encoding (`ProfileV3AgentSubmission.analyticsValue(of:)`): strings, numbers, lists, baggage as item → count, and `NSNull` for a cleared value.
      - `profile_agent_form_saved` fires once per traveller (the household's ticked IDs, else the form's profile), with `changedSections` and `oldSections` as key → value. A partial save reports saved for its saved IDs and failed for the rest.
      - Travellers saved and added carry `{name, phone, passport_number, relationship}`. A relationship edit is a traveller save.
      - `account_deletion_reason_submitted` adds `reason`.
    - KYC uploads:
      - New public `ProfileV3KYC.Event.photoUploaded(PhotoUpload)`, with photo, traveller ID, whether it's a co-traveller, the flow (`.capture` or `.passportUpdate`) and `actionSource`. The capture view model sends it after each successful upload; the hub decides co-traveller. The agent flow sends it after each "Upload new passport" upload, through the new `makeAgentFlow(kyc:isCoTraveller:)`.
      - Ayl (AI-Native, uncommitted): the host sends the spec's names (`passport_front_uploaded` / `co_traveler_passport_front_uploaded`, `passport_back_uploaded` / `co_traveler_passport_back_uploaded`, `selfie_uploaded` / `co_traveler_photo_scanned`) with `travellerId` and `actionSource`. It sends the ITR decision and response with the capture's `actionSource`. Its profile capture screens come from ATCaptureKit, which sends none of these, so nothing is doubled.
      - vdcios (`AppIntegration`, not compiled here): forwards only `.passportUpdate`, because its own KYC screens already send the capture uploads.
    - Dictionaries and arrays go to the host as they are. Ayl hands them to PostHog and Pulse unchanged; how CleverTap stores nested values wasn't checked.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3Analytics.scopedReporter` is a `@TaskLocal` that overrides the installed sink, so suites running in parallel record only their own events. `ProfileV3AnalyticsEventsTests` (13) covers names, the hub, the picker and details, the programmes page, settings rows and updates, agent edits and saves, a failed save, travellers, and both upload paths. The hub's `viewDidAppear` screen view has no test.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … build` 0 errors and 0 warnings; `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 208 of 209 pass. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`. Ayl builds (Debug, arm64 simulator, `BUILD SUCCEEDED`) and compiles `AYLProfileV3KYCHost.swift` against this working tree. It can, because AI-Native's `project.xcproj` has an uncommitted local package reference to `../AI-Profile` and its `Package.resolved` no longer pins AI-Profile; neither change is from this session. Nothing was run in Ayl or on a device, so no event was seen arriving in PostHog, CleverTap or Pulse.

130. **Secure credential store card: the shield art is Figma's own render (QA-1885 sub-issue pasted by the user, 2026-10-27: "Shield icon on Secure credential store card not matching design"; Figma `3664:13912`; then, on a hand-drawn glass rim: "still it's different … if we are trying to render it natively - lets use the image asset itself").**
    - Position bug, fixed first: the native art `ZStack` was 144pt tall (the 163pt big shield pulled 19pt above the card), its `frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity, alignment: .topTrailing)` kept that height, and a plain `.background` centred it on the 122pt card, so every piece drew 11pt above its design position (small shield top at 16.75 instead of 27.75).
    - Why an image: both shield vectors carry a Figma `GLASS` effect (refraction 1, depth 64.3, light angle −45°, intensity 0.8, dispersion 0.1, splay 0.43, read with `use_figma`). The SVG export drops it. A fitted native rim got to a mean luma difference of 4.3, and the user asked for the image instead. Figma renders glass against what is behind it, so an export of a shield node includes the card's gradient, not transparency.
    - Asset: `profile_v3_credential_card_art` (`ProfileV3ConnectorGlyph.credentialCardArt`) is the right-hand 144×119pt of the frame's own 3× render (x 236–380, 432×357 px): the big shield's glass edge, the small glass shield and the padlock, with the card's rounded top-right corner transparent. It stops at 119pt because Figma's render has its own darker band along the bottom 2pt, and the app draws its 2.37pt bottom edge on top.
    - View: `art` is that image at `Sizes.CredentialStore.cardArt` (144×119), with its left `cardArtFade` (16pt) faded so it blends into the native gradient on cards narrower than Figma's 380pt. It is pinned top-trailing in a full-card frame, then masked by the card shape lifted by `cardInsetShadow`, so the band along the rounded bottom-right corner isn't doubled. `ProfileV3CredentialGlassShield`, `credentialGlassRim`, the `cardBigShield*`, `cardShield*` and `cardLock*` sizes, the glass rim sizes and the three `credentialCard*Shield`/`Lock` opacities are removed. `ProfileV3CredentialLock` stays for the store sheet.
    - Match at 4× against the frame export, small shield box without the padlock: mean luma difference 16.0 originally, 4.3 with the native rim, 1.0 with the image.
    - The QA ticket's "green profile badge ("G" avatar) overlapping the shield" is not in the design: neither `3664:13912` nor the Settings frame `3664:16930` has one. It is most likely a Figma collaborator's avatar captured in the QA screenshot.
    - Test: `ProfileV3CredentialStoreCardTests` renders the card at 4× and finds the shield's top edge down column 322pt. It is within 1pt of Figma's 27.75 and failed against the old card.
131. **Opening the vault with Face ID also covers revealing passwords (QA-1885 sub-issue pasted by the user, 2026-10-27: "Redundant biometric prompt when viewing password after vault entry"). Reverses item 66's "opening the vault and then revealing asks twice".**
    - `ProfileV3VaultDevice.unlockVault()` tries `.deviceOwnerAuthenticationWithBiometrics` first and keeps that evaluated `LAContext` (`unlockedContext`, an `OSAllocatedUnfairLock`). `authenticate()`, which the reveal calls before signing the challenge and opening the envelope, returns that context when one is kept, so the Secure Enclave key (`.biometryCurrentSet`) is used through `kSecUseAuthenticationContext` without a second prompt. The "this phone has no biometrics" check still runs first.
    - The passcode only opens the vault: after `userFallback` or `biometryLockout`, or when biometrics can't be evaluated, `unlockWithPasscode()` runs the old `.deviceOwnerAuthentication` check and keeps no context, so each reveal asks for Face ID as before. Keeping a passcode-only context would make the key ask for Face ID mid-signature, and a cancel there falls into the sign failure path, which deletes the device key.
    - The session ends in `lockVault()` (invalidates the context): at the start of every unlock, in `ProfileV3CredentialStoreModel.cancelReads()` (the vault sheet's `onDismiss`, and Settings closing), and on `didEnterBackgroundNotification` (the model's new `lock` closure). A reveal after coming back to an open vault asks for Face ID again.
    - Trade-off: the fort-knox guide (6b) asks for one Face ID per reveal, which item 66 kept. Now one Face ID covers every reveal while the vault stays open. The server can't tell the difference, since the Secure Enclave signature is the same.
    - Test: `closingTheVaultOrLeavingTheAppEndsItsFaceIDSession` (`ProfileV3VaultLoginTests`, 18). Reusing the `LAContext` can't be tested on a simulator, so no prompt was seen live.
    - Verified for items 128, 130 and 131 on a copy of `c8afbc3` plus only this session's files. The working tree didn't build because of another session's analytics work at the time. `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted) passed 197 of 198 tests, with 0 errors and 0 warnings. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`. A Release build passed too. Not run in Ayl, against the backend or on a device.
132. **Glove box dials use Figma's glyphs (QA-1881 sub-issue pasted by the user, 2026-10-27: "GloveBox: Third card icon (credit card) not matching design"; Figma `5202:19338`).**
    - The cabinet's three dials drew ATUIKit's `mail`, `call` and `credit_card`. ATUIKit's credit card is a square-cornered outline. Figma's is the filled, rounded Material Symbols card with a cut-out stripe, which is what QA saw. Figma's mail and call are the rounded variants too.
    - New template assets `profile_v3_glovebox_dial_mail`, `_call` and `_credit_card` (`ProfileV3GloveBoxArt.dialMail`/`dialCall`/`dialCreditCard`) are Figma's SVGs. They keep Figma's root attributes like the other bundled SVGs, minus two things. The no-op `mask` wrapper is gone (it only clipped to the icon's bounds). The credit card's 50% group opacity is gone: Figma paints it #1C1B1F at 50%, which on the #FCFCFC face composites to the #8C8B8D the dial already tints every glyph (mail and call are #8C8B8D themselves).
    - Catalog gotcha: with `preserves-vector-representation` the glyphs drew nothing in the dials' `Canvas` (`context.resolve` plus `shading`), though a plain `Image` showed them. They now use ATUIKit's imageset form, a `1x` SVG with empty `2x`/`3x` slots and no vector flag, so Xcode rasterises them and the Canvas draws them.
    - `ProfileV3GloveBoxCabinet.drawDials` and `ProfileV3SettingsGloveBoxCard.dials` (item 115, the same three dials) use the new glyphs. The Virtual card section's header icon still uses ATUIKit's credit card.
    - Match: a 4× render of the cabinet against Figma's 4× export of the dial row. Glyph coverage (pixels darker than 200): mail 1,786 vs Figma 1,859, call 670 vs 702, credit card 1,618 vs 1,629. The rest of the luma difference is the dial body's shading, which wasn't changed.
    - Verified for items 130 (image) and 132 on a copy of `c8afbc3` plus this session's files: 197 of 198 tests on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
133. **Glove box shows the locked "ready to unlock" intro while the main traveler's KYC isn't done (QA-1881 sub-issue pasted by the user, 2026-10-27: "GloveBox: KYC not done state UI mismatch with design"). The user chose the board's KYC as the signal and KYC onboarding as the CTA.**
    - Before: the live `ProfileV3GloveBoxViewModel` built one intro for any account without an address, "Activate your Glove box" / "A travel email that works for you…" / Activate, which creates the address. The design's locked state (items 79 and 95) existed only in the DEBUG fixtures. Jaqen's `/me` `eligible` is decoded but unused. Nothing documents that it means KYC, so it wasn't used.
    - Now `ProfileV3GloveBoxViewModel(repository:isKYCComplete:)` asks, when `/me` has no address, whether KYC is done. If not, it shows the locked intro: "Your Glove box is ready to unlock", "A travel email, number and card that work for you. Your real ones stay private.", the ACTIVATE pill with the bolt (`status: .locked`), and "Complete KYC" (`action: .completeKYC`). With KYC done, the activate intro is unchanged. An account that already has an address goes to the home whatever the KYC state.
    - KYC source: `ProfileV3SettingsViewModel.isMainTravellerKYCComplete()` reads the main profile's `home.kycStatus.isComplete` from the board store's cached board, else `loadLatestBoard()`. With no board repository or a failed fetch it answers true, the old behaviour. An expired KYC counts as not done.
    - Complete KYC: `tapIntroAction` hands `.completeKYC` to `onIntroAction` and creates no address. `ProfileV3GloveBoxLiveFlow` takes `isKYCComplete` and `onCompleteKYC`. Settings passes `completeKYCFromGloveBox()`, which closes the cover, and `gloveBoxDidClose()` then calls `router?.presentConnectorOnboarding()`: the Connectors path that closes Settings and starts `kyc.startOnboarding` (item 84's presenter). The status re-fetch is skipped that time.
    - Copy: three hand-added keys in the toolkit's format, as in items 113, 115 and 120 (no Ditto token in this session): `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-title-unlock`, `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-description-unlock` and `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-button-complete-kyc` (`ProfileV3GloveBoxCopy.unlockTitle`/`unlockSubtitle`/`completeKYC`). They need adding in Ditto. The pill reuses `general-button-activate`. The catalog has 388 keys.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3GloveBoxTests`): `withoutKYCTheIntroIsLockedAndAsksForKYC` (copy, status, the action reported, no address created) and `withKYCTheIntroOffersToActivate` (the activate intro creates the address). `EmptyInboxStub` takes an account and counts creates. The Settings hand-off to onboarding has no test, because it needs a `ProfileV3SettingsRouter` fake.
    - Verified on the working tree, which by then included other sessions' uncommitted analytics work: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted) passed 212 of 213 tests with 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build passed with 0 errors and 0 warnings. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`. Not run in Ayl, against Jaqen or on a device, so the cover-then-onboarding hand-off wasn't seen running.
    - Items 128–133 (with item 129's analytics) are `7ec88f0` (2026-10-27, "Follow analytics spec and match vault and glove box designs", 69 files), on top of `c8afbc3`. The user's `/commit` staged the whole tree. `origin/master` was `e94a37e` (tag `1.0.10`) at the time. Both commits have since been pushed, and `7ec88f0` is tagged `1.0.11`.
134. **A performance playbook and tracker for the team (user, 2026-10-28: "formulate a generic way to check and optimize each module regularly or the whole app occasionally for release process … precautionary items … share the items to my team … a report and way to track this in professional way").** No package change.
    - Playbook: the Claude Doc "Atlys iOS Performance Playbook", https://claude.ai/artifact/Rit9hSYA1oC1uUk63YWzpq. It holds the 13 Profile findings with cost, fix and status, and four check levels: every PR, a sprint module check, a release gate and a quarterly deep dive. It also has 16 precautions with the evidence for each, proposed budgets beside today's hub numbers, the module-check steps, the release-gate checklist, the measurement commands, and Linear conventions, severities and roles.
    - The commands include `parse_gpu.py`, the per-second GPU parser behind the GPU numbers. It was recovered from this session's transcript, because the scratchpad had been cleared overnight.
    - Tracker: "iOS Performance Tracker", https://claude.ai/artifact/6fMcGQguAMmw91Qtn83PLJ. It is a page with a shared database (`db`, plus `user` with the `profile` scope) holding the collections `findings`, `runs`, `gates` and `modules`.
      - It scores each run against the budgets, reading the higher number of a range. It marks any value more than 10% worse than the same scenario's previous run, and tracks each release gate's nine checks and sign-offs.
      - Seeded with Ayl's 11 feature modules (from `AI-Native/Ayl/Features`), the 13 Profile findings and the three hub runs of 2026-10-27. The release gates list is empty.
    - Both are private until shared from their Share menus. The team needs Contributor access on the tracker to write.
    - The budgets are proposals for the team's first review. The hub at rest misses three: app GPU, compositor GPU and GPU active.
    - Two facts changed after the GPU work and are reflected there. Ayl turned PostHog session replay off by default (`50acf5e`, "perf(app): stop recording the screen…"). `isFloatingControlGlassEnabled` is `true` again at `7ec88f0`.

135. **Past trips: pick several countries, add them undated, then add dates from the card (user, 2026-10-28; Figma `2896:61624` country step, `2813:40903` undated card with "+ Add dates", `2869:59112` duration step, `2869:59997` actions: "the add country flow has been updated. 1. We now have multiple select - check for any existing multi select component if exists. 2. we can just add the countries after multi select or single select. 3. update dates post country addition.").**
    - Existing component: `ProfileV3CountryRow` already had the design's row (24pt mark, 12pt gaps, 24pt flag in a 32pt box, B1 SemiBold name, 18pt vertical padding for a 68pt pitch, divider inset 40). It gained `mark: .radio | .checkbox`. The new `ProfileV3CheckboxMark` mirrors `ProfileV3RadioMark` with ATUIKit's `checkBox`/`checkBoxOutlineBlank`, the same icons `ProfileV3OptionPickerRow` (the agent multi-select) uses. `ProfileV3CountryList` takes `selections: [ProfileV3Country]?`; when set, rows show checkboxes and selection comes from the list.
    - Country step: when the add flow adds without dates (`asksForDate` false: the host's `addsPastTripsWithoutDate`, or an `add_trip` with no `duration`), `ProfileV3AddPastTripViewModel.selectsMany` is true. Taps toggle `selectedCountries` in tap order, and the served CTA ("Done") calls `submitCountries()`. That posts the add `submit` with each `country` and no date, one request at a time, since the contract takes one country per submit. A country already on the page (dated or undated) gets the duplicate message and nothing is sent. If one add fails after others succeeded, the sheet still closes on the added ones, the page reloads, and the failure shows as the page notice. If the first add fails, the sheet stays with the error. The dated flow (vdcios, `addsPastTripsWithoutDate` false) keeps single select, then the duration.
    - Dates afterwards: an undated card (`trip.date == nil`) with an `onAddDates` handler drops its flag and name `undatedDrop` (25pt), as in `2813:40903`, and shows the "+ Add dates" pill `addDatesTop` (20.5pt) under the name: 32pt capsule, white at 19%, a 0.5pt #E5E5E5 border, a 16pt ATUIKit `add`, B2 SemiBold 12 white, 14.4/16pt side padding and a 5% shadow. The card hides its children from VoiceOver, so the pill is also an accessibility action. `ProfileV3PastTripsViewModel.tapAddDates(_:)` opens the editor in the new `.addDates(trip)` mode: the duration step for the pinned country (no way back to the country step), using the add screen's duration, then its `cta_label` ("Add country"). Saving posts the add `submit` with `country`, `year` and `month`. The server then lists the dated trip and drops the undated `country:XX` entry (the mock and test stub do the same through `listed`). Undated trips still have no Edit, so the actions sheet shows Delete alone.
    - Step indicator: add and add-dates show two steps, as in the frames (country is 1, duration is 2). Edit keeps its single step. `canGoBack` replaces the old `!isEditing` check.
    - Saves report `[ProfileV3PastTripDraft]`. `reload(revealing:)` reveals and focuses the first reloaded trip that matches any of them.
    - Copy: one hand-added key in the toolkit's format, `mo-aiprofile-profile-button-add-trip-dates` ("Add dates", `ProfileV3Strings.pastTripAddDates`); it needs adding in Ditto. The catalog has 389 keys.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3PastTripsTests`, 20): `severalCountriesAreAddedInOneGo` (toggling, tap order, two undated adds, the first revealed), `aFailedCountryKeepsTheOnesAlreadyAdded` (partial failure closes and shows the notice), `addingDatesTurnsAnUndatedCountryIntoADatedTrip` (no pill action for dated trips, the pinned duration step, the dated add replacing the undated card). Two older tests now expect two steps. The stub serves France too and can fail chosen countries.
    - Match: throwaway `ImageRenderer` renders compared with 3× and 2× Figma exports. Relative to the flag, the name and pill land where Figma has them after the 2pt pill fix. The checkbox rows match in geometry. Left as is: ATUIKit's checkbox has square corners where the design's Material Symbols one is rounded, and the row's mark is pure black like its radio where the design uses #323232. Pre-existing and not changed: every card's flag and name sit about 7.5pt above Figma's (a dated card's flag is at about 142pt against 149.5pt).
    - Ayl: the multi-select flow needs `addsPastTripsWithoutDate` true on `AYLProfileV3AppHost` (item 122), plus a package release.
    - Dates after adding are off for now (user, the same day: "let's remove the date update with a client config for now. no post date updates."). `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isPastTripAddDatesEnabled = false`, and `ProfileV3PastTripsView` passes `onAddDates` only while it's on. With it off, undated cards look as they did before this item: no pill, no accessibility action, flag and name at the dated position. `tapAddDates`, the `.addDates` mode and their test stay, so setting the flag to `true` brings it back. Verified with the flag off: 215 of 216 tests, 0 errors and 0 warnings (the failure is item 123's), and a Release build.
    - Verified: `xcodebuild … test` on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), 215 of 216 tests with 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build. The only failure is item 123's `theHubOpensALinkOnlyWhileItIsOnScreen`. Not run in Ayl, against the backend or on a device, so the sequential adds and the remote card photos weren't seen.

136. **Custom rules on agent forms, stored on the device until the backend supports them (user, 2026-10-28; Figma `6006:42055` form with "+ Custom", `6006:45838` the CUSTOM RULES section, `6006:43584`/`6006:44343` add, `6006:45105` saved, `6006:48143`/`6006:49635` edit: "new flow inside the agents - custom rules. this is yet to be supported by BE. let's add it here in client.").**
    - Data: `ProfileV3CustomRule` (id, text, `createdAt`) and `ProfileV3CustomRulesRepository` (`loadCustomRules`, `saveCustomRule` as an upsert, `deleteCustomRule`, each by `agentID` and `profileID`). `ProfileV3CustomRulesStore` keeps them under `profileV3.customRules.<owner>.<profileID or main>.<agentID>`, newest first. Live uses `UserDefaults.standard` with the session's `currentUserID` as owner, so rules don't cross accounts. The mock uses an in-memory store per instance. When the backend lands, only the three Live methods change.
    - Model: `ProfileV3CustomRulesModel` (rules, `editor` add or edit, `draft`, `phase`, `dropped`, `errorMessage`). The form view model creates it when `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isAgentCustomRulesEnabled` (true) and the repository conforms, the same cast the glove box card uses. It loads on init and is cancelled with the form.
      - `canSave` needs trimmed text, and in edit mode a change.
      - Adding saves, puts the rule first, clears the draft and keeps the editor open for another rule. `dropped` drives the drop animation and the "Custom rule saved" pill for `customRuleDropSeconds` (2.2s).
      - Editing saves through `ProfileV3SaveButton` ("save changes" → "Custom rule saved"), then closes after `savedDismissMilliseconds`.
      - The trash button deletes straight away and closes; there's no confirm step in the frames.
    - Form: `ProfileV3CustomRulesSection` follows the server sections when rules exist. It has `ProfileV3SectionHeader` "CUSTOM RULES" with the green tick, then a horizontal row of `ProfileV3CustomRuleCard`s: 202×170, 32pt corners, #FFF9ED→#D8D0BC with a 9pt-inset bevel at 24pt, Inter Medium 14 #333 at (24, 26) and 154pt wide, at most 5 lines, 17pt apart and bleeding to the screen edges. Tapping a card edits it. "+ Custom" (`ProfileV3CustomRuleButton`, the save button's white capsule chrome at 48pt, a 24pt `add`, Inter SemiBold 14) sits bottom-left in `ProfileV3AgentSaveBar` over the save bar fade, and gives way to the save bar while there are unsaved changes.
    - Editor: `ProfileV3CustomRuleEditorHost` overlays the agent sheet, fading in over white at 60% on `.ultraThinMaterial` (the frames use a 25pt background blur). The back button and title ("Add a rule"/"Edit a rule", H5 Inter SemiBold 20) sit where the agent header's are (`Agent.backTop`). The note stack sits 102pt below the back button's top. Under it is a back note at 50%, tilted 4.15° counterclockwise on the same centre. The front note is `ProfileV3CustomRuleNote`: 320×308, 20pt corners, #FFF9ED→#D8D0BC, a 13pt-inset bevel, "NOTE" at (30, 24), the text at (31, 62) and 247pt wide in Inter Medium 16 #1C1B1F, and the "ay" watermark (`profile_v3_custom_rule_watermark`, Figma's SVG) bottom-right. The text field is focused on open. The pill sits 64pt under the note: a black #1C1B1F "Save" (at least 149pt) for a new rule, the white "save changes" for an edit. The trash button (50pt white circle, #DFDFDF border, ATUIKit `delete` in #523600) is centred 10pt in from the note's top-right corner.
    - Saved motion, from `get_motion_context` on `6006:46652` (a 3.65s preview loop), played once: a copy of the saved note glows (radial rgba(77,217,112) 26%→13%→clear, to 65% in 0.55s and out in 0.57s), lifts 7pt, then falls 455pt while turning 3.9° clockwise and fading out (ends at 2.12s). The empty note underneath is the new one. Figma also straightens the back card into the next note; that isn't reproduced, and Reduce Motion skips the drop.
    - Copy: eight hand-added keys in the toolkit's format, all `mo-aiprofile-agents-*`: `button-custom-rule`, `title-custom-rules`, `title-add-rule`, `title-edit-rule`, `label-rule-note`, `button-save-rule`, `toast-custom-rule-saved`, `button-delete-rule`. They need adding in Ditto. The catalog has 397 keys.
    - Not matched: Figma's grain texture on the notes and cards (the inner area reads slightly lighter in Figma, which also shows the watermark more), Sometype Mono for "NOTE" (not bundled; the system monospaced bold stands in), the bottom bar's grey gradient with progressive blur (the existing white save bar fade is used), and the `eraser_size_1` button in the bottom bar, which no frame shows.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3CustomRulesTests` (5): adding keeps the editor open with the rule first, the draft cleared and the drop shown; editing saves only a change and then closes; deleting removes and closes; the store keeps rules per owner, traveler and agent and survives a new instance; an agent form loads its rules from the mock repository.

137. **Analytics QA round: the rows assigned to Aravindh (user, 2026-10-28, a pasted QA table with no other words; Amit's chat and eSIM rows and the default properties were left alone).**
    - `profile_connector_program_card_scrolled` was "missing". Ayl pins `7ec88f0` (1.0.11), which has it, the carousel writes every swipe through `selectItem`, and Ayl's `AYLEventName` doesn't filter names. But `trackSlideDepth` rounded down, so a long catalogue sent nothing until a quarter of the cards had gone by: the 12-card mock waited for the third card, and a 40-card catalogue would wait for the tenth. That is the likely cause; QA's exact steps aren't known. It now rounds up: the first swipe sends "25", each new quarter is sent once per filter, and "100" means the last quarter, not necessarily the last card.
    - Removed: `profile_programme_selected`, `profile_programme_added` and `profile_programme_edit_toggled`, with `ProgrammeEditState` and the `toState` key. `profile_programme_add_failed` stays, because the table doesn't list it.
    - Credential store, new events:
      - `pass_vault_clicked` (`actionSource: settings`): the Settings card tap, before Face ID (`tapCredentialStore`).
      - `pass_vault_add_credentials_clicked`: "+ Add credentials" (`tapAdd`).
      - `pass_vault_website_selected`: a picker row sends `website` (the login URL's bare host, `singaporeair.com`) and `cardName` (the site's name). "Other website" sends the typed host if it parses, and `cardName: other_website`.
      - `pass_vault_credentials_added`: after a new login saves. It sends `website`, `cardName` (the matched site's name, else the host) and `emailAdded` (the username as typed, which is personal data, as item 129 agreed). Edits and 409s send nothing.
      - `pass_vault_website_clicked`: the ↗ on the website field, in the add and saved-login sheets, through the new `ProfileV3AppCredentialsViewModel.openWebsite()`. `cardName` is the picked site's name while the website still matches it, else the saved login's name, else the host. The App access modes send nothing.
    - Glove box, new events:
      - `glove_box_opened`: the Settings card tap (`tapGloveBox`).
      - `glove_box_item_clicked`: every tap on a section, read literally from "when user clicks on email/phone/card". Email: copy address, an email, View all, pause or resume forwarding, send link. Phone: copy number or trip number, a call, View all. Card: the card art, a pay option, Add card, a transaction, View all. The cabinet dials aren't buttons. Detail-sheet actions (Tell me more, attachments) don't count.
      - `itemType` is `mail`, `phone` or `card`. `activated` means that item has been issued: an address, a real (copyable) number, a card with its last four. Today that is mail true, phone and card false.
    - `message_sent` with `actionSource: profile_card`: AI-Chat logs it, and a chat opened through `ChatDeepLinkPresenter` (the profile's insight Continue, item 77) builds its own view model, so the prompt reported `home`.
      - The fix is in AI-Chat: a new local clone at `/Users/aravindh/Desktop/Atlys/AI-Chat`, branch `feat/deeplink-action-source` off `main` `13575ea`, uncommitted.
      - `ChatDeepLinkRequest(text:context:sessionId:actionSource:)` (defaults to `nil`) is carried as `ChatFunnelViewModel.entryActionSource` and spent on the first send, the way `entryContext` marks only that send `trip_cta`. Later messages in the sheet keep the sheet's own source. Its README and CHANGELOG (Unreleased) are updated.
      - Ayl then needs `actionSource: "profile_card"` in `AYLProfileV3AppHost.openChat(prompt:)`, after an AI-Chat release and pin bump. That isn't applied: it wouldn't compile against 1.0.97.
      - Verified: AI-Chat builds for the iOS simulator (`BUILD SUCCEEDED`). The one warning in the touched files, `ChatFunnelViewModel.swift:2831` (`items` never mutated), is already on `main`. There's no AI-Chat test, because its suites never build a `ChatFunnelViewModel`, which needs a chat repository, an SSE client and a session. Not run in Ayl.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3AnalyticsEventsTests`, 15):
      - The picker test now checks that the second card alone sends "25", and that a successful add sends neither removed event. The programmes test checks no edit toggle.
      - `theCredentialStoreReportsEachStepOfAddingALogin` covers the funnel on the mock vault, a saved login's ↗ and App access sending nothing.
      - `theGloveBoxReportsOpeningAndEachItemTouched` covers an issued home, then the live mapper's placeholders: an uncopyable number sends nothing, and the call and Add card send `activated: false`.
    - `CHANGELOG.md` lists the changes under Unreleased › Analytics.
    - Verification: AI-Chat as above. The package's own run on an isolated copy was stopped when that session ended, and the item went into `1b81b56` untested. It first ran with item 143: `ProfileV3AnalyticsEventsTests` (15) passes, 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build passes. No event was seen arriving in Ayl's analytics.
138. **Stored phone numbers are read against the host's residence (PR #2, Amit Kumar, `962521e`, 2026-10-28 12:25; merged into local `master` as `3de2dd3` by a `git pull` at 12:38, outside this session).**
    - `PhoneCodeResolver.install(residency:)` keeps a `@Sendable () -> String`, and `ProfileV3Environment` installs the provider's `userResidencyCode` there. `defaultRegion` is that residence when it normalises to a region, else `Locale.current.region`, else `"IN"`. `resolve`'s `fallbackRegion` defaults to it (item 12).
    - `ProfileV3PhoneCodeResolverTests` (3, `.serialized`): a number without a country code takes the residence (AE, 971); one with `+1` keeps US; a blank residence falls back past the host.
    - The residence is a global (`nonisolated(unsafe) static var`). `.serialized` orders only that suite, so another suite reading a number without a code while it runs can see "AE". If a contact test turns flaky, start there.
    - PR #2 brought back `///` doc comments on `install` and `defaultRegion`, which item 27's rule keeps out of `Sources`. Its note is in `CHANGELOG.md` under Unreleased › Changed.
139. **KYC onboarding from the hub surface covers the host's tab bar (user, 2026-10-28, an Ayl screenshot of "A few to-dos only you can do" under the floating tab bar: "When presenting the KYC onboarding flow from the profile module - it's not hiding the bottom nav. this is properly handled for presenting the gmail connector though - check and handle it.").**
    - Cause: `makeHubSurface` puts the hub in its own `UINavigationController` inside the host's tab (item 41). The hub's `presentOnboardingKYC` and Settings › Connectors' `startConnectorOnboarding` passed that controller to `kyc.startOnboarding(on:)`, and Ayl's host pushes `KYCFlowHost.push(on:)` onto it, so the flow sat inside the tab with the bar over it. Ayl's bar is the system `TabView` bar, which `HomeView` shows on the Profile tab with `.toolbar(…, for: .tabBar)`. The Gmail connector never had this, because `presentKYCCapture` presents it `.fullScreen` from the hub, over the whole window.
    - Fix: the new `ProfileV3OnboardingLauncher.start(_:using:on:completion:)` (top-level, like `ProfileV3PassportScanLauncher`), with `ProfileV3OnboardingPresentation` `.push` or `.fullScreen`. `.push` is the old call. `.fullScreen` makes an empty, bar-hidden `UINavigationController`, hands it to `startOnboarding(on:)`, then presents it `.fullScreen` with the default slide-up, as the Gmail connector is, from the top of whatever `navCon` presents. A host that finishes inside `startOnboarding` gets nothing presented. On finish, the launcher waits out a presentation still running, dismisses the container if the flow hasn't, and only then reports, so `kycCompleted(resuming:)` can present the next sheet.
    - Which caller gets which: `ProfileV3HubHostingController` takes `onboarding:` (default `.push`), `ProfileV3HubSurface` passes `.fullScreen`, and the hub hands its value to its `ProfileV3SettingsSheetPresenter` (new `onboarding:` parameter). `makeHub(navCon:)` and `presentAddTraveller(navCon:)` keep `.push`: vdcios's host pushes onto the app's own `AVNavigationViewController` and accepts nothing else.
    - Ayl needs no change. In ATKYCFlow 1.0.7 (Ayl's pin, read from its DerivedData checkout), `KYCNavigator.dismiss` dismisses a presented navigation controller whose root is the flow and then calls back, the same path as Ayl's own `KYCFeature.presentOnboarding`. The launcher's dismiss is then skipped.
    - Behaviour change: onboarding now slides up and goes back down, like the Gmail connector, instead of being pushed from the right. Like the Gmail connector, a `.fullScreen` modal takes Ayl's root out of the window while it's up.
    - Not fixed, and not new: vdcios's `VDCProfileV3KYCHost.startOnboarding` asserts on anything but `AVNavigationViewController`, so onboarding from a hub surface fails there with or without this change. vdcios's SPM branch calls `makeHubSurface` (B5).
    - `README.md` (step 2) says which controller `startOnboarding(on:)` gets, and `CHANGELOG.md` has it under Unreleased › Changed.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3OnboardingPresentationTests` (4), in a window whose root is a `UITabBarController`: `.fullScreen` hands the host a separate container presented by the tab bar controller with nothing pushed on the tab's stack, and closes it before reporting `.completed`; a flow that closes its own container is reported once; a host that gives up at once presents nothing; `.push` hands the host the caller's own controller.
    - Verification: a `build-for-testing` of a scratch copy (`3de2dd3` plus this change), 0 errors and 0 warnings. The suite first ran with item 143 (`1b81b56` plus that item). **Two of the four `ProfileV3OnboardingPresentationTests` fail:** `theHubsOwnSurfaceRunsOnboardingOverTheTabBar` (lines 31–33) and `aFlowThatClosesItsOwnContainerIsReportedOnce` (lines 50–51). Both pass the presentation checks, then wait 5 s (`waitUntil`, line 94) for a dismissal that never completes, so nothing is reported and `tabs.presentedViewController` stays set. Their window is a bare `UIWindow(frame:)` with no window scene, where UIKit presentation hasn't completed in earlier probes (items 97 and 124), so this is probably the harness rather than the launcher. Not investigated. The flow hasn't been seen running in Ayl either.
140. **Custom rule editor: Save stays above the keyboard, and a new rule closes the editor and shows in the form (user, 2026-10-28, a device screenshot of "Add a rule" with the black Save pill half under the keyboard: "The save button is fixed - which is causing this cut off when keyboard is active"; then "once we save a custom rule - show the saved rule instead of showing again the new add custom notes flow. scroll to the bottom if needed.").** Changes item 136.
    - Cause of the cut-off: the editor was a top-aligned `VStack` of fixed pieces: header (`Agent.backTop` 30 + the 50pt back button), 52pt, the 308pt note, `pillTop` 64, the 40pt pill. That is 544pt, and the keyboard with its suggestion bar leaves about 538pt of the sheet on a 956pt phone, so the pill ran under it. The design's keyboard frame (`6006:44343`, 966pt tall, a 321pt keyboard with no suggestion bar) has the pill 64pt under the note and 32pt above the keyboard. Those were the frame's numbers, not a rule for other keyboards.
    - Layout: `ProfileV3CustomRuleEditorLayout` (private `Layout`) places the header, the notes and the bottom from `ProfileV3CustomRuleEditorFit(height:header:bottom:)`, which measures them each pass, so an error line above the pill counts. It keeps the design values while they fit. Otherwise it keeps Save `pillMinBottom` (16) above the bottom of the safe area, which is the keyboard's top, by closing the gap above the pill to `pillMinTop` (24), then the gap above the note to `noteMinTop` (24), then shortening the note to `noteMinHeight` (192). At 538pt only the pill gap closes (to 42); a 457pt space gives gaps of 24 and a 273pt note; 377pt (an SE with the keyboard) gives a 193pt note. Without the keyboard nothing moves. The layout follows the keyboard's own animation.
    - Note: `ProfileV3CustomRuleNote` takes the height it's offered, between 192 and 308 (ideal 308, so an unconstrained note is unchanged). The text area runs from `noteTextTop` to `noteTextBottom` above the bottom edge, and the watermark is pinned to the bottom-right by `watermarkInset`, which is derived from the measured `watermarkCentreOffset` (1.02, 4.64 at full size). The back note gets the same height.
    - After a save: a new rule now saves like an edit. "Save" turns into "Custom rule saved" (`phase == .saved`) for `savedDismissMilliseconds`, then the editor closes. Closing, whether by that timer or Back, publishes `revealedRuleID`. `ProfileV3CustomRulesSection` then scrolls its card row to the start, where the new card is, and calls `onReveal`, which `ProfileV3AgentFormView` (now inside a `ScrollViewReader`) uses to scroll to the section's bottom (`customRulesID`). Both use `Motion.customRuleReveal` (0.4s ease-in-out), or no animation under Reduce Motion. The section is the form's last element, so this is "scroll to the bottom": nothing moves when it's already in view. Edits close without a reveal; the card is already where the user tapped it.
    - Removed: the add flow's drop motion and its state (`Dropped`, `dropped`, `showsSavedToast`, `ProfileV3CustomRuleDrop`, its keyframes, `dropLift`…`dropTilt`, `glowRadius`, `ruleGlow` and its three palette stops, `ruleGlowPeak`, the `customRuleGlow…`/`Hold`/`Lift`/`Fall…`/`DropSeconds` timings). Item 136's "keeps the editor open for another rule" no longer applies.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3CustomRulesTests`, 7): `savingANewRuleClosesTheEditorAndRevealsTheRule` (saved state, close, reveal, and Back during the saved state revealing at once) replaces the keep-open test; the edit test checks it reveals nothing; `theEditorKeepsItsDesignSpacingWhenThereIsRoom` and `saveStaysAboveTheKeyboardByClosingGapsBeforeShorteningTheNote` (538, 457 and 377pt, Save exactly 16pt above each).
    - Verification: first run with item 143 (`1b81b56` plus that item): this item's tests pass, 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build passes. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
141. **Past trips: several countries go in one request (backend note pasted by the user, 2026-10-28: "Past trips — add several countries at once. DEPLOYED 2026-10-28 (PR #23426, ai-kyc only)"). Changes item 135's submit.**
    - Contract: POST `/board/submit` with the served `submit` plus `countries` (ISO-2 list) and no `year`/`month`; each country becomes its own undated card. All or nothing: an empty list, a code that isn't ISO-2, or more than 250 codes is a 422 `kyc_invalid_request` and nothing is saved. Duplicates in the list are ignored, and adding a country that's already there is fine (a deleted one comes back). A single `country`, with or without `year` + `month`, still works; `country` is ignored when `countries` is sent. 503 `trip_save_failed` means retry.
    - Client: `ProfileV3BoardSubmitRequest` gained `lists: [String: [String]]`, encoded after `extras` (an action field with the same key is left out). `ProfileV3PastTripsMapper.addRequest(_:countries:)` and the new `ProfileV3PastTripsRepository.addPastTrips(_:countries:)` (Live posts it; Mock adds each undated; Failing throws). `submitCountries()` sends one request in tap order. It used to loop one `addPastTrip` per country and, after a partial failure, closed the sheet on the countries that got through with the error as the page notice. Now a refusal keeps the sheet open with the server's message, the selection kept for a retry, and `ProfileV3PastTripsViewModel.saved(_:)` no longer turns an editor error into a notice. Single and dated adds kept `addPastTrip` and the `country` shape until item 142 removed them.
    - Unchanged on purpose: a country already on the page still gets the duplicate message and nothing is sent, though the server would now accept it.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3PastTripsTests`, 21): `severalCountriesGoAsOneListWithNoYearOrMonth` (the body, including a served `country` field left alongside), `severalCountriesAreAddedInOneGo` now expects one list request, and `aRefusedListAddsNothingAndKeepsTheSheetForARetry` replaces `aFailedCountryKeepsTheOnesAlreadyAdded`.
    - Verification: first run with item 143 (`1b81b56` plus that item): this item's tests pass, 0 errors and 0 warnings, and a Release build passes. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
142. **Past trips are country-only: the old add call, the month and year picker, Edit and "+ Add dates" are gone (user, 2026-10-28: "1. let's clean up the old add past trips api 2. let's clean the date flow completely from client also, remove config and the date picker screen, and add date button in the trips cell."; then "let's remove edit flow too").** Changes items 60, 121, 122, 135 and 141.
    - API: `ProfileV3PastTripsRepository` is now `loadPastTrips`, `loadAddTrip`, `addPastTrips(_:countries:)` and `deletePastTrip`. Removed: `addPastTrip(_:country:date:)`, `editPastTrip(_:year:month:)`, `ProfileV3PastTripsMapper.addRequest(_:country:date:)`, and their Live, Mock, Failing and test-stub versions (including `CachedBoardRepository` in `ProfileV3AgentBoxTests`).
    - Decoding: the `duration` block on `past_trips` and `add_trip`, and a trip's `edit` action, are no longer read; their DTOs and `ProfileV3TripDuration`/`ProfileV3TripMonth` are deleted. A trip's `month`, `year` and `month_label` still decode, so dated trips the server sends keep showing their month and year.

Drafts are country codes only (`ProfileV3PastTripDraft(countryCode:)`), so `matches(_:)` is also the duplicate check (`duplicates` removed): a country already on the page, dated or not, still gets the duplicate message and nothing is sent.
    - Trips page: the actions sheet has only Delete (`ProfileV3PastTripActionsSheet(deleteTitle:onDelete:)`, 116pt), and "more" opens only when the server serves a delete action; before, an edit action alone was enough. `tapEdit`, `tapAddDates`, the card's "+ Add dates" pill and its 25pt drop are removed, so item 121's scroll back to an edited trip is gone. A newly added trip is still revealed and focused.
    - Config: `ProfileV3FeatureFlags.isPastTripAddDatesEnabled` and the public `ProfileV3AppHost.addsPastTripsWithoutDate` (item 122, released in 1.0.10) are removed, with `addsTripsWithoutDate` on the hub and trips view models. A host that still implements the property keeps compiling. Ayl's `AYLProfileV3AppHost` has `var addsPastTripsWithoutDate: Bool { true }`, now unused: drop it once Ayl builds against a package with this change, not before, because with the GitHub-pinned package that line is what keeps Ayl on country-only adds. vdcios's host never set it, so the Atlys app now adds country-only trips too.
    - Deleted: `Components/ProfileV3DurationWheel.swift` (`ProfileV3DurationWheel`, `ProfileV3NotchedDivider`); the duration-step and wheel sizes in `TripSheet`; `actionsDividerGap`, `actionsSecondGap`, `actionsSingleHeight` and `dividerDash` (`actionsHeight` is now the one-row 116); the card's `undatedDrop` and `addDates…` sizes; `Counts.pastTripYears`; `Typography.tripWheelSize`; `Palette.tripNotch` and `tripAddDatesStroke`; the `tripLabel`, `tripNotch`, `tripDivider`, `tripWheelNear`, `tripWheelFar` and `tripAddDates…` opacities; `Motion.tripStep`; `ProfileV3TripCalendar.shortMonthName`; the mock store's `reschedule`; and the fixtures' durations and edit actions.
    - Strings: `mo-aiprofile-profile-button-add-trip-dates` (hand-added in item 135, never in Ditto) is removed from the catalog and the generated Swift with `ProfileV3Strings.pastTripAddDates`. The catalog has 396 keys.
    - Tests (`ProfileV3PastTripsTests`, 14): the mapping tests now carry `edit` and `duration` in their JSON to show they're ignored; `writesEchoTheServerActionAndReplaceItsSameNamedFields` (a list replaces an action field of the same name); `severalCountriesAreAddedInOneGo` also checks the CTA, `canSubmit`, focus and `onChange`; new `aTripOffersOnlyDeleteAndOnlyWhenTheServerServesIt`. Removed: the old country-only body, the duration cut-off, the dated add, both Edit tests, the add-dates test, and the two tests of the host and screen without a duration.

Afterwards the sheet closes, the removed cards leave the list, the hub is told (`programmesChanged`), a medium haptic plays (`deletions`) and the page reloads. If everything went, edit mode ends. 145. **The personal agent shows a passport placeholder when no passport is on file (user, 2026-10-28, a pasted Ayl response of `GET …/board/agent/personal?profile_id=…` with `passport_empty` and no `passport`, plus Figma `1598:54129` (USA), `1485:30506` (India) and the guilloche `4978:9080` inside a third copy, `4978:9077`: "we need to show a placeholder if no passports are available").**
    - Contract, from that response: `passport_empty {title, body, cta_label}` ("Upload passport", "Your details are ready whenever you need a visa. No forms, no retyping.", "Upload") comes instead of `passport`. It has no name, country or scan directive. The three Figma frames are the same 380×292 card; a page search found no full screen with it.
    - Mapping: `ProfileV3AgentScreenDTO.passportEmpty` (lossy `ProfileV3AgentPassportEmptyDTO`). With no `passport`, `ProfileV3AgentMapper` puts `ProfileV3PassportMapper.emptySection` first: the same `passport` section and "PASSPORT DETAILS" header, status pending, and a `ProfileV3PassportField` with `passport: nil`, no `rescan` or `previous`, and `emptyState` (`ProfileV3PassportEmptyState`: title, body, ctaLabel; blank → ""). A served `passport` wins; neither means no card, as before.
    - Name and country, which the payload lacks: the live repository passes the cached board's name for the screen's `profile_id` (`travellerName(for:)`; main when nil), and the account's passport nationality for the main traveller (`accountCountry(for:)`, as the details card does). Unknown leaves them blank. Ayl's `passportNationality` is nil (item 90), so in Ayl the empty card's country strip is empty.
    - Card: `ProfileV3PassportCard.Content.upload` now carries the empty state; it was never reached before and drew client copy. Same shell (F6F7F7, 12pt corners, pink glow, strip, divider, guilloche) and the details card's page edges, which the standalone frames don't show. The name is centred 72pt down (`emptyNameTop`; the details card keeps it leading at 70). The copy starts 105.5pt down (`promptTop`, was 34 with vertical centring): title H7 Inter Medium 16 #4D4D4D in a 26pt line box (`promptTitleLineHeight`, `typoGraphy` only spaces lines) up to 253pt wide, then 9pt, the body B2 Inter Medium 12 grey-400 at 223pt, then 20pt and the Upload capsule. Empty pieces hide; no `cta_label` means no button.
    - Button: `ProfileV3ContinueButton` hugging its content (`fixedSize`), with a new `horizontalPadding` (24, `promptButtonPadding`; 0 everywhere else) and the share glyph at 20pt (`iconSize: Icon.lg`). The glyph is Figma's rounded Material Symbols `ios_share`, bundled as `profile_v3_ios_share` (`ProfileV3Vector.iosShare`), in place of ATUIKit's square `iOS_share`, as item 132 did for the glove box dials.
    - Upload: `startPassportUpdate()` scans for the directive's profile, else the card's `rescan` profile, else, with no `rescan` (the empty card), the agent's own `profileID` (`passportScanProfileID`). The hub opens agents with the selected pass ID, so the main traveller's empty card scans with the main's own `profile_id`; the agent request and saves already send it. A first passport (no passport when the scan started) skips "What happened to your old passport" even if `passport/update` routes there (the mapper falls back to it when `next` is missing) and refreshes the form instead.
    - Strings: `ProfileV3Strings.uploadPassport`, `uploadPassportBlurb` and `upload` are removed. Their keys `mo-aiprofile-documents-button-upload-passport`, `-text-upload-blurb-start` and `-button-upload` are unused in the catalog until they are deleted from Ditto and the files rebuilt (item 125).
    - Tests: `ProfileV3PassportEmptyStateTests` (5): the pasted payload maps to a pending card with the served copy, name and Indian strip; a passport wins, and neither or an unreadable placeholder gives no card; blank copy stays empty; a first upload scans and uploads for the agent's traveller, skips the reason step and reloads; a replacement still scans for its `rescan` profile and asks why. Fixtures: `ProfileV3PassportFixtures.emptyState` / `emptyField`.
146. **The hub doesn't scroll while the "Build your household" card is selected (user, 2026-10-28, a screenshot of that card with the empty shells below it: "let's make sure that the page is not scrollable when it's at this add household card").**
    - `ProfileV3HubView` adds `viewModel.isHouseholdSelected` to the page `ScrollView`'s `scrollDisabled`. That modifier sets an environment value which also reaches the scroll views inside the page, so it alone would have frozen the pass carousel on the household card. The page content now sets `\.isScrollEnabled` back to the inherited value minus the existing locks (`locksScrolling`: no home, an expanded pass, a benefit or coupon hero), so inner scroll views behave exactly as before.
    - The carousel takes touches until the hub is half collapsed (`collapseInteractionLimit` 0.5), so the household card can be picked with the page partway down. Picking it then scrolls back to the top (`ScrollViewReader` to the top spacer's `topID`, `Motion.contentSwitch`, no animation under Reduce Motion), so the lock never leaves the page stuck mid-scroll.
    - Test: `ProfileV3HouseholdScrollTests` (1) hosts the hub on the mock: the page's `UIScrollView` is disabled with the household card selected while the carousel's stays enabled, and selecting a pass enables the page again. The scroll back to the top isn't covered; animated scrolls don't advance in a test window (item 121).
147. **The travel persona's delight loader has haptics (user, 2026-10-28: "for the delight loader for travel persona - let's add haptics").** There was no design or spec for them; the pattern below was chosen in this session.
    - Wave ticks: a soft impact at 0.5 intensity (`.sensoryFeedback(.impact(flexibility: .soft, intensity: Motion.personaWaveTickIntensity))`) each time the dial's colour wave peaks on a trait label, at its brightest stop (`ProfileV3PersonaWave.peak`, F5A6D1, 8.5% into the label's window), on the dial's own clock. `ProfileV3PersonaWave.nextPeak(after:)` only counts labels that have appeared (`ProfileV3PersonaProcessingSlot.entranceDelay`, 0.95s + 70ms × index), so the first tick is at 1.63s on Local. The ticks then follow the labels clockwise, ten per 4s loop once all ten are in (0.3–0.5s apart). A `.task` sleeps until each peak. Nothing ticks under Reduce Motion or when `animates` is off.
    - Reveal: a `.success` when the loader hands over to the radar with content (`ProfileV3TravelPersonaViewModel.decodedReveals`, counted in `finishDecodingLoop`). A failed load and the normal loader after the five-show cap (item 120) play nothing.
    - Tests: `hapticsTickEachLabelAsTheWaveLightsItOnceItHasAppeared` (`ProfileV3PersonaProcessingTests`, 4): 30 ticks in order, each on exactly one label that has appeared and at its brightest colour, six in the first loop and ten in each of the next two. The two loader tests in `ProfileV3TravelPersonaTests` check that a successful reveal counts once and a failed one doesn't.
    - Verification for items 145–147: a Release build of the sources, 0 errors and 0 warnings. The first test run didn't build: `ProfileV3HouseholdScrollTests` used `#expect(… .allSatisfy(\.isScrollEnabled))` (see Gotchas), now a local first. The second ran 239 tests (with a throwaway render probe): every new and touched suite passed except the haptic test, whose own bound was wrong (25 ticks reach only 9 into the third loop; it now takes 30 and checks the second and third loops). The final run, on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted), ran 238 tests in 36 suites with 0 errors and 0 warnings: 235 pass, and the three failures are the known ones (item 123's deep-link test, item 139's two onboarding tests). Not run in Ayl, against the backend or on a device, so the haptics weren't felt and the hub's scroll back to the top wasn't seen.
    - Render check (item 145), 1x `ImageRenderer` of the India and USA cards against Figma's 1x renders, measuring ink bands: name y 76–85 (Figma 77–86, same x), title 113–127 (exact), body lines 144/160/176 (Figma 144/159/175), Upload glyphs 223–239 (224–240) and capsule 207–256 × 129–250 (208–256 × 130–250). Pre-existing and left alone: the country strip sits about 1.5pt higher and is a little wider than Figma's, as the strip uses the system monospaced bold where Figma uses Sometype Mono; the details card shares it.
148. **The medical card's name wraps short of the caduceus (user, 2026-10-29, an Ayl screenshot of the Medical agent with "ANUJ MERUBHAI KHANDEKHA" running under the emblem's wings: "let's keep a buffer space on the right side - considering the medical symbol. Post that the name should wrap into a new line - breaking the name properly.").**
    - Cause: the name row had the card's whole content width. The emblem (109pt, `emblemBleed` 35pt past the card's right edge, 23pt down) has its wings level with the name's first line, so a name wider than about 235pt on a 393pt phone ran under them. The art is remote (`caduceus.png`, 220px square); its opaque pixels start 17px (8.4pt) inside the frame.
    - Now `ProfileV3MedicalCardView`'s name row ends `Metrics.nameTrailing` (50pt) before the content's right edge: the emblem's visible width (`emblem − emblemBleed`, 74) less the card padding, plus `nameEmblemGap` (8). The name stops 8pt before the emblem's frame, about 16pt before the wing tips, and wraps there. SwiftUI breaks between words, filling the first line: on a 393pt phone the screenshot's name reads "ANUJ MERUBHAI / KHANDEKHA"; on a 440pt phone it still fits on one line, about 30pt clear of the wings. A single word longer than the line is still broken mid-word, as before.
    - The flag, when the card has a country, lines up with the first line (`HStack(alignment: .top)`, pushed down by half the difference between the H6 line height and the 18pt flag) instead of floating between the lines. A one-line name looks as before.
    - The rows aren't changed: below the wings the art narrows to the staff, which starts at about the content's right edge, so values in the 196pt column don't reach it.
    - Test: `ProfileV3MedicalCardTests` (1) renders the card at 333pt (a 393pt phone) and reads its pure-white text bands: the name takes two lines, as wide as "ANUJ MERUBHAI" and "KHANDEKHA" in that font to within 3pt, and neither passes the reserve. With the reserve taken out it fails (one line).
    - Verified: renders at 315, 333 and 380pt, with the downloaded caduceus drawn over the card because the remote art doesn't load in a render: the screenshot's name, the same with an Indian flag, "VENKATA SATYA NARAYANA MURTHY KONIDELA" (three lines at 315), a short name with a flag, and a 30-letter single word. Then the suite on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted): 239 tests in 37 suites, 0 errors and 0 warnings, 236 pass, and the three failures are the known ones (item 123's deep-link test, item 139's two onboarding tests). A Release build has 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl or on a device.
149. **Permissions screen v2, on the harness API (user, 2026-10-29: Figma "Permissions v.2" `6346:5918`, "let's update the permissions screen"; then a backend Slack thread from Shubham Tiwari with `GET https://api.atlys.com/api/harness/v1/permissions` and `PUT …/permissions/{key}` `{"value": "always"}`: "this is the new BE for the permissions, remove the old APIs for the permissions flow now").**
    - Contract: `{"permissions": [{key, label, description, kind, parent_key, options: [String], default, value, updated_at}]}`. The example: `email` (permission, `on`/`ask`, value `ask`), `email_cc` (setting, parent `email`, `always`/`when_needed`, value `always`), `call` (permission, `on`/`ask`, value `ask`). The PUT's response isn't read.
    - Removed: the board's `/permissions` GET, the `save_permissions` submit (`ProfileV3SavePermissionsRequest`), `ProfileV3PermissionsScreenDTO`/`GroupDTO`/`OptionDTO` and `ProfileV3SettingsMapper.permissions`. `ProfileV3API.permissions` is now the harness GET (`harnessBasePath` `/api/harness/v1`), and `.updatePermission(key:body:)` the PUT. Live saves through `client.perform` with the 401 retry and doesn't invalidate the board store, since permissions aren't on the board.
    - Mapping (`ProfileV3PermissionsMapper`): entries without a key, repeated keys (the first wins) and rows without options are dropped. An entry with no `parent_key`, or whose parent isn't a top-level key, is a card (`ProfileV3PermissionSection`, `label` as the tag). An entry whose `parent_key` names a card is another row in it, in server order. A row (`ProfileV3PermissionRow`) shows its `description`, else for a child its `label`. Selection is `value` when it's one of the options, else `default`, else none. `kind` and `updated_at` aren't read. The domain lost the screen title and option subtitles; the sheet title is `ProfileV3Strings.permissions`.
    - Option labels are client copy: `on` "Yes" (`general-choice-yes`), `off` "No" (`general-choice-no`; not sent yet, but the design's call card shows No), `ask` "Always ask" (`agent-settings-always-ask`), and `always` "Always" and `when_needed` "When needed" (new hand-added keys `mo-aiprofile-agent-settings-always` and `-when-needed`, which need adding in Ditto). Other values show with underscores as spaces and a capital first letter. The five v1 `ProfileV3Strings` (connector and web access defaults, "Ask for some actions" and the two descriptions) are deleted; their keys stay unused in the catalog until they're deleted from Ditto. The catalog has 404 keys.
    - UI (Figma sheet `6346:5949`): white `profileV3AccessCard(cornerRadius: 40)` cards (the modifier now takes a radius), 31pt inset (30 plus the stroke), 24pt between the tag, rows and dashed dividers, 43pt between cards, the first 36pt under the chrome. The divider is #E6E7E8 with a 2/2 dash and no layout height, through negative padding, because SwiftUI doesn't draw a shape in a zero-height frame. A question is B1 Medium 14 #0B0B0B in a 19pt minimum line box, 24pt above its control.
    - `ProfileV3SegmentedControl` (new, Components): a 48pt #F9FAFB capsule with the #E6E7E8 hairline, 4pt inset, equal segments. The selected one is a white capsule with a #E8E8E8 hairline and the access-card shadow (15, y 4, 5%), sliding with `matchedGeometryEffect`. Labels are B2 SemiBold 12 black when selected, B2 Medium 12 at 50% otherwise, one line, scaling down to 0.8.
    - Model: `select(_:in:)` takes the row key and stays optimistic, reverting on failure. `ProfileV3PermissionsSettingsModel` depends on the new `ProfileV3PermissionsRepository`, which `ProfileV3SettingsRepository` refines. Analytics: `permissionChanged(key:to:from:)`. With the harness keys every change is `settings_action_permission_changed` (no key contains "connector"), and the event doesn't say which permission changed: email and call both send `ask`/`on`.
    - Left as served: the frame's copy ("SEND MESSAGES", "Ayl sends emails and messages on your behalf", "MAKE CALLS", the No / Always ask / Yes call options) differs from what the API sends. The API lists `on` before `ask`, so the control reads Yes | Always ask where the design has Always ask | Yes. The frame's second card has 16pt between question and control and the first has 24; the code uses 24 for both. The close button, grabber and backdrop are the shared settings sheet's, unchanged.
    - Tests: `ProfileV3PermissionsTests` (6): the Slack payload's cards, rows, labels and selection (equal to the mock fixture `ProfileV3PermissionFixtures`); the value-then-default fallback and an unknown option's label; dropped and orphaned entries; the GET and PUT paths, methods and body; a choice saving under its key, with no save for an unchanged choice; a refused save restoring the previous choice. The analytics test now changes `email` and `email_cc`.
    - Render check: a hosted 440pt window (`layer.render`) with the frame's copy, against Figma's render of `6346:5949`. Card 1 is 330pt and its tag, questions, controls and divider are within 0.5pt of the frame; card 2 is 191pt (Figma 183, the 16pt gap); the cards are 43pt apart. `layer.render` draws a stray 1pt straight segment at each end of the capsule track; an `ImageRenderer` render of the control alone has a clean curve, so it comes from the snapshot path.
    - Verified: the suite on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted): 245 tests in 38 suites, 0 errors and 0 warnings, 242 pass, and the three failures are the known ones (item 123's deep-link test, item 139's two onboarding tests). A Release build has 0 errors and 0 warnings. Not run in Ayl, against the harness API or on a device.
150. **Settings cards in a new order (user, 2026-10-29: "settings order: account center, connectors, glove box, secure store").** `ProfileV3SettingsView.panel` now stacks Accounts center, the server's concierge card when sent (it stays right after Accounts center, as before), Connectors, the glove box card (`hasGloveBox`), then the Secure credential store (`isCredentialStoreEnabled`). This moves item 115's glove box card from the top to third. The store's extra 5pt above it (`CredentialStore.cardTop`, which made item 64's 30pt gap under Connectors) is dropped, so every card is `cardSpacing` (25) apart; the constant is unused now.

The Marriott sample email from item 120 is gone; its four `mo-aiprofile-glovebox-sample-email-hotel-*` keys stay in the catalog, unused, until they're deleted from Ditto. The sample call (Marriott early check-in) and the sample transaction are unchanged. Real emails already replaced the samples (`ProfileV3GloveBoxMapper.section` shows samples only for an empty inbox with no next page, and the view model's `realEmails` drops them on rebuilds), so nothing changed there. Change it anytime."), edited by hand in the catalog and the generated fallback (no Ditto token here); it needs the same change in Ditto. The DEBUG fixture's `payBody` matches.

The catalog has 415 keys.

Then the full suite on a throwaway iOS 26.5 simulator (deleted): 246 tests in 38 suites, 0 errors and 0 warnings, 243 pass, and the three failures are the known ones (item 123's deep-link test, item 139's two onboarding tests).
